package com.bookmall.address;

import org.mybatis.spring.annotation.MapperScan;
import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
import org.springframework.cloud.client.discovery.EnableDiscoveryClient;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.EnableFeignClients;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.ComponentScan;

/**
 * 收货地址服务启动类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@SpringBootApplication
@EnableDiscoveryClient
@EnableFeignClients
@MapperScan("com.bookmall.address.mapper")
@ComponentScan(basePackages = {"com.bookmall.address", "com.bookmall.common"})
public class AddressApplication {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SpringApplication.run(AddressApplication.class, args);
        System.out.println("========================================");
        System.out.println("  📚 教材商城系统 - Address模块启动成功！");
        System.out.println("  🔗 访问地址: http://localhost:8088");
        System.out.println("  📖 API文档: http://localhost:8088/swagger-ui.html");
        System.out.println("  💾 Druid监控: http://localhost:8088/druid");
        System.out.println("========================================");
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Address\src\main\java\com\bookmall\address\config\AddressConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.address.config;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.DbType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.MybatisPlusInterceptor;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.inner.PaginationInnerInterceptor;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

/**
 * Address模块配置类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class AddressConfig {

    /**
     * MyBatis-Plus 分页插件
     */
    @Bean
    public MybatisPlusInterceptor mybatisPlusInterceptor() {
        MybatisPlusInterceptor interceptor = new MybatisPlusInterceptor();
        interceptor.addInnerInterceptor(new PaginationInnerInterceptor(DbType.MYSQL));
        return interceptor;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Address\src\main\java\com\bookmall\address\controller\AddressController.java ====================

package com.bookmall.address.controller;

import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressDTO;
import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressUpdateDTO;
import com.bookmall.address.service.AddressService;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Parameter;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.validation.Valid;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 收货地址控制器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/address")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "地址接口", description = "收货地址管理")
public class AddressController {

    private final AddressService addressService;

    /**
     * 从请求头获取用户ID
     * Gateway 的 AuthGlobalFilter 会将 userId 放入请求头 X-User-Id
     */
    private Long getUserId(HttpServletRequest request) {
        // 1. 优先从请求头获取
        String userIdHeader = request.getHeader("X-User-Id");
        if (userIdHeader != null && !userIdHeader.isEmpty()) {
            try {
                return Long.parseLong(userIdHeader);
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                log.warn("解析 userId 失败: {}", userIdHeader);
            }
        }
        // 2. 兼容从请求属性获取
        Object userIdAttr = request.getAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID);
        if (userIdAttr instanceof Long) {
            return (Long) userIdAttr;
        }
        throw new RuntimeException("用户未登录");
    }

    /**
     * 获取用户的所有地址
     */
    @GetMapping
    @Operation(summary = "获取地址列表")
    public Result<List<AddressDTO>> getAddressList(HttpServletRequest request) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        List<AddressDTO> addresses = addressService.getAddressListWithDefaultFirst(userId);
        return Result.success(addresses);
    }

    /**
     * 获取地址详情
     * 支持两种方式获取 userId：
     * 1. 从请求头 X-User-Id 获取（前端调用）
     * 2. 从请求参数获取（Feign 内部调用）
     */
    @GetMapping("/{addressId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取地址详情")
    public Result<AddressDTO> getAddressById(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @Parameter(description = "地址ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long addressId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long userId) {  // ✅ 添加 userId 参数

        log.info("获取地址详情: addressId={}, userId={}", addressId, userId);

        Long finalUserId = userId;
        if (finalUserId == null) {
            try {
                finalUserId = getUserId(request);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("获取 userId 失败: {}", e.getMessage());
                return Result.error("用户未登录");
            }
        }

        if (finalUserId == null) {
            return Result.error("用户ID不能为空");
        }

        AddressDTO address = addressService.getAddressById(finalUserId, addressId);
        return Result.success(address);
    }

    /**
     * 获取默认地址
     * 支持两种方式获取 userId：
     * 1. 从请求头 X-User-Id 获取（前端调用）
     * 2. 从请求参数获取（Feign 内部调用）
     */
    @GetMapping("/default")
    @Operation(summary = "获取默认地址")
    public Result<AddressDTO> getDefaultAddress(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long userId) {  // ✅ 添加 userId 参数

        log.info("获取默认地址: userId={}", userId);

        Long finalUserId = userId;
        if (finalUserId == null) {
            try {
                finalUserId = getUserId(request);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("获取 userId 失败: {}", e.getMessage());
                return Result.error("用户未登录");
            }
        }

        if (finalUserId == null) {
            return Result.error("用户ID不能为空");
        }

        AddressDTO address = addressService.getDefaultAddress(finalUserId);
        return Result.success(address);
    }

    /**
     * 创建地址
     */
    @PostMapping
    @Operation(summary = "新增地址")
    public Result<AddressDTO> createAddress(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @Valid @RequestBody AddressCreateDTO addressCreateDTO) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        AddressDTO address = addressService.createAddress(userId, addressCreateDTO);
        return Result.success("添加成功", address);
    }

    /**
     * 更新地址
     */
    @PutMapping("/{addressId}")
    @Operation(summary = "修改地址")
    public Result<AddressDTO> updateAddress(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @Parameter(description = "地址ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long addressId,
            @Valid @RequestBody AddressUpdateDTO addressUpdateDTO) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        AddressDTO address = addressService.updateAddress(userId, addressId, addressUpdateDTO);
        return Result.success("修改成功", address);
    }

    /**
     * 删除地址
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/{addressId}")
    @Operation(summary = "删除地址")
    public Result<Void> deleteAddress(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @Parameter(description = "地址ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long addressId) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        addressService.deleteAddress(userId, addressId);
        return Result.success("删除成功");
    }

    /**
     * 设置默认地址
     */
    @PutMapping("/{addressId}/default")
    @Operation(summary = "设置默认地址")
    public Result<Void> setDefaultAddress(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @Parameter(description = "地址ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long addressId) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        addressService.setDefaultAddress(userId, addressId);
        return Result.success("设置默认地址成功");
    }

    /**
     * 检查用户是否有地址
     */
    @GetMapping("/check")
    @Operation(summary = "检查用户是否有地址", description = "内部调用")
    public Result<Boolean> hasAddress(
            @RequestParam @NotNull Long userId) {
        boolean has = addressService.hasAddress(userId);
        return Result.success(has);
    }

    /**
     * 获取地址数量
     */
    @GetMapping("/count")
    @Operation(summary = "获取地址数量", description = "内部调用")
    public Result<Integer> getAddressCount(
            @RequestParam @NotNull Long userId) {
        Integer count = addressService.getAddressCount(userId);
        return Result.success(count);
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Address\src\main\java\com\bookmall\address\dto\AddressCreateDTO.java ====================

package com.bookmall.address.dto;

import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.media.Schema;
import lombok.Data;

import javax.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import javax.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import javax.validation.constraints.Size;

/**
 * 创建收货地址请求DTO
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Data
@Schema(description = "创建收货地址请求")
public class AddressCreateDTO {

    @NotBlank(message = "收货人姓名不能为空")
    @Size(max = 50, message = "收货人姓名不能超过50个字符")
    @Schema(description = "收货人姓名", example = "张三")
    private String receiverName;

    @NotBlank(message = "收货人电话不能为空")
    @Pattern(regexp = "^1[3-9]\\d{9}$", message = "手机号格式不正确")
    @Schema(description = "收货人电话", example = "13800138000")
    private String receiverPhone;

    @Schema(description = "省份", example = "江西省")
    private String province;

    @Schema(description = "城市", example = "南昌市")
    private String city;

    @Schema(description = "区/县", example = "青山湖区")
    private String district;

    @NotBlank(message = "详细地址不能为空")
    @Size(max = 200, message = "详细地址不能超过200个字符")
    @Schema(description = "详细地址", example = "江西财经大学南区12栋301")
    private String detailAddress;

    @Schema(description = "是否默认：0-否 1-是", example = "1")
    private Integer isDefault = 0;
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Address\src\main\java\com\bookmall\address\dto\AddressDTO.java ====================

package com.bookmall.address.dto;

import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.media.Schema;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.Data;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * 收货地址响应DTO
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Data
@Builder
@Schema(description = "收货地址信息")
public class AddressDTO {

    @Schema(description = "地址ID", example = "1")
    private Long addressId;

    @Schema(description = "用户ID", example = "1")
    private Long userId;

    @Schema(description = "收货人姓名", example = "张三")
    private String receiverName;

    @Schema(description = "收货人电话", example = "13800138000")
    private String receiverPhone;

    @Schema(description = "省份", example = "江西省")
    private String province;

    @Schema(description = "城市", example = "南昌市")
    private String city;

    @Schema(description = "区/县", example = "青山湖区")
    private String district;

    @Schema(description = "详细地址", example = "江西财经大学南区12栋301")
    private String detailAddress;

    @Schema(description = "是否默认：0-否 1-是", example = "1")
    private Integer isDefault;

    @Schema(description = "完整地址", example = "江西省南昌市青山湖区江西财经大学南区12栋301")
    private String fullAddress;

    @Schema(description = "创建时间")
    private LocalDateTime createTime;
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Address\src\main\java\com\bookmall\address\entity\Address.java ====================

package com.bookmall.address.entity;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.*;
import lombok.Data;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * 收货地址实体类 - 对应 tb_address 表
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Data
@TableName("tb_address")
public class Address {

    @TableId(value = "address_id", type = IdType.AUTO)
    private Long addressId;

    @TableField("user_id")
    private Long userId;

    @TableField("receiver_name")
    private String receiverName;

    @TableField("receiver_phone")
    private String receiverPhone;

    @TableField("province")
    private String province;

    @TableField("city")
    private String city;

    @TableField("district")
    private String district;

    @TableField("detail_address")
    private String detailAddress;

    @TableField("is_default")
    private Integer isDefault;

    @TableField(value = "create_time", fill = FieldFill.INSERT)
    private LocalDateTime createTime;

    /**
     * 完整地址（非数据库字段）
     */
    @TableField(exist = false)
    private String fullAddress;
}

package com.bookmall.address.feign;

import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressDTO;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 收货地址服务 Feign 客户端
 * 供其他微服务调用
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@FeignClient(name = "address-service", path = "/api/address")
public interface AddressFeignClient {

    /**
     * 获取用户的所有地址
     */
    @GetMapping
    Result<List<AddressDTO>> getAddressList(@RequestParam("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 获取地址详情
     */
    @GetMapping("/{addressId}")
    Result<AddressDTO> getAddressById(@RequestParam("userId") Long userId,
                                      @PathVariable("addressId") Long addressId);

    /**
     * 获取默认地址
     */
    @GetMapping("/default")
    Result<AddressDTO> getDefaultAddress(@RequestParam("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 检查用户是否有地址
     */
    @GetMapping("/check")
    Result<Boolean> hasAddress(@RequestParam("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 检查地址是否存在且属于该用户
     */
    @GetMapping("/exists")
    Result<Boolean> existsByUserIdAndAddressId(@RequestParam("userId") Long userId,
                                               @RequestParam("addressId") Long addressId);

    /**
     * 获取地址数量
     */
    @GetMapping("/count")
    Result<Integer> getAddressCount(@RequestParam("userId") Long userId);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Address\src\main\java\com\bookmall\address\mapper\AddressMapper.java ====================

package com.bookmall.address.mapper;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.bookmall.address.entity.Address;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Update;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 收货地址数据访问层
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Mapper
public interface AddressMapper extends BaseMapper<Address> {

    /**
     * 根据用户ID查询所有地址（按创建时间降序）
     */
    @Select("SELECT address_id, user_id, receiver_name, receiver_phone, " +
            "province, city, district, detail_address, is_default, create_time " +
            "FROM tb_address WHERE user_id = #{userId} ORDER BY create_time DESC")
    List<Address> selectByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 查询用户的所有地址（默认地址排最前）
     */
    @Select("SELECT address_id, user_id, receiver_name, receiver_phone, " +
            "province, city, district, detail_address, is_default, create_time " +
            "FROM tb_address WHERE user_id = #{userId} " +
            "ORDER BY is_default DESC, create_time DESC")
    List<Address> selectByUserIdWithDefaultFirst(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 获取用户的默认地址
     */
    @Select("SELECT address_id, user_id, receiver_name, receiver_phone, " +
            "province, city, district, detail_address, is_default, create_time " +
            "FROM tb_address WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND is_default = 1 LIMIT 1")
    Address selectDefaultByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 检查用户是否存在默认地址
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_address WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND is_default = 1")
    int countDefaultByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 取消用户的所有默认地址
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_address SET is_default = 0 WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND is_default = 1")
    int clearDefaultByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 设置指定地址为默认
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_address SET is_default = 1 " +
            "WHERE address_id = #{addressId} AND user_id = #{userId}")
    int setDefault(@Param("addressId") Long addressId, @Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 统计用户的地址数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_address WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    int countByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 批量删除用户的地址
     */
    int batchDeleteByUserIds(@Param("userIds") List<Long> userIds);

    /**
     * 根据用户ID和地址ID查询
     */
    @Select("SELECT address_id, user_id, receiver_name, receiver_phone, " +
            "province, city, district, detail_address, is_default, create_time " +
            "FROM tb_address WHERE address_id = #{addressId} AND user_id = #{userId}")
    Address selectByUserIdAndAddressId(@Param("userId") Long userId,
                                       @Param("addressId") Long addressId);

    /**
     * 获取用户的地址数量（用于限制）
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_address WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    int countAddressesByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 检查地址是否属于用户
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_address WHERE address_id = #{addressId} AND user_id = #{userId}")
    int countByAddressIdAndUserId(@Param("addressId") Long addressId,
                                  @Param("userId") Long userId);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Address\src\main\java\com\bookmall\address\service\AddressService.java ====================

package com.bookmall.address.service;

import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressDTO;
import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressUpdateDTO;
import com.bookmall.address.entity.Address;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 收货地址服务接口
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
public interface AddressService {

    // ==================== 查询 ====================

    /**
     * 获取用户的所有地址
     */
    List<AddressDTO> getAddressList(Long userId);

    /**
     * 获取用户的所有地址（默认地址排最前）
     */
    List<AddressDTO> getAddressListWithDefaultFirst(Long userId);

    /**
     * 获取用户的默认地址
     */
    AddressDTO getDefaultAddress(Long userId);

    /**
     * 获取地址详情
     */
    AddressDTO getAddressById(Long userId, Long addressId);

    /**
     * 获取地址实体
     */
    Address getAddressEntity(Long userId, Long addressId);

    // ==================== 管理 ====================

    /**
     * 创建地址
     */
    AddressDTO createAddress(Long userId, AddressCreateDTO request);

    /**
     * 更新地址
     */
    AddressDTO updateAddress(Long userId, Long addressId, AddressUpdateDTO request);

    /**
     * 删除地址
     */
    void deleteAddress(Long userId, Long addressId);

    /**
     * 设置默认地址
     */
    void setDefaultAddress(Long userId, Long addressId);

    /**
     * 批量删除用户地址（用户注销时使用）
     */
    void batchDeleteByUserIds(List<Long> userIds);

    // ==================== 验证 ====================

    /**
     * 检查地址是否存在且属于该用户
     */
    boolean existsByUserIdAndAddressId(Long userId, Long addressId);

    /**
     * 检查用户是否有地址
     */
    boolean hasAddress(Long userId);

    /**
     * 获取用户地址数量
     */
    Integer getAddressCount(Long userId);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Address\src\main\java\com\bookmall\address\service\impl\AddressServiceImpl.java ====================

package com.bookmall.address.service.impl;

import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressDTO;
import com.bookmall.address.dto.AddressUpdateDTO;
import com.bookmall.address.entity.Address;
import com.bookmall.address.mapper.AddressMapper;
import com.bookmall.address.service.AddressService;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * 收货地址服务实现类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AddressServiceImpl implements AddressService {

    private final AddressMapper addressMapper;

    // 每个用户最大地址数量
    private static final int MAX_ADDRESS_COUNT = 10;

    // ==================== 查询 ====================

    @Override
    public List<AddressDTO> getAddressList(Long userId) {
        List<Address> addresses = addressMapper.selectByUserId(userId);
        return addresses.stream()
                .map(this::convertToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    @Override
    public List<AddressDTO> getAddressListWithDefaultFirst(Long userId) {
        List<Address> addresses = addressMapper.selectByUserIdWithDefaultFirst(userId);
        return addresses.stream()
                .map(this::convertToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    @Override
    public AddressDTO getDefaultAddress(Long userId) {
        Address address = addressMapper.selectDefaultByUserId(userId);
        if (address == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return convertToDTO(address);
    }

    @Override
    public AddressDTO getAddressById(Long userId, Long addressId) {
        Address address = getAddressEntity(userId, addressId);
        return convertToDTO(address);
    }

    @Override
    public Address getAddressEntity(Long userId, Long addressId) {
        Address address = addressMapper.selectByUserIdAndAddressId(userId, addressId);
        if (address == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ADDRESS_NOT_EXIST);
        }
        return address;
    }

    // ==================== 管理 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public AddressDTO createAddress(Long userId, AddressCreateDTO request) {
        // 检查地址数量是否超限
        int count = addressMapper.countAddressesByUserId(userId);
        if (count >= MAX_ADDRESS_COUNT) {
            throw new BusinessException("地址数量已达上限（" + MAX_ADDRESS_COUNT + "个）");
        }

        // 如果设置为默认地址，先取消所有默认
        if (request.getIsDefault() != null && request.getIsDefault() == 1) {
            addressMapper.clearDefaultByUserId(userId);
        }
        // 如果是第一个地址，自动设为默认
        if (count == 0) {
            request.setIsDefault(1);
        }

        // 创建地址
        Address address = new Address();
        address.setUserId(userId);
        address.setReceiverName(request.getReceiverName());
        address.setReceiverPhone(request.getReceiverPhone());
        address.setProvince(request.getProvince());
        address.setCity(request.getCity());
        address.setDistrict(request.getDistrict());
        address.setDetailAddress(request.getDetailAddress());
        address.setIsDefault(request.getIsDefault() != null ? request.getIsDefault() : 0);

        addressMapper.insert(address);

        log.info("创建地址成功: addressId={}, userId={}", address.getAddressId(), userId);

        return convertToDTO(address);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public AddressDTO updateAddress(Long userId, Long addressId, AddressUpdateDTO request) {
        Address address = getAddressEntity(userId, addressId);

        // 如果设置为默认地址，先取消其他默认地址
        if (request.getIsDefault() != null && request.getIsDefault() == 1) {
            addressMapper.clearDefaultByUserId(userId);
        }

        // 更新
        address.setReceiverName(request.getReceiverName());
        address.setReceiverPhone(request.getReceiverPhone());
        address.setProvince(request.getProvince());
        address.setCity(request.getCity());
        address.setDistrict(request.getDistrict());
        address.setDetailAddress(request.getDetailAddress());
        address.setIsDefault(request.getIsDefault());

        addressMapper.updateById(address);

        log.info("更新地址成功: addressId={}, userId={}", addressId, userId);

        return convertToDTO(address);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteAddress(Long userId, Long addressId) {
        Address address = getAddressEntity(userId, addressId);

        // 如果删除的是默认地址，需要将其他地址设为默认
        if (address.getIsDefault() == 1) {
            // 查找用户的其他地址
            List<Address> otherAddresses = addressMapper.selectByUserId(userId);
            otherAddresses.removeIf(a -> a.getAddressId().equals(addressId));
            if (!otherAddresses.isEmpty()) {
                // 将第一个设为默认
                Address newDefault = otherAddresses.get(0);
                newDefault.setIsDefault(1);
                addressMapper.updateById(newDefault);
            }
        }

        addressMapper.deleteById(addressId);

        log.info("删除地址成功: addressId={}, userId={}", addressId, userId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void setDefaultAddress(Long userId, Long addressId) {
        // 检查地址是否存在且属于该用户
        Address address = getAddressEntity(userId, addressId);

        // 如果已经是默认地址，直接返回
        if (address.getIsDefault() == 1) {
            return;
        }

        // 取消所有默认地址
        addressMapper.clearDefaultByUserId(userId);

        // 设置新默认地址
        addressMapper.setDefault(addressId, userId);

        log.info("设置默认地址成功: addressId={}, userId={}", addressId, userId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void batchDeleteByUserIds(List<Long> userIds) {
        if (userIds == null || userIds.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        addressMapper.batchDeleteByUserIds(userIds);
        log.info("批量删除用户地址成功: userIds={}", userIds);
    }

    // ==================== 验证 ====================

    @Override
    public boolean existsByUserIdAndAddressId(Long userId, Long addressId) {
        return addressMapper.countByAddressIdAndUserId(addressId, userId) > 0;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean hasAddress(Long userId) {
        return addressMapper.countByUserId(userId) > 0;
    }

    @Override
    public Integer getAddressCount(Long userId) {
        return addressMapper.countByUserId(userId);
    }

    // ==================== 私有方法 ====================

    /**
     * 实体转DTO
     */
    private AddressDTO convertToDTO(Address address) {
        if (address == null) {
            return null;
        }
        // 构建完整地址
        String fullAddress = buildFullAddress(address);

        return AddressDTO.builder()
                .addressId(address.getAddressId())
                .userId(address.getUserId())
                .receiverName(address.getReceiverName())
                .receiverPhone(address.getReceiverPhone())
                .province(address.getProvince())
                .city(address.getCity())
                .district(address.getDistrict())
                .detailAddress(address.getDetailAddress())
                .isDefault(address.getIsDefault())
                .fullAddress(fullAddress)
                .createTime(address.getCreateTime())
                .build();
    }

    /**
     * 构建完整地址
     */
    private String buildFullAddress(Address address) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        if (StringUtils.hasText(address.getProvince())) {
            sb.append(address.getProvince());
        }
        if (StringUtils.hasText(address.getCity())) {
            sb.append(address.getCity());
        }
        if (StringUtils.hasText(address.getDistrict())) {
            sb.append(address.getDistrict());
        }
        if (StringUtils.hasText(address.getDetailAddress())) {
            sb.append(address.getDetailAddress());
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Address\src\main\resources\mapper\AddressMapper.xml ====================

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">
<mapper namespace="com.bookmall.address.mapper.AddressMapper">

    <!-- 结果映射 -->
    <resultMap id="BaseResultMap" type="com.bookmall.address.entity.Address">
        <id column="address_id" property="addressId"/>
        <result column="user_id" property="userId"/>
        <result column="receiver_name" property="receiverName"/>
        <result column="receiver_phone" property="receiverPhone"/>
        <result column="province" property="province"/>
        <result column="city" property="city"/>
        <result column="district" property="district"/>
        <result column="detail_address" property="detailAddress"/>
        <result column="is_default" property="isDefault"/>
        <result column="create_time" property="createTime"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 通用查询字段 -->
    <sql id="Base_Column_List">
        address_id, user_id, receiver_name, receiver_phone,
        province, city, district, detail_address, is_default, create_time
    </sql>

    <!-- 根据用户ID查询所有地址 -->
    <select id="selectByUserId" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_address
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
        ORDER BY create_time DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 查询用户的所有地址（默认地址排最前） -->
    <select id="selectByUserIdWithDefaultFirst" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_address
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
        ORDER BY is_default DESC, create_time DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 获取用户的默认地址 -->
    <select id="selectDefaultByUserId" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_address
        WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND is_default = 1
        LIMIT 1
    </select>

    <!-- 根据用户ID和地址ID查询 -->
    <select id="selectByUserIdAndAddressId" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_address
        WHERE address_id = #{addressId} AND user_id = #{userId}
    </select>

    <!-- 批量删除用户的地址 -->
    <delete id="batchDeleteByUserIds">
        DELETE FROM tb_address
        WHERE user_id IN
        <foreach collection="userIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </delete>

    <!-- 检查地址是否属于用户 -->
    <select id="countByAddressIdAndUserId" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_address
        WHERE address_id = #{addressId} AND user_id = #{userId}
    </select>

    <!-- 获取用户的地址数量 -->
    <select id="countAddressesByUserId" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_address
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
    </select>

    <!-- 统计用户地址数量 -->
    <select id="countByUserId" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_address
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
    </select>

    <!-- 检查用户是否存在默认地址 -->
    <select id="countDefaultByUserId" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_address
        WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND is_default = 1
    </select>

    <!-- 取消用户的所有默认地址 -->
    <update id="clearDefaultByUserId">
        UPDATE tb_address
        SET is_default = 0
        WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND is_default = 1
    </update>

    <!-- 设置指定地址为默认 -->
    <update id="setDefault">
        UPDATE tb_address
        SET is_default = 1
        WHERE address_id = #{addressId} AND user_id = #{userId}
    </update>

</mapper>

package com.bookmall.admin;

import org.mybatis.spring.annotation.MapperScan;
import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.security.servlet.SecurityAutoConfiguration;
import org.springframework.cloud.client.discovery.EnableDiscoveryClient;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.EnableFeignClients;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.ComponentScan;

/**
 * 管理后台服务启动类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@SpringBootApplication(exclude = SecurityAutoConfiguration.class)
@EnableDiscoveryClient
@EnableFeignClients
@MapperScan("com.bookmall.admin.mapper")
@ComponentScan(basePackages = {"com.bookmall.admin", "com.bookmall.common"})
public class AdminApplication {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SpringApplication.run(AdminApplication.class, args);
        System.out.println("========================================");
        System.out.println("  📚 教材商城系统 - Admin模块启动成功！");

        System.out.println("  📖 API文档: http://localhost:8087/swagger-ui.html");
        System.out.println("  💾 Druid监控: http://localhost:8087/druid");
        System.out.println("========================================");
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Admin\src\main\java\com\bookmall\admin\config\AdminConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.admin.config;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.DbType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.MybatisPlusInterceptor;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.inner.PaginationInnerInterceptor;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

/**
 * Admin模块配置类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class AdminConfig {

    /**
     * MyBatis-Plus 分页插件
     */
    @Bean
    public MybatisPlusInterceptor mybatisPlusInterceptor() {
        MybatisPlusInterceptor interceptor = new MybatisPlusInterceptor();
        interceptor.addInnerInterceptor(new PaginationInnerInterceptor(DbType.MYSQL));
        return interceptor;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Admin\src\main\java\com\bookmall\admin\config\AdminSecurityConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.admin.config;

import com.bookmall.admin.interceptor.AdminAuthInterceptor;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.InterceptorRegistry;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.WebMvcConfigurer;

/**
 * 管理后台安全配置
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminSecurityConfig implements WebMvcConfigurer {

    private final AdminAuthInterceptor adminAuthInterceptor;

    @Override
    public void addInterceptors(InterceptorRegistry registry) {
        registry.addInterceptor(adminAuthInterceptor)
                .addPathPatterns("/api/admin/**")
                .excludePathPatterns(
                        // 登录接口
                        "/api/admin/login",
                        // Swagger
                        "/swagger-ui/**",
                        "/v3/api-docs/**",
                        "/doc.html",
                        "/webjars/**"
                );
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Admin\src\main\java\com\bookmall\admin\config\QuickPasswordGen.java ====================

package com.bookmall.admin.config;

import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;

/**
 * 快速生成 BCrypt 加密密码
 * 运行 main 方法即可生成
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
public class QuickPasswordGen {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        BCryptPasswordEncoder encoder = new BCryptPasswordEncoder();

        // ============================================================
        // 修改下面的密码即可生成对应的加密密码
        // ============================================================
        String password = "admin123";

        String encoded = encoder.encode(password);

        System.out.println("========================================");
        System.out.println("  明文密码: " + password);
        System.out.println("  加密密码: " + encoded);
        System.out.println("========================================");
        System.out.println();
        System.out.println("SQL 更新语句:");
        System.out.println("UPDATE tb_admin SET password = '" + encoded + "' WHERE username = 'admin';");
        System.out.println();
        System.out.println("或者插入新管理员:");
        System.out.println("INSERT INTO tb_admin (username, password, role, status) VALUES ('admin', '" + encoded + "', 'admin', 1);");
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Admin\src\main\java\com\bookmall\admin\controller\AdminAuthController.java ====================

package com.bookmall.admin.controller;

import com.bookmall.admin.dto.AdminLoginDTO;
import com.bookmall.admin.dto.AdminLoginVO;
import com.bookmall.admin.service.AdminService;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.validation.Valid;

/**
 * 管理员认证控制器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/admin")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "管理员认证接口", description = "管理员登录、退出")
public class AdminAuthController {

    private final AdminService adminService;

    /**
     * 管理员登录
     */
    @PostMapping("/login")
    @Operation(summary = "管理员登录")
    public Result<AdminLoginVO> login(@Valid @RequestBody AdminLoginDTO request,
                                      HttpServletRequest httpRequest) {
        String ip = getClientIp(httpRequest);
        AdminLoginVO result = adminService.login(request, ip);
        return Result.success("登录成功", result);
    }

    /**
     * 管理员退出登录
     */
    @PostMapping("/logout")
    @Operation(summary = "管理员退出登录")
    public Result<Void> logout(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String token = request.getHeader("Authorization");
        if (token != null && token.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            token = token.substring(7);
        }
        adminService.logout(token);
        return Result.success("退出成功");
    }

    /**
     * 获取客户端IP
     */
    private String getClientIp(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String ip = request.getHeader("X-Forwarded-For");
        if (ip == null || ip.isEmpty() || "unknown".equalsIgnoreCase(ip)) {
            ip = request.getHeader("X-Real-IP");
        }
        if (ip == null || ip.isEmpty() || "unknown".equalsIgnoreCase(ip)) {
            ip = request.getHeader("Proxy-Client-IP");
        }
        if (ip == null || ip.isEmpty() || "unknown".equalsIgnoreCase(ip)) {
            ip = request.getHeader("WL-Proxy-Client-IP");
        }
        if (ip == null || ip.isEmpty() || "unknown".equalsIgnoreCase(ip)) {
            ip = request.getRemoteAddr();
        }
        // 如果有多个IP，取第一个
        if (ip != null && ip.contains(",")) {
            ip = ip.split(",")[0].trim();
        }
        return ip;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Admin\src\main\java\com\bookmall\admin\controller\AdminBookController.java ====================

package com.bookmall.admin.controller;

import com.bookmall.admin.feign.BookFeignClient;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.PageResult;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Parameter;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 管理后台 - 教材管理控制器
 * 通过Feign调用Book服务
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/admin/books")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "管理后台-教材管理", description = "管理员教材管理接口")
public class AdminBookController {

    private final BookFeignClient bookFeignClient;

    // ==================== 教材管理 ====================

    /**
     * 分页查询教材列表（管理员）
     */
    @GetMapping
    @Operation(summary = "分页查询教材列表")
    public Result<PageResult<Map<String, Object>>> getBookList(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long categoryId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) BigDecimal minPrice,
            @RequestParam(required = false) BigDecimal maxPrice,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String sortBy,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {

        Result<PageResult<Map<String, Object>>> result = bookFeignClient.getBookList(
                keyword, categoryId, status, minPrice, maxPrice, sortBy, pageNum, pageSize);
        return result;
    }

    /**
     * 获取教材详情（管理员）
     */
    @GetMapping("/{bookId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取教材详情")
    public Result<Map<String, Object>> getBookDetail(
            @Parameter(description = "教材ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long bookId) {
        return bookFeignClient.getBookDetail(bookId);
    }

    /**
     * 创建教材
     */
    @PostMapping
    @Operation(summary = "创建教材")
    public Result<Map<String, Object>> createBook(@RequestBody Map<String, Object> request) {

/**

@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated

            @RequestParam(required = false) Long userId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime startTime,
            @RequestParam(required = false) @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime endTime,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {

        log.info("管理员获取订单列表: userId={}, status={}, startTime={}, endTime={}",
                userId, status, startTime, endTime);
        return orderFeignClient.getAllOrders(userId, status, startTime, endTime, pageNum, pageSize);
    }

        return orderFeignClient.getOrderDetail(orderId);

            @PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId) {
        log.info("管理员发货: orderId={}", orderId);
        return orderFeignClient.deliverOrder(orderId);
    }

    /**

     */
    @PutMapping("/batch/deliver")
    @Operation(summary = "批量发货")

        log.info("管理员批量发货: orderIds={}", orderIds);
        return orderFeignClient.batchDeliverOrders(orderIds);

package com.bookmall.admin.controller;

import com.bookmall.admin.dto.AdminInfoVO;
import com.bookmall.admin.dto.AdminUpdatePasswordDTO;
import com.bookmall.admin.service.AdminService;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.validation.Valid;

/**
 * 管理员个人信息控制器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/admin/profile")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "管理员个人信息接口", description = "管理员个人信息管理")
public class AdminProfileController {

    private final AdminService adminService;

    /**
     * 获取当前管理员信息
     */
    @GetMapping
    @Operation(summary = "获取当前管理员信息")
    public Result<AdminInfoVO> getProfile(@RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long adminId) {
        AdminInfoVO info = adminService.getAdminInfo(adminId);
        return Result.success(info);
    }

    /**
     * 修改密码
     */
    @PutMapping("/password")
    @Operation(summary = "修改密码")
    public Result<Void> updatePassword(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long adminId,
            @Valid @RequestBody AdminUpdatePasswordDTO request) {
        adminService.updatePassword(adminId, request);
        return Result.success("密码修改成功，请重新登录");
    }

    /**
     * 修改个人信息
     */
    @PutMapping
    @Operation(summary = "修改个人信息")
    public Result<Void> updateProfile(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long adminId,
            @Valid @RequestBody AdminInfoVO request) {
        adminService.updateProfile(adminId, request);
        return Result.success("信息修改成功");
    }
}

package com.bookmall.admin.controller;

import com.bookmall.admin.dto.AdminInfoVO;
import com.bookmall.admin.feign.UserFeignClient;
import com.bookmall.admin.service.AdminService;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.PageResult;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;

import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Parameter;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 管理员管理控制器（超级管理员专用）
 *
 * 包含两个部分：
 * 1. /admin/users       —— 管理员账号管理（tb_admin）
 * 2. /admin/users/members —— 普通用户管理（tb_user）
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/admin/users")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "管理员管理接口", description = "管理员账号管理 + 普通用户管理")
public class AdminUserController {

    private final AdminService adminService;
    private final UserFeignClient userFeignClient;

    // ============================================================
    // 一、管理员账号管理（原有功能，保留不变）
    // ============================================================

    /**
     * 获取所有管理员列表
     */
    @GetMapping
    @Operation(summary = "获取所有管理员列表")
    public Result<List<AdminInfoVO>> getAllAdmins() {
        List<AdminInfoVO> admins = adminService.getAllAdmins();
        return Result.success(admins);
    }

    /**
     * 获取启用的管理员列表
     */
    @GetMapping("/active")
    @Operation(summary = "获取启用的管理员列表")
    public Result<List<AdminInfoVO>> getActiveAdmins() {
        List<AdminInfoVO> admins = adminService.getActiveAdmins();
        return Result.success(admins);
    }

    /**
     * 获取管理员详情
     */
    @GetMapping("/{adminId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取管理员详情")
    public Result<AdminInfoVO> getAdminById(
            @Parameter(description = "管理员ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long adminId) {
        AdminInfoVO admin = adminService.getAdminInfoById(adminId);
        return Result.success(admin);
    }

    /**
     * 创建管理员
     */
    @PostMapping
    @Operation(summary = "创建管理员")
    public Result<AdminInfoVO> createAdmin(@RequestBody AdminInfoVO request) {
        AdminInfoVO admin = adminService.createAdmin(request);
        return Result.success("创建成功", admin);
    }

    /**
     * 更新管理员
     */
    @PutMapping("/{adminId}")
    @Operation(summary = "更新管理员")
    public Result<AdminInfoVO> updateAdmin(
            @Parameter(description = "管理员ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long adminId,
            @RequestBody AdminInfoVO request) {
        AdminInfoVO admin = adminService.updateAdmin(adminId, request);
        return Result.success("更新成功", admin);
    }

    /**
     * 启用管理员
     */
    @PutMapping("/{adminId}/enable")
    @Operation(summary = "启用管理员")
    public Result<Void> enableAdmin(
            @Parameter(description = "管理员ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long adminId) {
        adminService.enableAdmin(adminId);
        return Result.success("启用成功");
    }

    /**
     * 禁用管理员
     */
    @PutMapping("/{adminId}/disable")
    @Operation(summary = "禁用管理员")
    public Result<Void> disableAdmin(
            @Parameter(description = "管理员ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long adminId) {
        adminService.disableAdmin(adminId);
        return Result.success("禁用成功");
    }

    /**
     * 删除管理员
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/{adminId}")
    @Operation(summary = "删除管理员")
    public Result<Void> deleteAdmin(
            @Parameter(description = "管理员ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long adminId) {
        adminService.deleteAdmin(adminId);
        return Result.success("删除成功");
    }

    /**
     * 批量删除管理员
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/batch")
    @Operation(summary = "批量删除管理员")
    public Result<Void> batchDeleteAdmins(@RequestBody List<Long> adminIds) {
        adminService.batchDeleteAdmins(adminIds);
        return Result.success("批量删除成功");
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 二、普通用户管理（新增，通过 Feign 调用 user-service）
    // 注意：路径统一加 /members 前缀，避免和管理员接口冲突
    // ============================================================

    /**
     * 分页查询普通用户列表
     * 支持按用户名模糊搜索、按状态筛选
     */
    @GetMapping("/members")
    @Operation(summary = "分页查询普通用户列表")
    public Result<PageResult<Map<String, Object>>> getUserList(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {
        log.info("管理员查询普通用户列表: keyword={}, status={}, pageNum={}, pageSize={}",
                keyword, status, pageNum, pageSize);
        return userFeignClient.getUserList(keyword, status, pageNum, pageSize);
    }

    /**
     * 获取普通用户详情
     */
    @GetMapping("/members/{userId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取普通用户详情")
    public Result<Map<String, Object>> getUserDetail(
            @Parameter(description = "用户ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long userId) {
        log.info("管理员查询普通用户详情: userId={}", userId);
        return userFeignClient.getUserDetail(userId);
    }

    /**
     * 启用/禁用普通用户
     * status: 0-禁用 1-正常
     */
    @PutMapping("/members/{userId}/status")
    @Operation(summary = "启用/禁用普通用户")
    public Result<Void> updateUserStatus(
            @Parameter(description = "用户ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long userId,
            @RequestParam @NotNull Integer status) {
        log.info("管理员修改普通用户状态: userId={}, status={}", userId, status);
        return userFeignClient.updateUserStatus(userId, status);
    }

    /**
     * 获取普通用户的余额
     */
    @GetMapping("/members/{userId}/balance")
    @Operation(summary = "获取普通用户余额")
    public Result<BigDecimal> getUserBalance(
            @Parameter(description = "用户ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long userId) {
        log.info("管理员查询普通用户余额: userId={}", userId);
        return userFeignClient.getUserBalance(userId);
    }

    /**
     * 删除普通用户（物理删除）
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/members/{userId}")
    @Operation(summary = "删除普通用户")
    public Result<Void> deleteUser(
            @Parameter(description = "用户ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long userId) {
        log.info("管理员删除普通用户: userId={}", userId);
        return userFeignClient.deleteUser(userId);
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Admin\src\main\java\com\bookmall\admin\enums\AdminRole.java ====================

package com.bookmall.admin.enums;

import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Getter;

/**
 * 管理员角色枚举
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Getter
@AllArgsConstructor
public enum AdminRole {

    SUPER_ADMIN("super_admin", "超级管理员"),
    ADMIN("admin", "管理员"),
    OPERATOR("operator", "运营人员"),
    VIEWER("viewer", "查看者");

    private final String code;
    private final String desc;

    public static AdminRole fromCode(String code) {
        for (AdminRole role : values()) {
            if (role.code.equals(code)) {
                return role;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    public static String getDesc(String code) {
        AdminRole role = fromCode(code);
        return role != null ? role.desc : "未知";
    }

    /**
     * 判断是否有权限
     */
    public boolean hasPermission(AdminRole requiredRole) {
        return this.ordinal() <= requiredRole.ordinal();
    }

    /**
     * 判断是否是超级管理员
     */
    public boolean isSuperAdmin() {
        return this == SUPER_ADMIN;
    }

    /**
     * 判断是否是管理员或以上
     */
    public boolean isAdminOrAbove() {
        return this.ordinal() <= ADMIN.ordinal();
    }
}

package com.bookmall.admin.feign;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.PageResult;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 教材服务 Feign 客户端
 *
 * 路径说明：
 * - Book 服务的 BookController 映射为 @RequestMapping("/books")
 * - 管理员接口与公开接口共用 /books 前缀
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@FeignClient(name = "book-service", path = "/books")
public interface BookFeignClient {

    @GetMapping
    Result<PageResult<Map<String, Object>>> getBookList(
            @RequestParam(value = "keyword", required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(value = "categoryId", required = false) Long categoryId,
            @RequestParam(value = "status", required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(value = "minPrice", required = false) BigDecimal minPrice,
            @RequestParam(value = "maxPrice", required = false) BigDecimal maxPrice,
            @RequestParam(value = "sortBy", required = false) String sortBy,
            @RequestParam(value = "pageNum", defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(value = "pageSize", defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize);

    @GetMapping("/{bookId}")
    Result<Map<String, Object>> getBookDetail(@PathVariable("bookId") Long bookId);

    @PostMapping
    Result<Map<String, Object>> createBook(@RequestBody Map<String, Object> request);

    @PutMapping("/{bookId}")
    Result<Map<String, Object>> updateBook(
            @PathVariable("bookId") Long bookId,
            @RequestBody Map<String, Object> request);

    @DeleteMapping("/{bookId}")
    Result<Void> deleteBook(@PathVariable("bookId") Long bookId);

    @DeleteMapping("/batch")
    Result<Void> batchDeleteBooks(@RequestBody List<Long> bookIds);

    @PutMapping("/{bookId}/on-sale")
    Result<Void> onSale(@PathVariable("bookId") Long bookId);

    @PutMapping("/{bookId}/off-sale")
    Result<Void> offSale(@PathVariable("bookId") Long bookId);

    @PutMapping("/batch/on-sale")
    Result<Void> batchOnSale(@RequestBody List<Long> bookIds);

    @PutMapping("/batch/off-sale")
    Result<Void> batchOffSale(@RequestBody List<Long> bookIds);

    @PutMapping("/{bookId}/stock")
    Result<Void> updateStock(
            @PathVariable("bookId") Long bookId,
            @RequestParam("stock") Integer stock);

    @GetMapping("/statistics")
    Result<Map<String, Object>> getStatistics();

    @GetMapping("/hot")
    Result<List<Map<String, Object>>> getHotBooks(
            @RequestParam(value = "limit", defaultValue = "10") int limit);

    @GetMapping("/new")
    Result<List<Map<String, Object>>> getNewBooks(
            @RequestParam(value = "limit", defaultValue = "10") int limit);

    @GetMapping("/statistics/category")
    Result<List<Map<String, Object>>> countByCategory();
}

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;

import org.springframework.format.annotation.DateTimeFormat;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 订单服务 Feign 客户端
 *
 * 路径说明：
 * - Order 服务的 OrderController 映射为 @RequestMapping("/order")
 * - 管理员接口路径为 /order/admin/orders/**
 * - Feign 的 path 必须与之一致，即 /order
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@FeignClient(name = "order-service", path = "/order")
public interface OrderFeignClient {

    // ✅ 修正：/admin/list → /admin/orders
    @GetMapping("/admin/orders")
    Result<PageResult<Map<String, Object>>> getAllOrders(
            @RequestParam(value = "userId", required = false) Long userId,
            @RequestParam(value = "status", required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(value = "startTime", required = false)
            @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime startTime,
            @RequestParam(value = "endTime", required = false)
            @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime endTime,
            @RequestParam(value = "pageNum", defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(value = "pageSize", defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize);

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}")
    Result<Map<String, Object>> getOrderDetail(@PathVariable("orderId") String orderId);

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}/deliver")
    Result<Void> deliverOrder(@PathVariable("orderId") String orderId);

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/batch/deliver")
    Result<Void> batchDeliverOrders(@RequestBody List<String> orderIds);

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}/cancel")
    Result<Void> cancelOrderByAdmin(@PathVariable("orderId") String orderId);

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/batch/cancel")
    Result<Void> batchCancelOrders(@RequestBody List<String> orderIds);

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}/remark")
    Result<Void> updateOrderRemark(
            @PathVariable("orderId") String orderId,
            @RequestParam("remark") String remark);

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}/receiver")
    Result<Void> updateReceiverInfo(
            @PathVariable("orderId") String orderId,
            @RequestBody Map<String, String> receiverInfo);

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics")
    Result<Map<String, Object>> getOrderStatistics();

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics/status")
    Result<Map<Integer, Long>> countByStatus();

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics/user/{userId}")
    Result<Map<String, Object>> getUserOrderStatistics(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics/today")
    Result<Map<String, Object>> getTodayStatistics();

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics/range")
    Result<Map<String, Object>> getRangeStatistics(
            @RequestParam("startTime")
            @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime startTime,
            @RequestParam("endTime")
            @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime endTime);

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/export")
    Result<String> exportOrders(
            @RequestParam(value = "status", required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(value = "startTime", required = false)
            @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime startTime,
            @RequestParam(value = "endTime", required = false)
            @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime endTime,
            @RequestParam(value = "format", defaultValue = "csv") String format);
}

package com.bookmall.admin.feign;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

/**
 * 支付服务 Feign 客户端
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */

package com.bookmall.admin.feign;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.PageResult;

/**
 * 用户服务 Feign 客户端
 *
 * 路径说明：
 * - User 服务的 UserController / AdminUserController 都映射在 /user 下
 * - Feign 的 path 用 /user（不带 /api，/api 是网关用的）
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@FeignClient(name = "user-service", path = "/user")
public interface UserFeignClient {

    // ==================== 原有：统计接口 ====================

    /**
     * 获取用户总数

    // ==================== 新增：普通用户管理 ====================

     * 对应 user-service 的 GET /user/admin/list

            @RequestParam(value = "keyword", required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(value = "status", required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(value = "pageNum", defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,

    /**

     * 对应 user-service 的 GET /user/admin/{userId}

    @GetMapping("/admin/{userId}")
    Result<Map<String, Object>> getUserDetail(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 启用/禁用用户（管理员）
     * 对应 user-service 的 PUT /user/admin/{userId}/status
     */
    @PutMapping("/admin/{userId}/status")
    Result<Void> updateUserStatus(
            @PathVariable("userId") Long userId,
            @RequestParam("status") Integer status);

    /**
     * 删除用户（管理员）
     * 对应 user-service 的 DELETE /user/admin/{userId}
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/admin/{userId}")
    Result<Void> deleteUser(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Admin\src\main\java\com\bookmall\admin\handler\AdminExceptionHandler.java ====================

package com.bookmall.admin.handler;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import feign.FeignException;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;

/**
 * 管理后台异常处理器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestControllerAdvice
public class AdminExceptionHandler {

    /**
     * Feign调用异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(FeignException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleFeignException(FeignException e) {
        log.error("Feign调用失败: status={}, message={}", e.status(), e.getMessage());
        if (e.status() == 404) {
            return Result.error("服务不可用，请稍后再试");
        }
        if (e.status() >= 500) {
            return Result.error("服务异常，请稍后再试");
        }
        return Result.error("请求失败，请稍后再试");
    }

    /**
     * 业务异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(BusinessException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleBusinessException(BusinessException e) {
        log.warn("业务异常: code={}, message={}", e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
        return Result.error(e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
    }

    /**
     * 系统异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public Result<Void> handleException(Exception e) {
        log.error("系统异常: ", e);
        return Result.error(ExceptionEnum.SYSTEM_ERROR.getMessage());
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Admin\src\main\java\com\bookmall\admin\interceptor\AdminAuthInterceptor.java ====================

package com.bookmall.admin.interceptor;

import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.JwtUtil;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.RedisUtil;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.HandlerInterceptor;

import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

/**
 * 管理员认证拦截器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminAuthInterceptor implements HandlerInterceptor {

    private final RedisUtil redisUtil;

    // Redis Key前缀
    private static final String REDIS_ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY = "admin:token:";

    @Override
    public boolean preHandle(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response, Object handler) {
        String header = request.getHeader(Constants.TOKEN_HEADER);

        // 检查Token是否存在
        if (!StringUtils.hasText(header)) {
            log.warn("管理员请求缺少Token: {}", request.getRequestURI());
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.LOGIN_REQUIRED);
        }

        // 去掉Bearer前缀
        String token = header;
        if (token.startsWith(Constants.TOKEN_PREFIX)) {
            token = token.substring(Constants.TOKEN_PREFIX.length());
        }

        // 验证Token
        if (!JwtUtil.validateToken(token)) {
            log.warn("管理员Token无效或已过期: {}", token);
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.TOKEN_INVALID);
        }

        // 从Token获取管理员信息
        Long adminId = JwtUtil.getUserIdFromToken(token);
        String username = JwtUtil.getUsernameFromToken(token);
        String role = JwtUtil.getRoleFromToken(token);

        // 验证Redis中的Token
        String cacheKey = REDIS_ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY + adminId;
        Object cachedToken = redisUtil.get(cacheKey);
        if (cachedToken == null || !cachedToken.equals(token)) {
            log.warn("管理员Token已失效: adminId={}", adminId);
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.TOKEN_INVALID);
        }

        // 存入请求上下文
        request.setAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID, adminId);
        request.setAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USERNAME, username);
        request.setAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_ROLE, role);

        log.debug("管理员认证通过: adminId={}, username={}, role={}", adminId, username, role);
        return true;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Admin\src\main\java\com\bookmall\admin\mapper\AdminMapper.java ====================

package com.bookmall.admin.mapper;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.bookmall.admin.entity.Admin;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Update;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 管理员数据访问层
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Mapper
public interface AdminMapper extends BaseMapper<Admin> {

    /**
     * 根据用户名查询管理员
     */
    @Select("SELECT admin_id, username, password, role, status, real_name, phone, email, " +
            "avatar, last_login_ip, last_login_time, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_admin WHERE username = #{username}")
    Admin selectByUsername(@Param("username") String username);

    /**
     * 查询所有管理员
     */
    @Select("SELECT admin_id, username, role, status, real_name, phone, email, " +
            "avatar, last_login_ip, last_login_time, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_admin ORDER BY create_time DESC")
    List<Admin> selectAllAdmins();

    /**
     * 查询启用的管理员
     */
    @Select("SELECT admin_id, username, role, status, real_name, phone, email, " +
            "avatar, last_login_ip, last_login_time, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_admin WHERE status = 1 ORDER BY create_time DESC")
    List<Admin> selectActiveAdmins();

    /**
     * 更新登录信息
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_admin SET last_login_ip = #{ip}, last_login_time = NOW() " +
            "WHERE admin_id = #{adminId}")
    int updateLoginInfo(@Param("adminId") Long adminId,
                        @Param("ip") String ip);

    /**
     * 更新密码
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_admin SET password = #{password} WHERE admin_id = #{adminId}")
    int updatePassword(@Param("adminId") Long adminId,
                       @Param("password") String password);

    /**
     * 启用管理员
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_admin SET status = 1 WHERE admin_id = #{adminId}")
    int enableAdmin(@Param("adminId") Long adminId);

    /**
     * 禁用管理员
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_admin SET status = 0 WHERE admin_id = #{adminId}")
    int disableAdmin(@Param("adminId") Long adminId);

    /**
     * 检查用户名是否存在
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_admin WHERE username = #{username}")
    int countByUsername(@Param("username") String username);

    /**
     * 检查用户名是否存在（排除自身）
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_admin WHERE username = #{username} AND admin_id != #{adminId}")
    int countByUsernameExcludeId(@Param("username") String username,
                                 @Param("adminId") Long adminId);

    /**
     * 统计管理员总数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_admin")
    Long countTotal();

    /**
     * 统计启用的管理员数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_admin WHERE status = 1")
    Long countActive();

    /**
     * 批量删除管理员
     */
    int batchDelete(@Param("adminIds") List<Long> adminIds);
}

package com.bookmall.admin.mapper;

import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

/**
 * 仪表盘统计 Mapper
 */
@Mapper
public interface DashboardMapper {

    // ---------- 用户相关 ----------
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user")
    Long countTotalUsers();

    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE DATE(create_time) = CURDATE()")
    Long countTodayNewUsers();

    // ---------- 教材相关 ----------
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_book")
    Long countTotalBooks();

    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_book WHERE status = 1")
    Long countOnSaleBooks();

    // ---------- 订单相关 ----------
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_order")
    Long countTotalOrders();

    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_order WHERE DATE(create_time) = CURDATE()")
    Long countTodayOrders();

    // 0-待付款 1-待发货
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_order WHERE order_status IN (0, 1)")
    Long countPendingOrders();

    // 已完成订单(3)的销售额总和
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(total_amount), 0) FROM tb_order WHERE order_status = 3")
    BigDecimal sumTotalSales();

    // 今日已完成订单的销售额
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(total_amount), 0) FROM tb_order WHERE order_status = 3 AND DATE(create_time) = CURDATE()")
    BigDecimal sumTodaySales();

    // ---------- 评价相关 ----------
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_review")
    Long countTotalReviews();

    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(AVG(rating), 0) FROM tb_review")
    Double avgRating();

    // ---------- 支付相关 ----------
    // 2-支付成功
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(amount), 0) FROM tb_payment WHERE status = 2")
    BigDecimal sumTotalPaymentAmount();

    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(amount), 0) FROM tb_payment WHERE status = 2 AND DATE(pay_time) = CURDATE()")
    BigDecimal sumTodayPaymentAmount();
}
