==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Cart\src\main\java\com\bookmall\cart\handler\CartExceptionHandler.java ====================

package com.bookmall.cart.handler;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;

import java.net.ConnectException;

/**
 * 购物车模块异常处理器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestControllerAdvice
public class CartExceptionHandler {

    /**
     * Feign调用异常处理
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(ConnectException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleConnectException(ConnectException e) {
        log.error("服务调用失败: {}", e.getMessage());
        return Result.error("服务暂时不可用，请稍后再试");
    }

    /**
     * 其他业务异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(BusinessException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleBusinessException(BusinessException e) {
        log.warn("业务异常: code={}, message={}", e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
        return Result.error(e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
    }

    /**
     * 系统异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public Result<Void> handleException(Exception e) {
        log.error("系统异常: ", e);
        return Result.error(ExceptionEnum.SYSTEM_ERROR.getMessage());
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Cart\src\main\java\com\bookmall\cart\mapper\CartMapper.java ====================

package com.bookmall.cart.mapper;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.bookmall.cart.entity.Cart;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.*;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 购物车数据访问层
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Mapper
public interface CartMapper extends BaseMapper<Cart> {

    /**
     * 根据用户ID查询购物车列表（含教材信息）
     */
    List<Cart> selectCartListByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 根据用户ID和教材ID查询购物车项
     */
    @Select("SELECT cart_id, user_id, book_id, quantity, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_cart WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND book_id = #{bookId}")
    Cart selectByUserIdAndBookId(@Param("userId") Long userId, @Param("bookId") Long bookId);

    /**
     * 更新购物车数量
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_cart SET quantity = quantity + #{quantity} " +
            "WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND book_id = #{bookId}")
    int incrementQuantity(@Param("userId") Long userId,
                          @Param("bookId") Long bookId,
                          @Param("quantity") Integer quantity);

    /**
     * 统计用户购物车商品数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT SUM(quantity) FROM tb_cart WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    Integer sumQuantityByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 统计用户购物车商品种类数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_cart WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    Integer countByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * ✅ 修复：将 @Select 改为 @Delete
     * 清空用户购物车，返回删除行数
     */
    @Delete("DELETE FROM tb_cart WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    int clearByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 批量删除购物车项
     */
    int batchDelete(@Param("cartIds") List<Long> cartIds);

    /**
     * 批量删除用户购物车（用户注销时使用）
     */
    int batchDeleteByUserIds(@Param("userIds") List<Long> userIds);

    /**
     * 获取用户购物车选中的商品总金额
     */
    @Select("SELECT SUM(c.quantity * b.price) " +
            "FROM tb_cart c " +
            "INNER JOIN tb_book b ON c.book_id = b.book_id " +
            "WHERE c.user_id = #{userId} AND b.status = 1")
    java.math.BigDecimal sumTotalAmountByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Cart\src\main\java\com\bookmall\cart\service\CartService.java ====================

package com.bookmall.cart.service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

/**

 * @author BookMall Team
 */
public interface CartService {

    // ==================== 查询 ====================

    /**
     * 获取用户购物车列表

    /**
     * 获取用户购物车商品总数量

    /**
     * 获取用户购物车总金额

     * 获取购物车项详情

    /**
     * 获取购物车实体

    Cart getCartEntity(Long userId, Long cartId);

    // ==================== 操作 ====================

     * 添加到购物车
     */
    CartDTO addToCart(Long userId, CartAddDTO request);

    /**
     * 更新购物车数量
     */

    /**
     * 删除购物车项
     */

     * 批量删除购物车项
     */

     * 清空购物车
     */

     */

     * 检查用户购物车是否为空

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Cart\src\main\java\com\bookmall\cart\service\impl\CartServiceImpl.java ====================

package com.bookmall.cart.service.impl;

import com.bookmall.book.dto.BookDTO;
import com.bookmall.cart.dto.CartAddDTO;
import com.bookmall.cart.dto.CartDTO;
import com.bookmall.cart.dto.CartUpdateDTO;
import com.bookmall.cart.entity.Cart;
import com.bookmall.cart.feign.BookFeignClient;
import com.bookmall.cart.feign.UserFeignClient;
import com.bookmall.cart.mapper.CartMapper;
import com.bookmall.cart.service.CartService;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.RedisUtil;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * 购物车服务实现类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CartServiceImpl implements CartService {

    private final CartMapper cartMapper;
    private final BookFeignClient bookFeignClient;
    private final UserFeignClient userFeignClient;
    private final RedisUtil redisUtil;

    // Redis缓存Key前缀
    private static final String REDIS_CART_KEY = "cart:";
    private static final String REDIS_CART_COUNT_KEY = "cart:count:";

    // ==================== 查询 ====================

    @Override
    public List<CartDTO> getCartList(Long userId) {
        // 从数据库查询
        List<Cart> carts = cartMapper.selectCartListByUserId(userId);
        return carts.stream()
                .map(this::convertToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    @Override
    public Integer getCartCount(Long userId) {
        // 先从Redis缓存获取
        String cacheKey = REDIS_CART_COUNT_KEY + userId;
        Object cached = redisUtil.get(cacheKey);
        if (cached != null) {
            try {
                return (Integer) cached;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("Redis缓存解析失败，从数据库查询");
            }
        }

        Integer count = cartMapper.sumQuantityByUserId(userId);
        if (count == null) {
            count = 0;
        }

        // 缓存到Redis（30分钟过期）
        redisUtil.set(cacheKey, count, 30, TimeUnit.MINUTES);

        return count;
    }

    @Override
    public BigDecimal getCartTotalAmount(Long userId) {
        BigDecimal total = cartMapper.sumTotalAmountByUserId(userId);
        return total != null ? total : BigDecimal.ZERO;
    }

    @Override
    public CartDTO getCartItem(Long userId, Long cartId) {
        Cart cart = getCartEntity(userId, cartId);
        return convertToDTO(cart);
    }

    @Override
    public Cart getCartEntity(Long userId, Long cartId) {
        Cart cart = cartMapper.selectById(cartId);
        if (cart == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.CART_ITEM_NOT_EXIST);
        }
        if (!cart.getUserId().equals(userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException("购物车项不属于该用户");
        }
        return cart;
    }

    // ==================== 操作 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CartDTO addToCart(Long userId, CartAddDTO request) {
        log.info("添加到购物车: userId={}, bookId={}, quantity={}", userId, request.getBookId(), request.getQuantity());

        // 1. 验证用户是否存在
        try {
            Result<Boolean> userResult = userFeignClient.checkUserExists(userId);
            if (userResult == null || !userResult.isSuccess() || !userResult.getData()) {
                throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.USER_NOT_EXIST);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("调用用户服务失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            throw new BusinessException("用户服务不可用，请稍后再试");
        }

        // 2. ✅ 获取图书详情（替代 existsById + isOnSale + getStock 三次调用）
        BookDTO book;
        try {
            Result<com.bookmall.book.dto.BookDTO> bookResult = bookFeignClient.getBookDetail(request.getBookId());
            if (bookResult == null || !bookResult.isSuccess() || bookResult.getData() == null) {
                throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.BOOK_NOT_EXIST);
            }
            book = bookResult.getData();
            log.info("获取图书信息成功: bookId={}, bookName={}, price={}, stock={}, status={}",
                    book.getBookId(), book.getBookName(), book.getPrice(), book.getStock(), book.getStatus());
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("调用图书服务失败: bookId={}, error={}", request.getBookId(), e.getMessage());
            throw new BusinessException("图书服务不可用，请稍后再试");
        }

        // 3. 检查图书是否上架
        if (book.getStatus() == null || book.getStatus() != 1) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.BOOK_OFF_SALE);
        }

        // 4. 验证库存
        if (book.getStock() == null || book.getStock() < request.getQuantity()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.STOCK_INSUFFICIENT);
        }

        // 5. 检查是否已存在该商品
        Cart existCart = cartMapper.selectByUserIdAndBookId(userId, request.getBookId());

        if (existCart != null) {
            // 已存在，增加数量
            int newQuantity = existCart.getQuantity() + request.getQuantity();
            if (newQuantity > book.getStock()) {
                throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.STOCK_INSUFFICIENT);
            }
            existCart.setQuantity(newQuantity);
            cartMapper.updateById(existCart);
            log.info("购物车商品数量增加: cartId={}, quantity={}", existCart.getCartId(), newQuantity);
            clearCache(userId);
            return convertToDTO(existCart);
        } else {
            // 新增
            Cart cart = new Cart();
            cart.setUserId(userId);
            cart.setBookId(request.getBookId());
            cart.setQuantity(request.getQuantity());
            cartMapper.insert(cart);
            log.info("添加购物车成功: userId={}, bookId={}, quantity={}",
                    userId, request.getBookId(), request.getQuantity());
            clearCache(userId);
            return convertToDTO(cart);
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CartDTO updateCartItem(Long userId, Long cartId, CartUpdateDTO request) {
        Cart cart = getCartEntity(userId, cartId);

        // 验证库存（使用 Feign 调用）
        try {
            Result<Integer> stockResult = bookFeignClient.getStock(cart.getBookId());
            Integer stock = stockResult != null && stockResult.isSuccess() ? stockResult.getData() : 0;
            if (stock < request.getQuantity()) {
                throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.STOCK_INSUFFICIENT);
            }
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("调用图书服务获取库存失败: bookId={}", cart.getBookId());
            throw new BusinessException("图书服务不可用，请稍后再试");
        }

        cart.setQuantity(request.getQuantity());
        cartMapper.updateById(cart);

        clearCache(userId);

        log.info("更新购物车成功: cartId={}, quantity={}", cartId, request.getQuantity());

        return convertToDTO(cart);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteCartItem(Long userId, Long cartId) {
        Cart cart = getCartEntity(userId, cartId);
        cartMapper.deleteById(cartId);

        clearCache(userId);

        log.info("删除购物车项成功: cartId={}, userId={}", cartId, userId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void batchDeleteCartItems(Long userId, List<Long> cartIds) {
        if (cartIds == null || cartIds.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        // 验证所有购物车项都属于该用户
        for (Long cartId : cartIds) {
            getCartEntity(userId, cartId);
        }

        cartMapper.batchDelete(cartIds);

        clearCache(userId);

        log.info("批量删除购物车项成功: userId={}, cartIds={}", userId, cartIds);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void clearCart(Long userId) {
        cartMapper.clearByUserId(userId);

        clearCache(userId);

        log.info("清空购物车成功: userId={}", userId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void batchDeleteByUserIds(List<Long> userIds) {
        if (userIds == null || userIds.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        cartMapper.batchDeleteByUserIds(userIds);

        // 清除缓存
        for (Long userId : userIds) {
            clearCache(userId);
        }

        log.info("批量删除用户购物车成功: userIds={}", userIds);
    }

    // ==================== 验证 ====================

    @Override
    public boolean belongsToUser(Long userId, Long cartId) {
        Cart cart = cartMapper.selectById(cartId);
        return cart != null && cart.getUserId().equals(userId);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isCartEmpty(Long userId) {
        Integer count = cartMapper.countByUserId(userId);
        return count == null || count == 0;
    }

    // ==================== 私有方法 ====================

    /**
     * 实体转DTO
     */
    private CartDTO convertToDTO(Cart cart) {
        if (cart == null) {
            return null;
        }

        BigDecimal price = cart.getPrice() != null ? cart.getPrice() : BigDecimal.ZERO;
        BigDecimal subtotal = price.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(cart.getQuantity()));

        return CartDTO.builder()
                .cartId(cart.getCartId())
                .userId(cart.getUserId())
                .bookId(cart.getBookId())
                .bookName(cart.getBookName())
                .bookCover(cart.getBookCover())
                .author(cart.getAuthor())
                .price(price)
                .quantity(cart.getQuantity())
                .subtotal(subtotal)
                .stock(cart.getStock())
                .onSale(cart.getOnSale() != null && cart.getOnSale())
                .createTime(cart.getCreateTime())
                .updateTime(cart.getUpdateTime())
                .build();
    }

    /**
     * 清除用户购物车缓存
     */
    private void clearCache(Long userId) {
        redisUtil.delete(REDIS_CART_COUNT_KEY + userId);
        redisUtil.delete(REDIS_CART_KEY + userId);
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Cart\src\main\resources\mapper\CartMapper.xml ====================

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">
<mapper namespace="com.bookmall.cart.mapper.CartMapper">

    <!-- 结果映射 -->
    <resultMap id="BaseResultMap" type="com.bookmall.cart.entity.Cart">
        <id column="cart_id" property="cartId"/>
        <result column="user_id" property="userId"/>
        <result column="book_id" property="bookId"/>
        <result column="quantity" property="quantity"/>
        <result column="create_time" property="createTime"/>
        <result column="update_time" property="updateTime"/>
        <!-- 非数据库字段 -->
        <result column="book_name" property="bookName"/>
        <result column="book_cover" property="bookCover"/>
        <result column="price" property="price"/>
        <result column="stock" property="stock"/>
        <result column="author" property="author"/>
        <result column="on_sale" property="onSale"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 通用查询字段 -->
    <sql id="Base_Column_List">
        c.cart_id, c.user_id, c.book_id, c.quantity, c.create_time, c.update_time
    </sql>

    <!-- 查询购物车列表（含教材信息） -->
    <select id="selectCartListByUserId" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>,
        b.book_name,
        b.cover_image AS book_cover,
        b.price,
        b.stock,
        b.author,
        CASE WHEN b.status = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS on_sale
        FROM tb_cart c
        INNER JOIN tb_book b ON c.book_id = b.book_id
        WHERE c.user_id = #{userId}
        ORDER BY c.update_time DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 批量删除购物车项 -->
    <delete id="batchDelete">
        DELETE FROM tb_cart
        WHERE cart_id IN
        <foreach collection="cartIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </delete>

    <!-- 批量删除用户购物车 -->
    <delete id="batchDeleteByUserIds">
        DELETE FROM tb_cart
        WHERE user_id IN
        <foreach collection="userIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </delete>

</mapper>

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Category\src\main\java\com\bookmall\category\config\CategoryConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.category.config;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.DbType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.MybatisPlusInterceptor;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.inner.PaginationInnerInterceptor;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

/**
 * Category模块配置类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class CategoryConfig {

    /**
     * MyBatis-Plus 分页插件
     */
    @Bean
    public MybatisPlusInterceptor mybatisPlusInterceptor() {
        MybatisPlusInterceptor interceptor = new MybatisPlusInterceptor();
        interceptor.addInnerInterceptor(new PaginationInnerInterceptor(DbType.MYSQL));
        return interceptor;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Category\src\main\java\com\bookmall\category\controller\CategoryController.java ====================

package com.bookmall.category.controller;

import com.bookmall.category.dto.CategoryCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.category.dto.CategoryDTO;
import com.bookmall.category.dto.CategoryListVO;
import com.bookmall.category.entity.Category;
import com.bookmall.category.service.CategoryService;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Parameter;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.validation.Valid;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 分类控制器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/categories")  // 修改：去掉 /api，Gateway 的 StripPrefix=1 会移除 /api 前缀
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "分类接口", description = "教材分类管理")
public class CategoryController {

    private final CategoryService categoryService;

    // ==================== 公开接口 ====================

    /**
     * 获取所有分类列表（含教材数量）
     */
    @GetMapping
    @Operation(summary = "获取所有分类", description = "返回所有分类，含教材数量，按排序升序")
    public Result<List<CategoryDTO>> getCategories() {
        List<CategoryDTO> categories = categoryService.getAllCategories();
        return Result.success(categories);
    }

    /**
     * 获取分类详情
     */
    @GetMapping("/{categoryId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取分类详情")
    public Result<CategoryDTO> getCategoryById(
            @Parameter(description = "分类ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long categoryId) {
        CategoryDTO category = categoryService.getCategoryById(categoryId);
        return Result.success(category);
    }

    /**
     * 获取分类下的教材数量
     */
    @GetMapping("/{categoryId}/book-count")
    @Operation(summary = "获取分类下的教材数量")
    public Result<Integer> getBookCount(
            @Parameter(description = "分类ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long categoryId) {
        Integer count = categoryService.getBookCountByCategoryId(categoryId);
        return Result.success(count);
    }

    // ==================== 管理接口（需要管理员权限） ====================

    /**
     * 创建分类
     */
    @PostMapping
    @Operation(summary = "创建分类", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<CategoryDTO> createCategory(
            @Valid @RequestBody CategoryCreateDTO request) {
        CategoryDTO category = categoryService.createCategory(request);
        return Result.success("创建成功", category);
    }

    /**
     * 更新分类
     */
    @PutMapping("/{categoryId}")
    @Operation(summary = "更新分类", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<CategoryDTO> updateCategory(
            @Parameter(description = "分类ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long categoryId,
            @Valid @RequestBody CategoryCreateDTO request) {
        CategoryDTO category = categoryService.updateCategory(categoryId, request);
        return Result.success("更新成功", category);
    }

    /**
     * 删除分类
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/{categoryId}")
    @Operation(summary = "删除分类", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> deleteCategory(
            @Parameter(description = "分类ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long categoryId) {
        categoryService.deleteCategory(categoryId);
        return Result.success("删除成功");
    }

    /**
     * 批量删除分类
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/batch")
    @Operation(summary = "批量删除分类", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> batchDeleteCategories(
            @RequestBody List<Long> categoryIds) {
        categoryService.batchDeleteCategories(categoryIds);
        return Result.success("批量删除成功");
    }

    /**
     * 更新分类排序
     */
    @PutMapping("/{categoryId}/sort")
    @Operation(summary = "更新分类排序", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> updateSortOrder(
            @Parameter(description = "分类ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long categoryId,
            @RequestParam @NotNull Integer sortOrder) {
        categoryService.updateSortOrder(categoryId, sortOrder);
        return Result.success("排序更新成功");
    }

    /**
     * 检查分类是否存在
     */
    @GetMapping("/exists/{categoryId}")
    @Operation(summary = "检查分类是否存在", description = "内部调用")
    public Result<Boolean> existsById(
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long categoryId) {
        boolean exists = categoryService.existsById(categoryId);
        return Result.success(exists);
    }
}

package com.bookmall.category.entity;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.*;
import lombok.Data;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * 分类实体类 - 对应 tb_category 表
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Data
@TableName("tb_category")
public class Category {

    @TableId(value = "category_id", type = IdType.AUTO)
    private Long categoryId;

    @TableField("category_name")
    private String categoryName;

    @TableField("sort_order")
    private Integer sortOrder;

    @TableField(value = "create_time", fill = FieldFill.INSERT)
    private LocalDateTime createTime;

    /**
     * 该分类下的教材数量（非数据库字段）
     */
    @TableField(exist = false)
    private Integer bookCount;
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Category\src\main\java\com\bookmall\category\mapper\CategoryMapper.java ====================

package com.bookmall.category.mapper;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.bookmall.category.entity.Category;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Update;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 分类数据访问层
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Mapper
public interface CategoryMapper extends BaseMapper<Category> {

    /**
     * 查询所有分类（按排序升序）
     */
    @Select("SELECT category_id, category_name, sort_order, create_time " +
            "FROM tb_category ORDER BY sort_order ASC")
    List<Category> selectAllOrderBySort();

    /**
     * 查询所有分类并统计教材数量
     */
    List<Category> selectAllWithBookCount();

    /**
     * 根据分类名称查询
     */
    @Select("SELECT category_id, category_name, sort_order, create_time " +
            "FROM tb_category WHERE category_name = #{categoryName}")
    Category selectByCategoryName(@Param("categoryName") String categoryName);

    /**
     * 检查分类名称是否存在（排除自身）
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_category " +
            "WHERE category_name = #{categoryName} AND category_id != #{categoryId}")
    int countByNameExcludeId(@Param("categoryName") String categoryName,
                             @Param("categoryId") Long categoryId);

    /**
     * 统计该分类下的教材数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_book WHERE category_id = #{categoryId} AND status = 1")
    int countBooksByCategoryId(@Param("categoryId") Long categoryId);

    /**
     * 批量删除分类（物理删除，XML实现）
     */
    int batchDelete(@Param("categoryIds") List<Long> categoryIds);

    /**
     * 批量更新排序（XML实现）
     */
    int batchUpdateSort(@Param("list") List<Category> categories);

    /**
     * 获取最大排序值
     */
    @Select("SELECT MAX(sort_order) FROM tb_category")
    Integer getMaxSortOrder();

    /**
     * 更新分类名称
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_category SET category_name = #{categoryName} " +
            "WHERE category_id = #{categoryId}")
    int updateName(@Param("categoryId") Long categoryId,
                   @Param("categoryName") String categoryName);

    /**
     * 更新排序
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_category SET sort_order = #{sortOrder} " +
            "WHERE category_id = #{categoryId}")
    int updateSortOrder(@Param("categoryId") Long categoryId,
                        @Param("sortOrder") Integer sortOrder);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Category\src\main\java\com\bookmall\category\service\CategoryService.java ====================

package com.bookmall.category.service;

import com.bookmall.category.dto.CategoryCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.category.dto.CategoryDTO;
import com.bookmall.category.entity.Category;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 分类服务接口
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
public interface CategoryService {

    /**
     * 获取所有分类列表（含教材数量）
     */
    List<CategoryDTO> getAllCategories();

    /**
     * 获取所有分类（简单列表，不含数量）
     */
    List<Category> getAllCategoriesSimple();

    /**
     * 根据ID获取分类详情
     */
    CategoryDTO getCategoryById(Long categoryId);

    /**
     * 根据ID获取分类实体
     */
    Category getCategoryEntityById(Long categoryId);

    /**
     * 创建分类
     */
    CategoryDTO createCategory(CategoryCreateDTO request);

    /**
     * 更新分类
     */
    CategoryDTO updateCategory(Long categoryId, CategoryCreateDTO request);

    /**
     * 删除分类
     */
    void deleteCategory(Long categoryId);

    /**
     * 批量删除分类
     */
    void batchDeleteCategories(List<Long> categoryIds);

    /**
     * 更新排序
     */
    void updateSortOrder(Long categoryId, Integer sortOrder);

    /**
     * 检查分类是否存在
     */
    boolean existsById(Long categoryId);

    /**
     * 获取分类下的教材数量
     */
    Integer getBookCountByCategoryId(Long categoryId);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Category\src\main\java\com\bookmall\category\service\impl\CategoryServiceImpl.java ====================

package com.bookmall.category.service.impl;

import com.bookmall.category.dto.CategoryCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.category.dto.CategoryDTO;
import com.bookmall.category.entity.Category;
import com.bookmall.category.mapper.CategoryMapper;
import com.bookmall.category.service.CategoryService;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.RedisUtil;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * 分类服务实现类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CategoryServiceImpl implements CategoryService {

    private final CategoryMapper categoryMapper;
    private final RedisUtil redisUtil;

    private static final String REDIS_CATEGORY_KEY = "category:list";

    @Override
    public List<CategoryDTO> getAllCategories() {
        // 尝试从Redis获取缓存
        Object cached = redisUtil.get(REDIS_CATEGORY_KEY);
        if (cached != null) {
            try {
                @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
                List<CategoryDTO> cachedList = (List<CategoryDTO>) cached;
                return cachedList;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("Redis缓存解析失败，从数据库查询");
            }
        }

        // 从数据库查询
        List<Category> categories = categoryMapper.selectAllWithBookCount();
        List<CategoryDTO> result = categories.stream()
                .map(this::convertToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());

        // 缓存到Redis（5分钟过期）
        redisUtil.set(REDIS_CATEGORY_KEY, result, 5, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MINUTES);

        return result;
    }

    @Override
    public List<Category> getAllCategoriesSimple() {
        return categoryMapper.selectAllOrderBySort();
    }

    @Override
    public CategoryDTO getCategoryById(Long categoryId) {
        Category category = getCategoryEntityById(categoryId);
        return convertToDTO(category);
    }

    @Override
    public Category getCategoryEntityById(Long categoryId) {
        Category category = categoryMapper.selectById(categoryId);
        if (category == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.CATEGORY_NOT_EXIST);
        }
        return category;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CategoryDTO createCategory(CategoryCreateDTO request) {
        // 检查分类名称是否已存在
        Category existCategory = categoryMapper.selectByCategoryName(request.getCategoryName());
        if (existCategory != null) {
            throw new BusinessException("分类名称已存在");
        }

        // 创建分类
        Category category = new Category();
        category.setCategoryName(request.getCategoryName());
        category.setSortOrder(request.getSortOrder());

        categoryMapper.insert(category);

        // 清除Redis缓存
        redisUtil.delete(REDIS_CATEGORY_KEY);

        log.info("创建分类成功: categoryId={}, categoryName={}",
                category.getCategoryId(), category.getCategoryName());

        return convertToDTO(category);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CategoryDTO updateCategory(Long categoryId, CategoryCreateDTO request) {
        Category category = getCategoryEntityById(categoryId);

        // 检查分类名称是否被其他分类使用
        int count = categoryMapper.countByNameExcludeId(request.getCategoryName(), categoryId);
        if (count > 0) {
            throw new BusinessException("分类名称已被其他分类使用");
        }

        // 更新
        category.setCategoryName(request.getCategoryName());
        category.setSortOrder(request.getSortOrder());
        categoryMapper.updateById(category);

        // 清除Redis缓存
        redisUtil.delete(REDIS_CATEGORY_KEY);

        log.info("更新分类成功: categoryId={}, categoryName={}",
                categoryId, request.getCategoryName());

        return convertToDTO(category);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteCategory(Long categoryId) {
        Category category = getCategoryEntityById(categoryId);

        // 检查分类下是否有教材
        int bookCount = categoryMapper.countBooksByCategoryId(categoryId);
        if (bookCount > 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.CATEGORY_HAS_BOOKS);
        }

        categoryMapper.deleteById(categoryId);

        // 清除Redis缓存
        redisUtil.delete(REDIS_CATEGORY_KEY);

        log.info("删除分类成功: categoryId={}, categoryName={}",
                categoryId, category.getCategoryName());
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void batchDeleteCategories(List<Long> categoryIds) {
        if (categoryIds == null || categoryIds.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        // 检查每个分类下是否有教材
        for (Long categoryId : categoryIds) {
            int bookCount = categoryMapper.countBooksByCategoryId(categoryId);
            if (bookCount > 0) {
                Category category = categoryMapper.selectById(categoryId);
                throw new BusinessException("分类【" + category.getCategoryName() + "】下存在教材，无法删除");
            }
        }

        categoryMapper.batchDelete(categoryIds);

        // 清除Redis缓存
        redisUtil.delete(REDIS_CATEGORY_KEY);

        log.info("批量删除分类成功: categoryIds={}", categoryIds);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updateSortOrder(Long categoryId, Integer sortOrder) {
        Category category = getCategoryEntityById(categoryId);
        category.setSortOrder(sortOrder);
        categoryMapper.updateById(category);

        // 清除Redis缓存
        redisUtil.delete(REDIS_CATEGORY_KEY);

        log.info("更新分类排序成功: categoryId={}, sortOrder={}", categoryId, sortOrder);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean existsById(Long categoryId) {
        return categoryMapper.selectById(categoryId) != null;
    }

    @Override
    public Integer getBookCountByCategoryId(Long categoryId) {
        return categoryMapper.countBooksByCategoryId(categoryId);
    }

    // ==================== 私有方法 ====================

    /**
     * 实体转DTO
     */
    private CategoryDTO convertToDTO(Category category) {
        if (category == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return CategoryDTO.builder()
                .categoryId(category.getCategoryId())
                .categoryName(category.getCategoryName())
                .sortOrder(category.getSortOrder())
                .bookCount(category.getBookCount() != null ? category.getBookCount() : 0)
                .build();
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Category\src\main\resources\mapper\CategoryMapper.xml ====================

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">
<mapper namespace="com.bookmall.category.mapper.CategoryMapper">

    <!-- 结果映射 -->
    <resultMap id="BaseResultMap" type="com.bookmall.category.entity.Category">
        <id column="category_id" property="categoryId"/>
        <result column="category_name" property="categoryName"/>
        <result column="sort_order" property="sortOrder"/>
        <result column="create_time" property="createTime"/>
        <result column="book_count" property="bookCount"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 通用查询字段 -->
    <sql id="Base_Column_List">
        category_id, category_name, sort_order, create_time
    </sql>

    <!-- 查询所有分类并统计教材数量 -->
    <select id="selectAllWithBookCount" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        c.category_id,
        c.category_name,
        c.sort_order,
        c.create_time,
        COUNT(b.book_id) AS book_count
        FROM tb_category c
        LEFT JOIN tb_book b ON c.category_id = b.category_id AND b.status = 1
        GROUP BY c.category_id, c.category_name, c.sort_order, c.create_time
        ORDER BY c.sort_order ASC
    </select>

    <!-- 批量删除分类 -->
    <delete id="batchDelete">
        DELETE FROM tb_category
        WHERE category_id IN
        <foreach collection="categoryIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </delete>

    <!-- 批量更新排序 -->
    <update id="batchUpdateSort">
        <foreach collection="list" item="item" separator=";">
            UPDATE tb_category
            SET sort_order = #{item.sortOrder}
            WHERE category_id = #{item.categoryId}
        </foreach>
    </update>

</mapper>

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\config\FeignConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.config;

import feign.RequestInterceptor;
import feign.RequestTemplate;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnClass;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnWebApplication;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.web.context.request.RequestContextHolder;
import org.springframework.web.context.request.ServletRequestAttributes;

import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;

/**
 * Feign 配置 - 透传认证信息
 *
 * 作用：
 * 微服务之间通过 Feign 调用时，默认不会携带调用方的请求头。
 * 这会导致下游服务的 AuthInterceptor 读取不到 Token，报"请先登录"。
 *
 * 本配置创建一个 Feign 的 RequestInterceptor，
 * 在每次 Feign 调用前，把当前请求的认证信息（X-User-Id、Authorization 等）
 * 透传到 Feign 请求头中，让下游服务能正确识别用户身份。
 *
 * 注意事项：
 * 1. 只在 Servlet Web 环境生效（Gateway 是 WebFlux，不会加载）
 * 2. 使用 @ConditionalOnClass 避免在非 Feign 环境报错
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Configuration
@ConditionalOnClass(RequestInterceptor.class)
@ConditionalOnWebApplication(type = ConditionalOnWebApplication.Type.SERVLET)
public class FeignConfig {

    /**
     * Feign 请求拦截器 - 透传认证信息
     */
    @Bean
    public RequestInterceptor feignTokenInterceptor() {
        return new RequestInterceptor() {
            @Override
            public void apply(RequestTemplate template) {
                ServletRequestAttributes attributes =
                        (ServletRequestAttributes) RequestContextHolder.getRequestAttributes();

                // 非 Web 请求上下文（如定时任务、异步线程），不处理
                if (attributes == null) {
                    return;
                }

                HttpServletRequest request = attributes.getRequest();

                // 1. 透传网关解析后的用户信息（下游服务的 AuthInterceptor 优先读这些）
                String userId = request.getHeader("X-User-Id");
                String username = request.getHeader("X-Username");
                String role = request.getHeader("X-User-Role");

                if (userId != null && !userId.isEmpty()) {
                    template.header("X-User-Id", userId);
                }
                if (username != null && !username.isEmpty()) {
                    template.header("X-Username", username);
                }
                if (role != null && !role.isEmpty()) {
                    template.header("X-User-Role", role);
                }

                // 2. 透传原始 Authorization 头（兼容只认 Authorization 的下游服务）
                String authorization = request.getHeader("Authorization");
                if (authorization != null && !authorization.isEmpty()) {
                    template.header("Authorization", authorization);
                }

                // 3. 透传链路追踪 ID（便于排查问题）
                String traceId = request.getHeader("X-Trace-Id");
                if (traceId != null && !traceId.isEmpty()) {
                    template.header("X-Trace-Id", traceId);
                }

                log.debug("Feign 透传认证信息: url={}, userId={}, hasAuth={}",
                        template.url(), userId, authorization != null);
            }
        };
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\config\GlobalExceptionHandler.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.config;

import com.bookmall.common.constant.StatusCode;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.converter.HttpMessageNotReadableException;
import org.springframework.validation.BindException;
import org.springframework.validation.FieldError;
import org.springframework.web.HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MethodArgumentNotValidException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MissingServletRequestParameterException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ResponseStatus;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.NoHandlerFoundException;

import javax.validation.ConstraintViolation;
import javax.validation.ConstraintViolationException;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * 全局异常处理器
 */
@Slf4j
@RestControllerAdvice
public class GlobalExceptionHandler {

    /**
     * 业务异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(BusinessException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleBusinessException(BusinessException e) {
        log.warn("业务异常: code={}, message={}", e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
        return Result.error(e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
    }

    /**
     * 参数校验异常 - @Valid
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST)
    public Result<Void> handleMethodArgumentNotValidException(MethodArgumentNotValidException e) {
        String message = e.getBindingResult().getFieldErrors().stream()
                .map(FieldError::getDefaultMessage)
                .collect(Collectors.joining("; "));
        log.warn("参数校验失败: {}", message);
        return Result.badRequest(message);
    }

    /**
     * 参数绑定异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(BindException.class)
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST)
    public Result<Void> handleBindException(BindException e) {
        String message = e.getBindingResult().getFieldErrors().stream()
                .map(FieldError::getDefaultMessage)
                .collect(Collectors.joining("; "));
        log.warn("参数绑定失败: {}", message);
        return Result.badRequest(message);
    }

    /**
     * 单个参数校验异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(ConstraintViolationException.class)
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST)
    public Result<Void> handleConstraintViolationException(ConstraintViolationException e) {
        String message = e.getConstraintViolations().stream()
                .map(ConstraintViolation::getMessage)
                .collect(Collectors.joining("; "));
        log.warn("参数校验失败: {}", message);
        return Result.badRequest(message);
    }

    /**
     * 参数缺失异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(MissingServletRequestParameterException.class)
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST)
    public Result<Void> handleMissingServletRequestParameterException(MissingServletRequestParameterException e) {
        log.warn("参数缺失: {}", e.getMessage());
        return Result.badRequest("参数缺失: " + e.getParameterName());
    }

    /**
     * 请求体解析异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(HttpMessageNotReadableException.class)
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST)
    public Result<Void> handleHttpMessageNotReadableException(HttpMessageNotReadableException e) {
        log.warn("请求体解析失败: {}", e.getMessage());
        return Result.badRequest("请求体格式错误");
    }

    /**
     * 请求方法不支持
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException.class)
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED)
    public Result<Void> handleHttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException(HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException e) {
        log.warn("请求方法不支持: {}", e.getMessage());
        return Result.error(StatusCode.METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED, "请求方法不支持: " + e.getMethod());
    }

    /**
     * 404处理
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(NoHandlerFoundException.class)
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND)
    public Result<Void> handleNoHandlerFoundException(NoHandlerFoundException e) {
        log.warn("接口不存在: {}", e.getRequestURL());
        return Result.notFound("接口不存在");
    }

    /**
     * 其他异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
    public Result<Void> handleException(Exception e) {
        log.error("系统异常: ", e);
        return Result.error(ExceptionEnum.SYSTEM_ERROR.getMessage());
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\config\RedisConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.config;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.SerializationFeature;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.datatype.jsr310.JavaTimeModule;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnMissingBean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.data.redis.connection.RedisConnectionFactory;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.redis.serializer.GenericJackson2JsonRedisSerializer;
import org.springframework.data.redis.serializer.StringRedisSerializer;

/**
 * Redis配置类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class RedisConfig {

    /**
     * 配置 RedisTemplate
     * 使用 @ConditionalOnMissingBean 避免与 Spring Boot 自动配置冲突
     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(name = "redisTemplate")
    public RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate(RedisConnectionFactory connectionFactory) {
        RedisTemplate<String, Object> template = new RedisTemplate<>();
        template.setConnectionFactory(connectionFactory);

        // 使用 StringRedisSerializer 序列化 key
        StringRedisSerializer stringSerializer = new StringRedisSerializer();
        template.setKeySerializer(stringSerializer);
        template.setHashKeySerializer(stringSerializer);

        // 创建 ObjectMapper 并注册 JavaTimeModule（支持 LocalDateTime）
        ObjectMapper objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();
        objectMapper.registerModule(new JavaTimeModule());
        objectMapper.disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS);
        // 支持类型信息（用于反序列化时保留类型）
        objectMapper.activateDefaultTyping(
                objectMapper.getPolymorphicTypeValidator(),
                ObjectMapper.DefaultTyping.NON_FINAL
        );

        // 使用 GenericJackson2JsonRedisSerializer 序列化 value（支持 LocalDateTime）
        GenericJackson2JsonRedisSerializer jsonSerializer = new GenericJackson2JsonRedisSerializer(objectMapper);
        template.setValueSerializer(jsonSerializer);
        template.setHashValueSerializer(jsonSerializer);

        template.afterPropertiesSet();
        return template;
    }

    /**
     * 配置 StringRedisTemplate（可选）
     * 如果项目中需要使用 StringRedisTemplate，可以取消注释
     */
    // @Bean
    // @ConditionalOnMissingBean(name = "stringRedisTemplate")
    // public StringRedisTemplate stringRedisTemplate(RedisConnectionFactory connectionFactory) {
    //     return new StringRedisTemplate(connectionFactory);
    // }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\config\WebMvcConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.config;

import com.bookmall.common.interceptor.AuthInterceptor;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnWebApplication;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.CorsRegistry;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.InterceptorRegistry;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.WebMvcConfigurer;

/**
 * Web配置
 * 只在Servlet容器环境（普通Web服务）中加载，Gateway（Reactive环境）不会加载
 *
 * 路径说明：
 * - Gateway 统一使用 StripPrefix=1，将 /api/xxx 转为 /xxx
 * - 服务内部拦截器匹配的是 Gateway 转发后的路径（不含 /api 前缀）
 * - 所以拦截器路径配置为 /**，白名单配置为 /xxx/** 格式
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@ConditionalOnWebApplication(type = ConditionalOnWebApplication.Type.SERVLET)
public class WebMvcConfig implements WebMvcConfigurer {

    private final AuthInterceptor authInterceptor;

    @Override
    public void addInterceptors(InterceptorRegistry registry) {
        registry.addInterceptor(authInterceptor)
                // ✅ 修改：拦截所有请求（Gateway 已剥离 /api 前缀）
                .addPathPatterns("/**")
                .excludePathPatterns(
                        // ============================================================
                        // 白名单路径（不需要 Token 验证）
                        // 注意：这些路径是 Gateway 转发后的内部路径，不含 /api 前缀
                        // 前端请求 /api/xxx → Gateway StripPrefix=1 → /xxx
                        // ============================================================

                        // ===== 用户认证接口 =====
                        "/user/login",
                        "/user/register",
                        "/user/captcha",
                        "/user/forgot-password",
                        "/user/send-code",
                        "/user/check-username",

                        // ===== 用户内部调用（Feign 调用，不需要 Token） =====
                        "/user/exists/**",
                        "/user/info/**",
                        "/user/balance/**",

                        // ===== 管理员认证接口 =====
                        "/admin/login",

                        // ===== 商品公开接口 =====
                        "/books/**",
                        "/categories/**",

                        // ===== 评价公开接口（列表/详情不需要登录） =====
                        "/review/book/**",
                        "/review/latest",
                        "/review/statistics/**",

                        // ===== 支付回调（外部系统调用） =====
                        "/payment/callback/**",

                        // ===== ✅ 购物车服务内部调用（Feign 调用，不需要 Token） =====
                        "/cart/**",

                        // ===== ✅ 地址服务内部调用（Feign 调用，不需要 Token） =====
                        "/address/**",

                        // ===== Swagger API 文档 =====
                        "/swagger-ui/**",
                        "/v3/api-docs/**",
                        "/doc.html",
                        "/webjars/**",

                        // ===== 其他公共资源 =====
                        "/favicon.ico",
                        "/error",
                        "/actuator/**",

                        // ===== Druid 监控 =====
                        "/druid/**"
                );
    }

    @Override
    public void addCorsMappings(CorsRegistry registry) {
        registry.addMapping("/**")
                .allowedOriginPatterns("*")
                .allowedMethods("GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "OPTIONS")
                .allowedHeaders("*")
                .allowCredentials(true)
                .maxAge(3600);
    }
}

package com.bookmall.common.constant;

/**
 * 状态码常量
 *
 * 规范：
 * 200: 成功
 * 400-499: 客户端错误
 * 500-599: 服务端错误
 * 1000-1999: 用户相关
 * 2000-2999: 商品相关
 * 3000-3999: 订单相关
 * 4000-4999: 支付相关
 * 5000-5999: 购物车相关
 * 6000-6999: 分类相关
 * 7000-7999: 验证码相关
 * 8000-8999: 地址相关
 * 9000-9999: 评价相关
 */
public class StatusCode {

    // ========== 成功 ==========
    public static final int SUCCESS = 200;

    // ========== 商品相关 (2000-2999) ==========

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\entity\PageResult.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.entity;

import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.media.Schema;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;

import java.io.Serializable;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 分页响应结果
 */
@Data
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Schema(description = "分页响应结果")
public class PageResult<T> implements Serializable {

    private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;

    @Schema(description = "总记录数")
    private Long total;

    @Schema(description = "当前页码")
    private Integer pageNum;

    @Schema(description = "每页大小")
    private Integer pageSize;

    @Schema(description = "总页数")
    private Integer totalPages;

    @Schema(description = "数据列表")
    private List<T> list;

    public PageResult(Long total, Integer pageNum, Integer pageSize, List<T> list) {
        this.total = total;
        this.pageNum = pageNum;
        this.pageSize = pageSize;
        this.totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) total / pageSize);
        this.list = list;
    }

    /**
     * 空分页
     */
    public static <T> PageResult<T> empty() {
        return new PageResult<>(0L, 1, 10, Collections.emptyList());
    }

    /**
     * 构建分页结果 (MyBatis-Plus Page)
     */
    public static <T> PageResult<T> of(com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page<T> page) {
        return new PageResult<>(
                page.getTotal(),
                (int) page.getCurrent(),
                (int) page.getSize(),
                page.getRecords()
        );
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\entity\Result.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.entity;

import com.bookmall.common.constant.StatusCode;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.media.Schema;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;

import java.io.Serializable;

/**
 * 统一响应结果
 *
 * @param <T> 数据类型
 */
@Data
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Schema(description = "统一响应结果")
public class Result<T> implements Serializable {

    private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;

    @Schema(description = "状态码", example = "200")
    private int code;

    @Schema(description = "提示信息", example = "操作成功")
    private String message;

    @Schema(description = "数据")
    private T data;

    @Schema(description = "时间戳")
    private long timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();

    // ========== 成功响应 ==========
    public static <T> Result<T> success() {
        return new Result<>(StatusCode.SUCCESS, "操作成功", null, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> success(String message) {
        return new Result<>(StatusCode.SUCCESS, message, null, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> success(T data) {
        return new Result<>(StatusCode.SUCCESS, "操作成功", data, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> success(String message, T data) {
        return new Result<>(StatusCode.SUCCESS, message, data, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> success(int code, String message, T data) {
        return new Result<>(code, message, data, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    // ========== 失败响应 ==========
    public static <T> Result<T> error() {
        return new Result<>(StatusCode.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "操作失败", null, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> error(String message) {
        return new Result<>(StatusCode.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, message, null, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> error(int code, String message) {
        return new Result<>(code, message, null, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> error(int code, String message, T data) {
        return new Result<>(code, message, data, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    // ========== 快捷错误方法 ==========
    public static <T> Result<T> badRequest(String message) {
        return error(StatusCode.BAD_REQUEST, message);
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> unauthorized(String message) {
        return error(StatusCode.UNAUTHORIZED, message);
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> forbidden(String message) {
        return error(StatusCode.FORBIDDEN, message);
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> notFound(String message) {
        return error(StatusCode.NOT_FOUND, message);
    }

    public static <T> Result<T> businessError(String message) {
        return error(StatusCode.BUSINESS_ERROR, message);
    }

    /**
     * 判断是否成功
     */
    public boolean isSuccess() {
        return this.code == StatusCode.SUCCESS;
    }
}

package com.bookmall.common.exception;

import lombok.Data;
import lombok.EqualsAndHashCode;

/**
 * 业务异常
 */
@Data
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class BusinessException extends RuntimeException {

    private final int code;
    private final String message;

    public BusinessException(String message) {
        super(message);
        this.code = 1001;
        this.message = message;
    }

    public BusinessException(int code, String message) {
        super(message);
        this.code = code;
        this.message = message;
    }

    public BusinessException(ExceptionEnum exceptionEnum) {
        super(exceptionEnum.getMessage());
        this.code = exceptionEnum.getCode();
        this.message = exceptionEnum.getMessage();
    }

    public BusinessException(ExceptionEnum exceptionEnum, String extraMessage) {
        super(exceptionEnum.getMessage() + ": " + extraMessage);
        this.code = exceptionEnum.getCode();
        this.message = exceptionEnum.getMessage() + ": " + extraMessage;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\interceptor\AuthInterceptor.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.interceptor;

import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.JwtUtil;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnWebApplication;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.HandlerInterceptor;

import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 认证拦截器
 * 只在Servlet容器环境（普通Web服务）中加载，Gateway（Reactive环境）不会加载
 *
 * 路径说明：
 * - Book 服务的 Controller 映射为 @RequestMapping("/books")
 * - 前端经 Gateway 访问时，Gateway StripPrefix=1 会去掉 /api，Book 服务收到 /books/**
 * - Feign 内部调用时，直接请求 /books/**
 * - 因此白名单要同时兼容 /books/** 和 /api/books/** 两种形式
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
@ConditionalOnWebApplication(type = ConditionalOnWebApplication.Type.SERVLET)
public class AuthInterceptor implements HandlerInterceptor {

    /**
     * 白名单路径 - 不需要Token验证
     * 注意：以 "/" 结尾的为前缀匹配，其余为精确匹配
     */
    private static final Set<String> WHITE_LIST = new HashSet<>(Arrays.asList(
            // ===== 静态资源（图片、样式、脚本等） =====
            "/images/",
            "/css/",
            "/js/",
            "/fonts/",
            "/static/",
            "/public/",

            // ===== 公开测试接口 =====
            "/api/user/test",
            "/api/user/status",
            "/api/user/welcome",
            "/api/user/endpoints",

            // ===== 认证接口（登录/注册不需要Token） =====
            "/api/user/login",
            "/api/user/register",
            "/api/user/check-username",

            // ===== Book 公开接口（浏览商品不需要登录） =====
            // 兼容两种前缀：/books/**（Book 服务真实路径）和 /api/books/**（Gateway 未 StripPrefix 时）
            "/books",
            "/books/",
            "/books/hot",
            "/books/new",
            "/books/page",
            "/books/detail/",
            "/books/search",
            "/books/category/",

            "/api/books",
            "/api/books/",
            "/api/books/hot",
            "/api/books/new",
            "/api/books/page",
            "/api/books/detail/",
            "/api/books/search",
            "/api/books/category/",

            // 分类接口
            "/categories/list",
            "/api/categories/list",

            // ===== Swagger API文档 =====
            "/swagger-ui.html",
            "/swagger-ui/",
            "/v3/api-docs",
            "/v3/api-docs/",
            "/doc.html",
            "/webjars/",

            // ===== Druid监控 =====
            "/druid",
            "/druid/",

            // ===== 其他公共资源 =====
            "/favicon.ico",
            "/error",
            "/actuator/health",
            "/actuator/info"
    ));

    /**
     * 图书库存/上架/价格等带动态ID的公开查询接口
     * 同时兼容带 /api 和不带 /api 两种形式：
     *   - /books/3/stock
     *   - /api/books/3/stock
     */
    private static final String BOOK_DYNAMIC_PATH_REGEX =
            "^(/api)?/books/\\d+/(stock|on-sale|price)$";

    @Override
    public boolean preHandle(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response, Object handler) {
        String path = request.getRequestURI();
        log.debug("拦截请求: {} {}", request.getMethod(), path);

        // 1. 检查是否在白名单中（精确匹配、前缀匹配、正则匹配）
        if (isWhiteListed(path)) {
            log.debug("白名单路径，放行: {}", path);
            return true;
        }

        // 2. 检查Token
        String header = request.getHeader(Constants.TOKEN_HEADER);

        // 检查Token是否存在
        if (!StringUtils.hasText(header)) {
            log.warn("请求缺少Token: {}", request.getRequestURI());
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.LOGIN_REQUIRED);
        }

        // 去掉Bearer前缀
        String token = header;
        if (token.startsWith(Constants.TOKEN_PREFIX)) {
            token = token.substring(Constants.TOKEN_PREFIX.length());
        }

        // 验证Token
        if (!JwtUtil.validateToken(token)) {
            log.warn("Token无效或已过期: {}", token);
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.TOKEN_INVALID);
        }

        // 从Token获取用户信息并存入请求上下文
        Long userId = JwtUtil.getUserIdFromToken(token);
        String username = JwtUtil.getUsernameFromToken(token);
        String role = JwtUtil.getRoleFromToken(token);

        request.setAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID, userId);
        request.setAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USERNAME, username);
        request.setAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_ROLE, role);

        log.debug("认证通过: userId={}, username={}, role={}", userId, username, role);
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * 判断路径是否在白名单中
     * 支持三种匹配方式：
     * 1. 精确匹配（如 /books/hot）
     * 2. 前缀匹配（以 "/" 结尾的白名单项，如 /images/）
     * 3. 正则匹配（针对带动态ID的公开接口，如 /books/3/stock）
     */
    private boolean isWhiteListed(String path) {
        // 1. 精确匹配
        if (WHITE_LIST.contains(path)) {
            return true;
        }

        // 2. 前缀匹配（白名单项以 "/" 结尾）
        for (String whitePath : WHITE_LIST) {
            if (whitePath.endsWith("/") && path.startsWith(whitePath)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        // 3. 正则匹配：/books/{id}/stock、/books/{id}/on-sale、/books/{id}/price
        //    同时兼容 /api/books/{id}/... 形式
        if (path.matches(BOOK_DYNAMIC_PATH_REGEX)) {
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }
}

package com.bookmall.common.utils;

import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;

/**
 * BCrypt加密工具
 */
public class BCryptUtil {

    private static final BCryptPasswordEncoder ENCODER = new BCryptPasswordEncoder();

    /**
     * 加密密码
     */
    public static String encode(String rawPassword) {
        return ENCODER.encode(rawPassword);
    }

    /**
     * 校验密码
     */
    public static boolean matches(String rawPassword, String encodedPassword) {
        return ENCODER.matches(rawPassword, encodedPassword);
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\utils\JwtUtil.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.utils;

import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import io.jsonwebtoken.Claims;
import io.jsonwebtoken.Jwts;
import io.jsonwebtoken.SignatureAlgorithm;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import java.util.Date;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.function.Function;

/**
 * JWT工具类
 */
@Slf4j
public class JwtUtil {

    /**
     * 生成Token
     */
    public static String generateToken(Long userId, String username, String role) {
        Map<String, Object> claims = new HashMap<>();
        claims.put("userId", userId);
        claims.put("username", username);
        claims.put("role", role);

        Date now = new Date();
        Date expiration = new Date(now.getTime() + Constants.JWT_EXPIRATION);

        return Jwts.builder()
                .setClaims(claims)
                .setSubject(String.valueOf(userId))
                .setIssuedAt(now)
                .setExpiration(expiration)
                .signWith(SignatureAlgorithm.HS256, Constants.JWT_SECRET)
                .compact();
    }

    /**
     * 从Token中获取用户ID
     */
    public static Long getUserIdFromToken(String token) {
        try {
            Claims claims = parseToken(token);
            return claims.get("userId", Long.class);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("解析Token获取userId失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 从Token中获取用户名
     */
    public static String getUsernameFromToken(String token) {
        try {
            Claims claims = parseToken(token);
            return claims.get("username", String.class);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("解析Token获取username失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 从Token中获取角色
     */
    public static String getRoleFromToken(String token) {
        try {
            Claims claims = parseToken(token);
            return claims.get("role", String.class);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("解析Token获取role失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 验证Token是否有效
     */
    public static Boolean validateToken(String token) {
        try {
            Claims claims = parseToken(token);
            return !claims.getExpiration().before(new Date());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Token验证失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 从Token中获取过期时间
     */
    public static Date getExpirationDateFromToken(String token) {
        return getClaimFromToken(token, Claims::getExpiration);
    }

    /**
     * 获取Token中的指定字段
     */
    public static <T> T getClaimFromToken(String token, Function<Claims, T> claimsResolver) {
        final Claims claims = parseToken(token);
        return claimsResolver.apply(claims);
    }

    /**
     * 解析Token
     */
    private static Claims parseToken(String token) {
        return Jwts.parser()
                .setSigningKey(Constants.JWT_SECRET)
                .parseClaimsJws(token)
                .getBody();
    }

    /**
     * 判断Token是否过期
     */
    public static Boolean isTokenExpired(String token) {
        final Date expiration = getExpirationDateFromToken(token);
        return expiration.before(new Date());
    }

    /**
     * 刷新Token
     */
    public static String refreshToken(String token) {
        if (!validateToken(token)) {
            return null;
        }
        Claims claims = parseToken(token);
        Long userId = claims.get("userId", Long.class);
        String username = claims.get("username", String.class);
        String role = claims.get("role", String.class);
        return generateToken(userId, username, role);
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\utils\RedisUtil.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.utils;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

/**
 * Redis工具类
 * 提供常用的Redis操作封装
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class RedisUtil {

    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;

    // ==================== String 操作 ====================

    /**
     * 设置值（永久）
     */
    public void set(String key, Object value) {
        redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(key, value);
    }

    /**
     * 设置值（带过期时间）
     */
    public void set(String key, Object value, long timeout, TimeUnit unit) {
        redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(key, value, timeout, unit);
    }

    /**
     * 获取值
     */
    public Object get(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(key);
    }

    /**
     * 获取字符串值
     */
    public String getString(String key) {
        Object value = get(key);
        return value != null ? value.toString() : null;
    }

    /**
     * 获取Long值
     */
    public Long getLong(String key) {
        Object value = get(key);
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }
        if (value instanceof Number) {
            return ((Number) value).longValue();
        }
        try {
            return Long.valueOf(value.toString());
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 获取Integer值
     */
    public Integer getInteger(String key) {
        Object value = get(key);
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }
        if (value instanceof Number) {
            return ((Number) value).intValue();
        }
        try {
            return Integer.valueOf(value.toString());
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 原子性设置值（仅当Key不存在时）
     */
    public Boolean setIfAbsent(String key, Object value) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(key, value);
    }

    /**
     * 原子性设置值（仅当Key不存在时，带过期时间）
     */
    public Boolean setIfAbsent(String key, Object value, long timeout, TimeUnit unit) {
        Boolean result = redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(key, value);
        if (result != null && result) {
            expire(key, timeout, unit);
        }
        return result != null && result;
    }

    /**
     * 原子性设置值（仅当Key存在时）
     */
    public Boolean setIfPresent(String key, Object value) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfPresent(key, value);
    }

    /**
     * 原子性设置值（仅当Key存在时，带过期时间）
     */
    public Boolean setIfPresent(String key, Object value, long timeout, TimeUnit unit) {
        Boolean result = redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfPresent(key, value);
        if (result != null && result) {
            expire(key, timeout, unit);
        }
        return result != null && result;
    }

    /**
     * 值自增（原子操作）
     */
    public Long increment(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(key);
    }

    /**
     * 值自增指定步长
     */
    public Long increment(String key, long delta) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(key, delta);
    }

    /**
     * 值自增指定步长（浮点数）
     */
    public Double increment(String key, double delta) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(key, delta);
    }

    /**
     * 值自减
     */
    public Long decrement(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().decrement(key);
    }

    /**
     * 值自减指定步长
     */
    public Long decrement(String key, long delta) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().decrement(key, delta);
    }

    /**
     * 批量获取
     */
    public List<Object> multiGet(Collection<String> keys) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().multiGet(keys);
    }

    /**
     * 批量设置
     */
    public void multiSet(Map<String, Object> map) {
        redisTemplate.opsForValue().multiSet(map);
    }

    // ==================== Hash 操作 ====================

    /**
     * 设置Hash值
     */
    public void hSet(String key, String hashKey, Object value) {
        redisTemplate.opsForHash().put(key, hashKey, value);
    }

    /**
     * 批量设置Hash值
     */
    public void hSetAll(String key, Map<String, Object> map) {
        redisTemplate.opsForHash().putAll(key, map);
    }

    /**
     * 获取Hash值
     */
    public Object hGet(String key, String hashKey) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForHash().get(key, hashKey);
    }

    /**
     * 获取所有Hash值
     */
    public Map<Object, Object> hGetAll(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForHash().entries(key);
    }

    /**
     * 获取Hash所有键
     */
    public Set<Object> hKeys(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForHash().keys(key);
    }

    /**
     * 获取Hash所有值
     */
    public List<Object> hValues(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForHash().values(key);
    }

    /**
     * 删除Hash键
     */
    public Long hDelete(String key, Object... hashKeys) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForHash().delete(key, hashKeys);
    }

    /**
     * 判断Hash键是否存在
     */
    public Boolean hHasKey(String key, String hashKey) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForHash().hasKey(key, hashKey);
    }

    /**
     * Hash值自增
     */
    public Long hIncrement(String key, String hashKey, long delta) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForHash().increment(key, hashKey, delta);
    }

    /**
     * Hash值自增（浮点数）
     */
    public Double hIncrement(String key, String hashKey, double delta) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForHash().increment(key, hashKey, delta);
    }

    // ==================== List 操作 ====================

    /**

    public Long lPush(String key, Object value) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForList().leftPush(key, value);
    }

    /**

    }

     */
    public Set<Object> zRangeByScore(String key, double min, double max) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForZSet().rangeByScore(key, min, max);
    }

    /**
     * 获取ZSet大小
     */
    public Long zSize(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForZSet().zCard(key);
    }

    // ==================== Key 操作 ====================

    /**
     * 删除键
     */
    public Boolean delete(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.delete(key);
    }

    /**
     * 批量删除键
     */
    public Long delete(Collection<String> keys) {
        return redisTemplate.delete(keys);
    }

     */
    public Boolean hasKey(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.hasKey(key);
    }

    /**
     * 设置过期时间
     */
    public Boolean expire(String key, long timeout, TimeUnit unit) {
        return redisTemplate.expire(key, timeout, unit);
    }

    /**
     * 设置过期时间（指定日期）
     */
    public Boolean expireAt(String key, java.util.Date date) {
        return redisTemplate.expireAt(key, date);
    }

    /**
     * 获取剩余过期时间
     */
    public Long getExpire(String key, TimeUnit unit) {
        return redisTemplate.getExpire(key, unit);
    }

    /**
     * 获取剩余过期时间（秒）
     */
    public Long getExpire(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.getExpire(key);
    }

    /**
     * 获取键类型
     */
    public String getType(String key) {
        return redisTemplate.type(key).name();
    }

    /**
     * 扫描匹配的键
     */
    public Set<String> keys(String pattern) {
        return redisTemplate.keys(pattern);
    }

    /**
     * 重命名键
     */
    public void rename(String oldKey, String newKey) {
        redisTemplate.rename(oldKey, newKey);
    }

    /**
     * 清空当前数据库
     */
    public void flushDb() {
        redisTemplate.getConnectionFactory().getConnection().flushDb();
    }

    // ==================== 分布式锁 ====================

    /**
     * 获取分布式锁（自旋等待）
     */
    public boolean tryLock(String lockKey, long timeout, TimeUnit unit) {
        long end = System.currentTimeMillis() + unit.toMillis(timeout);
        while (System.currentTimeMillis() < end) {
            Boolean success = setIfAbsent(lockKey, "1", timeout, unit);
            if (success != null && success) {
                return true;
            }
            try {
                Thread.sleep(50);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                return false;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * 释放分布式锁
     */
    public void unlock(String lockKey) {
        delete(lockKey);
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Common\src\main\java\com\bookmall\common\utils\SnowflakeIdWorker.java ====================

package com.bookmall.common.utils;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * 雪花算法ID生成器
 */
@Component
public class SnowflakeIdWorker {

    // 起始时间戳 (2024-01-01)
    private final long twepoch = 1704067200000L;

    // 机器ID位数
    private final long workerIdBits = 5L;
    // 数据中心ID位数
    private final long datacenterIdBits = 5L;
    // 序列号位数
    private final long sequenceBits = 12L;

    // 最大机器ID
    private final long maxWorkerId = -1L ^ (-1L << workerIdBits);
    // 最大数据中心ID
    private final long maxDatacenterId = -1L ^ (-1L << datacenterIdBits);

    // 移位
    private final long workerIdShift = sequenceBits;
    private final long datacenterIdShift = sequenceBits + workerIdBits;
    private final long timestampLeftShift = sequenceBits + workerIdBits + datacenterIdBits;

    // 序列号掩码
    private final long sequenceMask = -1L ^ (-1L << sequenceBits);

    private long workerId;
    private long datacenterId;
    private long sequence = 0L;
    private long lastTimestamp = -1L;

    public SnowflakeIdWorker() {
        this.workerId = 0;
        this.datacenterId = 0;
    }

    public SnowflakeIdWorker(long workerId, long datacenterId) {
        if (workerId > maxWorkerId || workerId < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(String.format("workerId can't be greater than %d or less than 0", maxWorkerId));
        }
        if (datacenterId > maxDatacenterId || datacenterId < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(String.format("datacenterId can't be greater than %d or less than 0", maxDatacenterId));
        }
        this.workerId = workerId;
        this.datacenterId = datacenterId;
    }

    public synchronized long nextId() {
        long timestamp = timeGen();

        // 时钟回拨处理
        if (timestamp < lastTimestamp) {
            throw new RuntimeException(String.format("Clock moved backwards. Refusing to generate id for %d milliseconds", lastTimestamp - timestamp));
        }

        if (lastTimestamp == timestamp) {
            sequence = (sequence + 1) & sequenceMask;
            if (sequence == 0) {
                timestamp = tilNextMillis(lastTimestamp);
            }
        } else {
            sequence = 0L;
        }

        lastTimestamp = timestamp;

        return ((timestamp - twepoch) << timestampLeftShift)
                | (datacenterId << datacenterIdShift)
                | (workerId << workerIdShift)
                | sequence;
    }

    protected long tilNextMillis(long lastTimestamp) {
        long timestamp = timeGen();
        while (timestamp <= lastTimestamp) {
            timestamp = timeGen();
        }
        return timestamp;
    }

    protected long timeGen() {
        return System.currentTimeMillis();
    }
}
