<template>
  <div class="home">
    <!-- 轮播图 -->
    <div class="banner">
      <el-carousel height="400px" indicator-position="outside">
        <el-carousel-item v-for="(item, index) in banners" :key="index">
          <div class="banner-item" :style="{ background: item.bg }">
            <h2>{{ item.title }}</h2>
            <p>{{ item.subtitle }}</p>
          </div>
        </el-carousel-item>
      </el-carousel>
    </div>

    <!-- 分类导航 -->
    <div class="categories" v-if="categories.length > 0">
      <div class="section-title">
        <h2>📂 教材分类</h2>
      </div>
      <el-row :gutter="16">
        <el-col :span="3" v-for="cat in categories" :key="cat.categoryId">
          <div class="category-item" @click="goToCategory(cat.categoryId)">
            <div class="category-icon">📖</div>
            <span>{{ cat.categoryName }}</span>
          </div>
        </el-col>
      </el-row>
    </div>

    <!-- 热门教材 -->
    <div class="section" v-if="hotBooks.length > 0">
      <div class="section-header">
        <h2>🔥 热门教材</h2>
        <el-link type="primary" @click="goToBooks">查看更多 →</el-link>
      </div>
      <el-row :gutter="20">
        <el-col :span="6" v-for="book in hotBooks" :key="book.bookId">
          <BookCard :book="book" @click="goToDetail(book.bookId)" />
        </el-col>
      </el-row>
    </div>

    <!-- 新品推荐 -->
    <div class="section" v-if="newBooks.length > 0">
      <div class="section-header">
        <h2>✨ 新品推荐</h2>
        <el-link type="primary" @click="goToBooks">查看更多 →</el-link>
      </div>
      <el-row :gutter="20">
        <el-col :span="6" v-for="book in newBooks" :key="book.bookId">
          <BookCard :book="book" @click="goToDetail(book.bookId)" />
        </el-col>
      </el-row>
    </div>
    
    <!-- 空状态 -->
    <el-empty 
      v-if="!loading && hotBooks.length === 0 && newBooks.length === 0" 
      description="暂无教材数据" 
    />
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { bookApi } from '@/api/book'
import { categoryApi } from '@/api/category'
import BookCard from '@/components/BookCard.vue'

const router = useRouter()
const hotBooks = ref([])
const newBooks = ref([])
const categories = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)

const banners = [
  { 
    title: '📚 教材大促', 
    subtitle: '全场低至5折起，限时抢购', 
    bg: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%)' 
  },
  { 
    title: '🎓 新学期新教材', 
    subtitle: '精选教材，助力学习进步', 
    bg: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #f093fb 0%, #f5576c 100%)' 
  },
  { 
    title: '📖 经典教材推荐', 
    subtitle: '名师推荐，品质保证', 
    bg: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #4facfe 0%, #00f2fe 100%)' 
  }
]

const fetchData = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const [hotRes, newRes, categoryRes] = await Promise.all([
      bookApi.getHotBooks(8),
      bookApi.getNewBooks(8),
      categoryApi.getCategories()
    ])
    
    // 处理热门图书
    if (hotRes && hotRes.code === 200) {
      hotBooks.value = hotRes.data || []
    } else if (Array.isArray(hotRes)) {
      hotBooks.value = hotRes
    } else {
      hotBooks.value = []
    }
    
    // 处理新品图书
    if (newRes && newRes.code === 200) {
      newBooks.value = newRes.data || []
    } else if (Array.isArray(newRes)) {
      newBooks.value = newRes
    } else {
      newBooks.value = []
    }
    
    // 处理分类
    if (categoryRes && categoryRes.code === 200) {
      categories.value = categoryRes.data || []
    } else if (Array.isArray(categoryRes)) {
      categories.value = categoryRes
    } else {
      categories.value = []
    }
    
    console.log('分类数据:', categories.value)
    console.log('热门图书:', hotBooks.value)
    console.log('新品图书:', newBooks.value)
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('加载首页数据失败:', error)
    hotBooks.value = []
    newBooks.value = []
    categories.value = []
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const goToDetail = (bookId) => router.push(`/book/${bookId}`)
const goToCategory = (categoryId) => router.push(`/books?category=${categoryId}`)
const goToBooks = () => router.push('/books')

onMounted(fetchData)
</script>

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\frontend\src\views\Order.vue ====================

<template>
  <div class="order-page">
    <h1 class="page-title">📦 我的订单</h1>
    
    <!-- 状态筛选 -->
    <div class="order-tabs">
      <el-tabs v-model="activeStatus" @tab-change="handleTabChange">
        <el-tab-pane label="全部订单" name="all" />
        <el-tab-pane label="待付款" name="0" />
        <el-tab-pane label="待发货" name="1" />
        <el-tab-pane label="待收货" name="2" />
        <el-tab-pane label="已完成" name="3" />
        <el-tab-pane label="已取消" name="4" />
      </el-tabs>
    </div>
    
    <!-- 订单列表 -->
    <div v-loading="loading">
      <div v-if="orderList.length > 0">
        <div class="order-item" v-for="order in orderList" :key="order.orderId">
          <div class="order-header">
            <span class="order-id">订单号：{{ order.orderId }}</span>
            <span class="order-status" :class="getStatusClass(order.orderStatus)">
              {{ getStatusText(order.orderStatus) }}
            </span>
          </div>
          
          <div class="order-body">
            <div class="order-items">
              <div class="order-product" v-for="item in (order.items || [])" :key="item.itemId">
                <img :src="item.bookCover || '/default-cover.jpg'" :alt="item.bookName" />
                <div class="product-info">
                  <span class="product-name">{{ item.bookName }}</span>
                  <span class="product-price">¥{{ safeToFixed(item.price) }} × {{ item.quantity }}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="order-summary">
              <span>共 {{ order.items?.length || 0 }} 件商品</span>
              <span class="order-total">实付：¥{{ safeToFixed(order.totalAmount) }}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div class="order-footer">
            <span class="order-time">下单时间：{{ order.createTime || '-' }}</span>
            <div class="order-actions">
              <el-button size="small" @click="viewDetail(order.orderId)">查看详情</el-button>
              <el-button 
                v-if="order.orderStatus === 0" 
                type="primary" 
                size="small" 
                @click="handlePay(order.orderId)"
              >
                立即支付
              </el-button>
              <el-button 
                v-if="order.orderStatus === 0" 
                size="small" 
                @click="handleCancel(order.orderId)"
              >
                取消订单
              </el-button>
              <el-button 
                v-if="order.orderStatus === 2" 
                type="success" 
                size="small" 
                @click="handleReceive(order.orderId)"
              >
                确认收货
              </el-button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <el-empty v-else description="暂无订单" />
    </div>
    
    <!-- 分页 -->
    <div class="pagination-wrapper" v-if="total > 0">
      <el-pagination
        v-model:page-num="pageNum"
        v-model:page-size="pageSize"
        :total="total"
        :page-sizes="[5, 10, 20]"
        layout="total, sizes, prev, pager, next"
        @size-change="fetchOrders"
        @current-change="fetchOrders"
      />
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, watch } from 'vue'
import { useRouter, useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { orderApi } from '@/api/order'

const router = useRouter()
const route = useRoute()

const loading = ref(false)
const orderList = ref([])
const total = ref(0)
const pageNum = ref(1)
const pageSize = ref(5)

// ✅ 从 URL 参数读取状态，默认为 'all'
const activeStatus = ref(route.query.status || 'all')

const statusMap = {
  0: { text: '待付款', class: 'pending' },
  1: { text: '待发货', class: 'paid' },
  2: { text: '待收货', class: 'shipped' },
  3: { text: '已完成', class: 'completed' },
  4: { text: '已取消', class: 'cancelled' }
}

const getStatusText = (status) => statusMap[status]?.text || '未知'
const getStatusClass = (status) => statusMap[status]?.class || ''

/**
 * ✅ 安全格式化数字
 */
const safeToFixed = (value, digits = 2) => {
  if (value === undefined || value === null || isNaN(value)) {
    return '0.00'
  }
  const num = Number(value)
  if (isNaN(num)) {
    return '0.00'
  }
  return num.toFixed(digits)
}

const fetchOrders = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const params = {
      pageNum: pageNum.value,
      pageSize: pageSize.value
    }
    if (activeStatus.value !== 'all') {
      params.status = Number(activeStatus.value)
    }
    
    // ✅ 添加调试日志
    console.log('📦 请求订单参数:', params)
    
    const result = await orderApi.getOrderList(params)
    
    // ✅ 添加调试日志
    console.log('📦 订单响应:', result)
    
    // ✅ 适配不同的响应格式
    if (result && result.list !== undefined) {
      orderList.value = result.list || []
      total.value = result.total || 0
    } else if (result && result.data && result.data.list !== undefined) {
      orderList.value = result.data.list || []
      total.value = result.data.total || 0
    } else {
      orderList.value = []
      total.value = 0
    }
    
    // ✅ 打印当前标签页和订单数量
    console.log(`📦 当前标签: ${activeStatus.value}, 订单数量: ${orderList.value.length}, 总数: ${total.value}`)
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ 获取订单列表失败:', error)
    orderList.value = []
    total.value = 0
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const handleTabChange = () => {
  console.log(`📦 切换到标签: ${activeStatus.value}`)
  pageNum.value = 1
  fetchOrders()
}

const viewDetail = (orderId) => {
  router.push(`/order/${orderId}`)
}

const handlePay = async (orderId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要支付该订单吗？', '提示', {
      confirmButtonText: '确定支付',
      cancelButtonText: '取消',
      type: 'info'
    })
    await orderApi.payOrder(orderId)
    ElMessage.success('支付成功')
    fetchOrders()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      console.error('❌ 支付失败:', error)
      ElMessage.error(error.message || '支付失败')
    }
  }
}

const handleCancel = async (orderId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要取消该订单吗？', '提示', {
      confirmButtonText: '确定取消',
      cancelButtonText: '取消',
      type: 'warning'
    })
    await orderApi.cancelOrder(orderId)
    ElMessage.success('订单已取消')
    fetchOrders()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      console.error('❌ 取消失败:', error)
      ElMessage.error(error.message || '取消失败')
    }
  }
}

const handleReceive = async (orderId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确认已收到商品？', '提示', {
      confirmButtonText: '确认收货',
      cancelButtonText: '取消',
      type: 'info'
    })
    await orderApi.confirmReceive(orderId)
    ElMessage.success('确认收货成功')
    fetchOrders()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      console.error('❌ 确认收货失败:', error)
      ElMessage.error(error.message || '确认收货失败')
    }
  }
}

// ✅ 监听路由参数变化（从其他页面跳转时携带 status 参数）
watch(
  () => route.query.status,
  (newStatus) => {
    if (newStatus !== undefined && newStatus !== null) {
      console.log(`📦 路由参数变化: status=${newStatus}`)
      activeStatus.value = newStatus
      fetchOrders()
    }
  },
  { immediate: false }
)

// ✅ 监听 activeStatus 变化，更新 URL（可选：实现浏览器前进/后退）
watch(
  activeStatus,
  (newStatus) => {
    // 不触发路由跳转，只更新查询参数（避免循环）
    const query = { ...route.query }
    if (newStatus === 'all') {
      delete query.status
    } else {
      query.status = newStatus
    }
    // 只在路径变化时更新
    if (route.query.status !== query.status) {
      router.replace({ query })
    }
  }
)

onMounted(() => {
  console.log('📦 订单页面初始化, status:', activeStatus.value)
  fetchOrders()
})
</script>

<style scoped>

  max-width: 1200px;

  box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.order-header {

  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;

}

.order-status {

}

.order-status.completed { color: #909399; }

.order-body {
  padding: 16px 20px;
}

.order-items {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
}

.order-product {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;

}

.order-product img {
  width: 50px;
  height: 70px;
  object-fit: cover;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: #f5f7fa;
}

.product-info {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  flex: 1;
}

.product-name {
  font-size: 14px;
  color: #303133;
}

.product-price {
  font-size: 13px;
  color: #909399;
}

.order-summary {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 24px;
  padding-top: 12px;
  margin-top: 12px;
  border-top: 1px solid #ebeef5;
  font-size: 14px;
  color: #606266;
}

.order-total {
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #f56c6c;
}

.order-footer {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 12px 20px;
  border-top: 1px solid #ebeef5;
}

  font-size: 12px;

  display: flex;

}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\frontend\src\views\OrderCreate.vue ====================

<template>
  <div class="order-create" v-loading="loading">
    <div class="page-header">
      <h1 class="page-title">📝 确认订单</h1>
      <el-button type="text" @click="goBack">
        <el-icon><ArrowLeft /></el-icon> 返回购物车
      </el-button>
    </div>
    
    <div v-if="cartItems.length > 0" class="order-container">
      <!-- 收货地址 -->
      <div class="section address-section">
        <div class="section-header">
          <h3>📍 收货地址</h3>
          <el-button type="primary" link @click="goToAddress">管理地址</el-button>
        </div>
        
        <div v-if="addresses.length > 0" class="address-list">
          <div 
            class="address-item" 
            v-for="addr in addresses" 
            :key="addr.addressId"
            :class="{ active: selectedAddress === addr.addressId }"
            @click="selectedAddress = addr.addressId"
          >
            <div class="address-radio">
              <el-radio :model-value="selectedAddress" :label="addr.addressId">
                {{ '' }}
              </el-radio>
            </div>
            <div class="address-info">
              <div class="address-name">
                <span class="receiver">{{ addr.receiverName }}</span>
                <span class="phone">{{ addr.receiverPhone }}</span>
                <el-tag v-if="addr.isDefault === 1" size="small" type="success" class="default-tag">默认</el-tag>
              </div>
              <div class="address-detail">{{ addr.fullAddress || addr.province + addr.city + addr.district + addr.detailAddress }}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div v-else class="empty-address">
          <el-empty description="暂无收货地址，请先添加" :image-size="80">
            <el-button type="primary" @click="goToAddress">添加地址</el-button>
          </el-empty>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 商品列表 -->
      <div class="section goods-section">
        <h3>📦 商品列表</h3>
        <div class="goods-list">
          <div class="goods-item" v-for="item in cartItems" :key="item.cartId">
            <div class="goods-image">
              <img :src="item.bookCover || '/default-cover.jpg'" :alt="item.bookName" />
            </div>
            <div class="goods-info">
              <div class="goods-name">{{ item.bookName }}</div>
              <div class="goods-meta">
                <span class="goods-price">¥{{ Number(item.price).toFixed(2) }}</span>
                <span class="goods-quantity">× {{ item.quantity }}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="goods-subtotal">
              ¥{{ (Number(item.price) * item.quantity).toFixed(2) }}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 订单备注 -->
      <div class="section remark-section">
        <h3>📝 订单备注</h3>
        <el-input 
          v-model="remark" 
          type="textarea" 
          :rows="2" 
          placeholder="选填，如有特殊要求请备注"
          maxlength="200"
          show-word-limit
        />
      </div>

      <!-- 结算信息 -->
      <div class="section summary-section">
        <div class="summary-content">
          <div class="summary-row">
            <span>商品金额</span>
            <span>¥{{ totalPrice.toFixed(2) }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="summary-row">
            <span>运费</span>
            <span>¥0.00</span>
          </div>
          <div class="summary-row total">
            <span>实付款</span>
            <span class="total-price">¥{{ totalPrice.toFixed(2) }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="summary-actions">
          <el-button size="large" @click="goBack">返回购物车</el-button>
          <el-button 
            type="primary" 
            size="large" 
            :loading="submitting"
            :disabled="!selectedAddress || addresses.length === 0"
            @click="submitOrder"
          >
            {{ submitting ? '提交中...' : '提交订单' }}
          </el-button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <el-empty v-else description="购物车是空的" />
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted, watch } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { ArrowLeft } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import { useCartStore } from '@/stores/cart'
import { useUserStore } from '@/stores/user' // ✅ 新增：引入用户状态
import { addressApi } from '@/api/address'
import { orderApi } from '@/api/order'

const router = useRouter()
const cartStore = useCartStore()
const userStore = useUserStore() // ✅ 新增：用户状态

const loading = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const addresses = ref([])
const selectedAddress = ref(null)
const remark = ref('')

// 记录当前用户ID，用于检测切换账号
const currentUserId = ref(null)

// 从购物车获取商品列表
const cartItems = computed(() => cartStore.cartItems || [])
const totalPrice = computed(() => cartStore.totalPrice || 0)

const goBack = () => {
  router.push('/cart')
}

const goToAddress = () => {
  router.push('/address')
}

// ✅ 重置页面数据（切换账号时调用）
const resetPageData = () => {
  addresses.value = []
  selectedAddress.value = null
  remark.value = ''
  // 不重置购物车，让 cartStore 自己管理
}

// ✅ 获取收货地址列表（增强版）
const fetchAddresses = async () => {
  try {
    // 获取当前用户ID
    const userId = userStore.userInfo?.userId || userStore.userId
    console.log('当前用户ID:', userId)
    
    // 如果用户ID发生变化，重置数据
    if (currentUserId.value && currentUserId.value !== userId) {
      console.log('检测到用户切换，重置数据')
      resetPageData()
    }
    currentUserId.value = userId
    
    const result = await addressApi.getAddressList()
    console.log('获取地址列表响应:', result)
    
    // 处理不同响应格式
    let addressList = []
    if (Array.isArray(result)) {
      addressList = result
    } else if (result && result.code === 200) {
      addressList = result.data || []
    } else {
      addressList = []
    }
    
    addresses.value = addressList
    
    // ✅ 默认选中默认地址或第一个地址（确保属于当前用户）
    if (addresses.value.length > 0) {
      // 优先选择默认地址
      let defaultAddr = addresses.value.find(a => a.isDefault === 1)
      // 如果没有默认地址，选择第一个
      if (!defaultAddr) {
        defaultAddr = addresses.value[0]
      }
      selectedAddress.value = defaultAddr.addressId
      console.log('选中的地址ID:', selectedAddress.value)
    } else {
      selectedAddress.value = null
      ElMessage.warning('暂无收货地址，请先添加')
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取地址列表失败:', error)
    addresses.value = []
    selectedAddress.value = null
    ElMessage.error('获取地址列表失败，请刷新重试')
  }
}

// ✅ 提交订单（增强版）
const submitOrder = async () => {
  // 检查是否选中地址
  if (!selectedAddress.value) {
    ElMessage.warning('请选择收货地址')
    return
  }
  
  // ✅ 新增：校验选中的地址是否属于当前用户（防止缓存问题）
  const isValidAddress = addresses.value.some(addr => addr.addressId === selectedAddress.value)
  if (!isValidAddress) {
    ElMessage.error('所选地址无效，请重新选择')
    // 自动切换到第一个有效地址
    if (addresses.value.length > 0) {
      selectedAddress.value = addresses.value[0].addressId
      ElMessage.info('已自动切换到有效地址')
    } else {
      ElMessage.warning('暂无有效地址，请添加收货地址')
      return
    }
    return
  }
  
  if (cartItems.value.length === 0) {
    ElMessage.warning('购物车为空')
    return
  }
  
  // 确认提交
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确认提交订单？', '提示', {
      confirmButtonText: '确认提交',
      cancelButtonText: '取消',
      type: 'info'
    })
  } catch {
    return
  }
  
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    const orderData = {
      addressId: selectedAddress.value,
      remark: remark.value
    }
    
    console.log('提交订单数据:', orderData) // ✅ 便于调试
    
    const result = await orderApi.createOrder(orderData)
    console.log('订单创建响应:', result) // ✅ 便于调试
    
    if (result && (result.code === 200 || result.code === undefined)) {
      ElMessage.success('订单创建成功')
      // 清空购物车数据
      await cartStore.fetchCart()
      // 跳转到订单详情或订单列表
      const orderId = result.data?.orderId
      if (orderId) {
        router.push(`/order/${orderId}`)
      } else {
        router.push('/orders')
      }
    } else {
      ElMessage.error(result?.message || '订单创建失败')
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('提交订单失败:', error)
    // ✅ 优化错误信息显示
    const errorMsg = error?.response?.data?.message || error?.message || '订单创建失败，请稍后重试'
    ElMessage.error(errorMsg)
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

// ✅ 监听用户切换（如果用户Store支持响应式）
watch(
  () => userStore.userInfo?.userId || userStore.userId,
  (newUserId, oldUserId) => {
    if (newUserId && oldUserId && newUserId !== oldUserId) {
      console.log(`用户从 ${oldUserId} 切换到 ${newUserId}，刷新数据`)
      resetPageData()
      // 重新获取数据
      fetchAddresses()
      cartStore.fetchCart()
    }
  },
  { immediate: false }
)

// ✅ 监听路由变化，确保每次进入页面都刷新数据
watch(
  () => router.currentRoute.value.path,
  () => {
    if (router.currentRoute.value.path === '/order/create') {
      console.log('重新进入订单确认页面，刷新数据')
      // 页面可见时刷新数据
      if (!loading.value) {
        fetchAddresses()
        cartStore.fetchCart()
      }
    }
  }
)

// ✅ 初始化（增强版）
onMounted(async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    // 先重置数据，确保干净状态
    resetPageData()
    
    // 并行获取数据
    await Promise.all([
      cartStore.fetchCart(),
      fetchAddresses()
    ])
    
    console.log('订单页面初始化完成')
    console.log('购物车商品:', cartItems.value)
    console.log('地址列表:', addresses.value)
    console.log('选中的地址:', selectedAddress.value)
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('初始化订单页面失败:', error)
    ElMessage.error('页面初始化失败，请刷新重试')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
})

// ✅ 关键修复：暴露方法和数据给外部调用（用于调试和测试）
defineExpose({
  submitOrder,
  fetchAddresses,
  resetPageData,
  selectedAddress,
  addresses,
  cartItems,
  totalPrice,
  loading,
  submitting,
  remark,
  userStore,
  currentUserId,
  cartStore,
  goBack,
  goToAddress
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="order-detail" v-loading="loading">
    <div v-if="order" class="detail-container">
      <!-- 面包屑 -->
      <div class="breadcrumb">
        <el-breadcrumb separator="/">
          <el-breadcrumb-item :to="{ path: '/' }">首页</el-breadcrumb-item>
          <el-breadcrumb-item :to="{ path: '/orders' }">我的订单</el-breadcrumb-item>
          <el-breadcrumb-item>订单详情</el-breadcrumb-item>
        </el-breadcrumb>
      </div>
      
      <!-- 订单状态 -->
      <div class="order-status-bar" :class="getStatusClass(order.orderStatus)">
        <div class="status-icon">{{ getStatusIcon(order.orderStatus) }}</div>
        <div class="status-info">
          <h2>{{ getStatusText(order.orderStatus) }}</h2>
          <p v-if="order.orderStatus === 0">请尽快完成支付，超时订单将自动取消</p>
          <p v-else-if="order.orderStatus === 1">商家正在准备发货，请耐心等待</p>
          <p v-else-if="order.orderStatus === 2">商品已发货，请注意查收</p>
          <p v-else-if="order.orderStatus === 3">订单已完成，感谢您的购买</p>
          <p v-else-if="order.orderStatus === 4">订单已取消</p>
        </div>
        <div class="status-actions" v-if="order.orderStatus === 0">
          <el-button type="primary" @click="handlePay">立即支付</el-button>
          <el-button @click="handleCancel">取消订单</el-button>
        </div>
        <div class="status-actions" v-if="order.orderStatus === 2">
          <el-button type="success" @click="handleReceive">确认收货</el-button>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <!-- 订单信息 -->
      <div class="detail-section">
        <h3>📋 订单信息</h3>
        <div class="info-grid">
          <div class="info-item">
            <span class="label">订单号</span>
            <span class="value">{{ order.orderId || '-' }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-item">
            <span class="label">下单时间</span>
            <span class="value">{{ order.createTime || '-' }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-item">
            <span class="label">支付方式</span>
            <span class="value">{{ order.payType === 0 ? '余额支付' : '其他' }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-item">
            <span class="label">支付时间</span>
            <span class="value">{{ order.payTime || '未支付' }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <!-- 收货信息 -->
      <div class="detail-section">
        <h3>📍 收货信息</h3>
        <div class="address-info">
          <p><strong>{{ order.receiverName || '-' }}</strong> {{ order.receiverPhone || '-' }}</p>
          <p>{{ order.receiverAddress || '-' }}</p>
          <p v-if="order.remark">备注：{{ order.remark }}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <!-- 商品列表 -->
      <div class="detail-section">
        <h3>📦 商品列表</h3>
        <div class="product-list">
          <div class="product-item" v-for="item in (order.items || [])" :key="item.itemId">
            <img :src="item.bookCover || '/default-cover.jpg'" :alt="item.bookName" />
            <div class="product-detail">
              <span class="product-name">{{ item.bookName || '未知商品' }}</span>
              <span class="product-price">¥{{ safeToFixed(item.price) }}</span>
            </div>
            <span class="product-quantity">×{{ item.quantity || 0 }}</span>
            <span class="product-total">¥{{ safeToFixed(item.totalPrice) }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="order-total-summary">
          <div class="summary-row">
            <span>商品金额</span>
            <span>¥{{ safeToFixed(order.totalAmount) }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="summary-row">
            <span>运费</span>
            <span>¥{{ safeToFixed(order.shippingFee || 0) }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="summary-row total">
            <span>实付款</span>
            <span>¥{{ safeToFixed(order.totalAmount) }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <!-- 评价按钮 -->
      <div class="detail-actions" v-if="order.orderStatus === 3">
        <el-button type="primary" @click="goToReview">去评价</el-button>
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <el-empty v-else-if="!loading" description="订单不存在" />
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { orderApi } from '@/api/order'

const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const loading = ref(false)
const order = ref(null)

const statusMap = {
  0: { text: '待付款', class: 'pending', icon: '⏳' },
  1: { text: '待发货', class: 'paid', icon: '📦' },
  2: { text: '待收货', class: 'shipped', icon: '🚚' },
  3: { text: '已完成', class: 'completed', icon: '✅' },
  4: { text: '已取消', class: 'cancelled', icon: '❌' }
}

/**
 * 安全格式化数字
 * @param {number|string|undefined} value - 要格式化的值
 * @param {number} digits - 小数位数
 * @returns {string} 格式化后的字符串
 */
const safeToFixed = (value, digits = 2) => {
  if (value === undefined || value === null || isNaN(value)) {
    return '0.00'
  }
  const num = Number(value)
  if (isNaN(num)) {
    return '0.00'
  }
  return num.toFixed(digits)
}

const getStatusText = (status) => statusMap[status]?.text || '未知'
const getStatusClass = (status) => statusMap[status]?.class || ''
const getStatusIcon = (status) => statusMap[status]?.icon || '📋'

const fetchOrderDetail = async () => {
  const orderId = route.params.id
  if (!orderId) {
    ElMessage.error('参数错误')
    router.push('/orders')
    return
  }
  
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const result = await orderApi.getOrderDetail(orderId)
    // 处理不同响应格式
    if (result && result.code === 200) {
      order.value = result.data || null
    } else if (result && result.orderId) {
      order.value = result
    } else {
      order.value = null
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取订单详情失败:', error)
    ElMessage.error(error.message || '获取订单详情失败')
    order.value = null
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const handlePay = async () => {
  if (!order.value) return
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要支付该订单吗？', '提示', {
      confirmButtonText: '确定支付',
      cancelButtonText: '取消',
      type: 'info'
    })
    await orderApi.payOrder(order.value.orderId)
    ElMessage.success('支付成功')
    await fetchOrderDetail()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      console.error('支付失败:', error)
      ElMessage.error(error.message || '支付失败')
    }
  }
}

const handleCancel = async () => {
  if (!order.value) return
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要取消该订单吗？', '提示', {
      confirmButtonText: '确定取消',
      cancelButtonText: '取消',
      type: 'warning'
    })
    await orderApi.cancelOrder(order.value.orderId)
    ElMessage.success('订单已取消')
    await fetchOrderDetail()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      console.error('取消失败:', error)
      ElMessage.error(error.message || '取消失败')
    }
  }
}

const handleReceive = async () => {
  if (!order.value) return
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确认已收到商品？', '提示', {
      confirmButtonText: '确认收货',
      cancelButtonText: '取消',
      type: 'info'
    })
    await orderApi.confirmReceive(order.value.orderId)
    ElMessage.success('确认收货成功')
    await fetchOrderDetail()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      console.error('确认收货失败:', error)
      ElMessage.error(error.message || '确认收货失败')
    }
  }
}

const goToReview = () => {
  if (!order.value) return
  router.push(`/review/create?orderId=${order.value.orderId}`)
}

onMounted(fetchOrderDetail)
</script>

<template>
  <div class="profile-page">
    <h1 class="page-title">👤 个人中心</h1>
    
    <div class="profile-container">
      <!-- 用户信息卡片 -->
      <div class="profile-card">
        <div class="avatar-wrapper">
          <el-avatar :size="80" :src="userInfo?.avatar || ''">
            {{ userInfo?.username?.charAt(0)?.toUpperCase() || 'U' }}
          </el-avatar>
        </div>
        <div class="user-info">
          <h2>{{ userInfo?.username || '用户' }}</h2>
          <p class="user-balance">余额：¥{{ (userInfo?.balance || 0).toFixed(2) }}</p>
          <p class="user-status" :class="userInfo?.status === 1 ? 'active' : 'inactive'">
            {{ userInfo?.status === 1 ? '✅ 正常' : '❌ 已禁用' }}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <!-- 右侧信息区域 -->
      <div class="right-panel">
        <!-- 基本信息 -->
        <div class="info-section">
          <h3>📝 基本信息</h3>
          <el-form :model="form" :rules="rules" ref="formRef" label-width="80px">
            <el-form-item label="用户名">
              <el-input v-model="form.username" disabled />
            </el-form-item>
            
            <el-form-item label="手机号" prop="phone">
              <el-input v-model="form.phone" placeholder="请输入手机号" />
            </el-form-item>
            
            <el-form-item label="邮箱" prop="email">
              <el-input v-model="form.email" placeholder="请输入邮箱" />
            </el-form-item>
            
            <el-form-item>
              <el-button type="primary" @click="saveProfile" :loading="saving">保存修改</el-button>
            </el-form-item>
          </el-form>
        </div>
        
        <!-- 修改密码 - 重新设计 -->
        <div class="info-section password-section">
          <h3>🔒 修改密码</h3>
          
          <div class="password-form">
            <!-- 当前密码 -->
            <div class="password-field">
              <label class="password-label">
                <span class="label-icon">🔐</span>
                当前密码
              </label>
              <div class="password-input-wrapper">
                <el-input 
                  v-model="pwdForm.oldPassword" 
                  type="password" 
                  placeholder="请输入当前密码" 
                  show-password 
                  size="large"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 新密码 -->
            <div class="password-field">
              <label class="password-label">
                <span class="label-icon">✨</span>
                新密码
              </label>
              <div class="password-input-wrapper">
                <el-input 
                  v-model="pwdForm.newPassword" 
                  type="password" 
                  placeholder="请输入新密码（6-20位）" 
                  show-password 
                  size="large"
                />
              </div>
              <div class="password-strength" v-if="pwdForm.newPassword">
                <span class="strength-label">密码强度：</span>
                <span class="strength-bar">
                  <span class="strength-level" :class="passwordStrength"></span>
                </span>
                <span class="strength-text">{{ strengthText }}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 确认新密码 -->
            <div class="password-field">
              <label class="password-label">
                <span class="label-icon">✅</span>
                确认新密码
              </label>
              <div class="password-input-wrapper">
                <el-input 
                  v-model="pwdForm.confirmPassword" 
                  type="password" 
                  placeholder="请再次输入新密码" 
                  show-password 
                  size="large"
                  :class="{ 'is-error': pwdForm.confirmPassword && pwdForm.confirmPassword !== pwdForm.newPassword }"
                />
              </div>
              <div class="password-match" v-if="pwdForm.confirmPassword">
                <span v-if="pwdForm.confirmPassword === pwdForm.newPassword" class="match-success">
                  ✅ 密码匹配
                </span>
                <span v-else class="match-error">
                  ❌ 密码不一致
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 提交按钮 -->
            <div class="password-submit">
              <el-button 
                type="primary" 
                @click="updatePassword" 
                :loading="pwdLoading" 
                size="large"
                :disabled="!pwdForm.oldPassword || !pwdForm.newPassword || !pwdForm.confirmPassword"
              >
                <span v-if="!pwdLoading">🔑 修改密码</span>
                <span v-else>提交中...</span>
              </el-button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { useUserStore } from '@/stores/user'
import { userApi } from '@/api/user'

const userStore = useUserStore()
const userInfo = ref(null)
const saving = ref(false)
const pwdLoading = ref(false)
const formRef = ref()

const form = reactive({
  username: '',
  phone: '',
  email: ''
})

const pwdForm = reactive({
  oldPassword: '',
  newPassword: '',
  confirmPassword: ''
})

// 密码强度计算
const passwordStrength = computed(() => {
  const pwd = pwdForm.newPassword
  if (!pwd || pwd.length < 6) return 'weak'
  let score = 0
  if (pwd.length >= 8) score++
  if (/[a-z]/.test(pwd) && /[A-Z]/.test(pwd)) score++
  if (/\d/.test(pwd)) score++
  if (/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/.test(pwd)) score++
  if (pwd.length >= 12) score++
  if (score >= 4) return 'strong'
  if (score >= 2) return 'medium'
  return 'weak'
})

const strengthText = computed(() => {
  const map = { weak: '弱', medium: '中等', strong: '强' }
  return map[passwordStrength.value] || ''
})

const rules = {
  phone: [
    { pattern: /^1[3-9]\d{9}$/, message: '手机号格式不正确', trigger: 'blur' }
  ],
  email: [
    { type: 'email', message: '邮箱格式不正确', trigger: 'blur' }
  ]
}

const loadUserInfo = async () => {
  try {
    const info = await userStore.fetchUserInfo()
    userInfo.value = info
    form.username = info.username
    form.phone = info.phone || ''
    form.email = info.email || ''
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取用户信息失败:', error)
  }
}

const saveProfile = async () => {
  if (!formRef.value) return
  
  await formRef.value.validate(async (valid) => {
    if (!valid) return
    
    saving.value = true
    try {
      await userApi.updateUserInfo({
        phone: form.phone,
        email: form.email
      })
      await userStore.fetchUserInfo()
      ElMessage.success('保存成功')
    } catch (error) {
      ElMessage.error('保存失败')
    } finally {
      saving.value = false
    }
  })
}

const updatePassword = async () => {
  // 前端验证
  if (!pwdForm.oldPassword) {
    ElMessage.warning('请输入当前密码')
    return
  }
  if (pwdForm.newPassword.length < 6 || pwdForm.newPassword.length > 20) {
    ElMessage.warning('新密码长度需为6-20位')
    return
  }
  if (pwdForm.newPassword !== pwdForm.confirmPassword) {
    ElMessage.warning('两次输入的密码不一致')
    return
  }
  
  pwdLoading.value = true
  try {
    await userApi.updatePassword({
      oldPassword: pwdForm.oldPassword,
      newPassword: pwdForm.newPassword
    })
    ElMessage.success('密码修改成功，请重新登录')
    // 清空密码表单
    pwdForm.oldPassword = ''
    pwdForm.newPassword = ''
    pwdForm.confirmPassword = ''
    await userStore.logout()
    setTimeout(() => {
      window.location.href = '/login'
    }, 1500)
  } catch (error) {
    ElMessage.error(error?.response?.data?.message || '密码修改失败')
  } finally {
    pwdLoading.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(loadUserInfo)
</script>

<template>
  <div class="register-container">
    <div class="register-card">
      <div class="register-header">
        <h1>📚 教材商城</h1>
        <p>注册新账号，开始您的购书之旅</p>
      </div>
      
      <el-form
        ref="formRef"
        :model="form"
        :rules="rules"
        label-width="80px"
        size="large"
      >
        <el-form-item label="用户名" prop="username">
          <el-input v-model="form.username" placeholder="3-20位字母、数字或下划线" clearable />
        </el-form-item>

        <el-form-item label="密码" prop="password">
          <el-input v-model="form.password" type="password" placeholder="6-20位" show-password clearable />
        </el-form-item>

        <el-form-item label="确认密码" prop="confirmPassword">
          <el-input v-model="form.confirmPassword" type="password" placeholder="再次输入密码" show-password clearable />
        </el-form-item>

        <el-form-item label="手机号" prop="phone">
          <el-input v-model="form.phone" placeholder="请输入手机号" clearable />
        </el-form-item>

        <el-form-item label="邮箱" prop="email">
          <el-input v-model="form.email" placeholder="请输入邮箱" clearable />
        </el-form-item>

        <el-form-item label="验证码" prop="captcha">
          <div class="captcha-wrapper">
            <el-input v-model="form.captcha" placeholder="请输入验证码" clearable />
            <el-button @click="getCaptcha" :disabled="captchaDisabled" class="captcha-btn">
              {{ captchaText }}
            </el-button>
          </div>
        </el-form-item>

        <el-form-item>
          <el-button type="primary" size="large" :loading="loading" @click="handleRegister" style="width: 100%">
            {{ loading ? '注册中...' : '注 册' }}
          </el-button>
        </el-form-item>

        <div class="register-footer">
          <span>已有账号？</span>
          <el-link type="primary" @click="goToLogin">立即登录</el-link>
        </div>
      </el-form>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { userApi } from '@/api/user'

const router = useRouter()
const loading = ref(false)
const formRef = ref()
const captchaDisabled = ref(false)
const captchaText = ref('获取验证码')

const form = reactive({
  username: '',
  password: '',
  confirmPassword: '',
  phone: '',
  email: '',
  captcha: ''
})

// 确认密码验证
const validateConfirm = (rule, value, callback) => {
  if (value !== form.password) {
    callback(new Error('两次输入的密码不一致'))
  } else {
    callback()
  }
}

const rules = {
  username: [
    { required: true, message: '请输入用户名', trigger: 'blur' },
    { min: 3, max: 20, message: '用户名长度为3-20位', trigger: 'blur' },
    { pattern: /^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$/, message: '用户名只能包含字母、数字、下划线', trigger: 'blur' }
  ],
  password: [
    { required: true, message: '请输入密码', trigger: 'blur' },
    { min: 6, max: 20, message: '密码长度为6-20位', trigger: 'blur' }
  ],
  confirmPassword: [
    { required: true, message: '请确认密码', trigger: 'blur' },
    { validator: validateConfirm, trigger: 'blur' }
  ],
  phone: [
    { pattern: /^1[3-9]\d{9}$/, message: '手机号格式不正确', trigger: 'blur' }
  ],
  email: [
    { type: 'email', message: '邮箱格式不正确', trigger: 'blur' }
  ],
  captcha: [
    { required: true, message: '请输入验证码', trigger: 'blur' },
    { len: 4, message: '验证码为4位数字', trigger: 'blur' },
    { pattern: /^\d{4}$/, message: '验证码为4位数字', trigger: 'blur' }
  ]
}

const getCaptcha = () => {
  captchaDisabled.value = true
  captchaText.value = '60s'
  let count = 60
  const timer = setInterval(() => {
    count--
    captchaText.value = `${count}s`
    if (count <= 0) {
      clearInterval(timer)
      captchaDisabled.value = false
      captchaText.value = '获取验证码'
    }
  }, 1000)
  ElMessage.success('验证码已发送到您的手机')
}

const handleRegister = async () => {
  if (!formRef.value) return
  
  await formRef.value.validate(async (valid) => {
    if (!valid) return
    
    loading.value = true
    try {
      await userApi.register(form)
      ElMessage.success('注册成功，请登录')
      router.push('/login')
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  })
}

const goToLogin = () => router.push('/login')
</script>

  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  align-items: center;
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  padding: 20px 0;

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\frontend\src\views\ReviewCreate.vue ====================

<template>
  <div class="review-create" v-loading="loading">
    <div class="page-header">
      <h1 class="page-title">✍️ 发表评价</h1>
      <el-button type="text" @click="goBack">
        <el-icon><ArrowLeft /></el-icon> 返回订单详情
      </el-button>
    </div>

    <div v-if="orderInfo" class="review-container">
      <!-- 订单信息 -->
      <div class="section order-section">
        <h3>📦 订单信息</h3>
        <div class="order-info">
          <span>订单号：{{ orderInfo.orderId }}</span>
          <span>下单时间：{{ orderInfo.createTime }}</span>
          <span>实付金额：¥{{ (orderInfo.totalAmount || 0).toFixed(2) }}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 商品列表 -->
      <div class="section book-section">
        <h3>📖 选择评价商品</h3>
        <div class="book-list">
          <div 
            class="book-item" 
            v-for="item in orderItems" 
            :key="item.bookId"
            :class="{ active: selectedBookId === item.bookId }"
            @click="selectBook(item)"
          >
            <img :src="item.bookCover || '/default-cover.jpg'" :alt="item.bookName" />
            <div class="book-info">
              <div class="book-name">{{ item.bookName }}</div>
              <div class="book-meta">
                <span>¥{{ (item.price || 0).toFixed(2) }} × {{ item.quantity }}</span>
                <el-tag 
                  v-if="hasReviewed(item.bookId)" 
                  size="small" 
                  type="success"
                >
                  已评价
                </el-tag>
              </div>
            </div>
            <el-radio 
              :model-value="selectedBookId" 
              :label="item.bookId"
              :disabled="hasReviewed(item.bookId)"
            >
              {{ '' }}
            </el-radio>
          </div>
        </div>
        <el-empty v-if="orderItems.length === 0" description="该订单暂无商品" />
      </div>

      <!-- 评价表单 -->
      <div class="section review-section" v-if="selectedBookId">
        <h3>⭐ 评价内容</h3>
        
        <el-form
          ref="formRef"
          :model="form"
          :rules="rules"
          label-width="80px"
        >
          <el-form-item label="评分" prop="rating">
            <el-rate 
              v-model="form.rating" 
              :texts="ratingTexts"
              show-text
              :colors="ratingColors"
              size="large"
            />
          </el-form-item>

          <el-form-item label="评价内容" prop="content">
            <el-input
              v-model="form.content"
              type="textarea"
              :rows="4"
              placeholder="请分享您的使用感受，帮助其他同学选择教材..."
              maxlength="500"
              show-word-limit
            />
          </el-form-item>

          <el-form-item>
            <el-button type="primary" size="large" :loading="submitting" @click="submitReview">
              提交评价
            </el-button>
            <el-button size="large" @click="resetForm">重置</el-button>
          </el-form-item>
        </el-form>
      </div>

      <!-- 未选择商品 -->
      <el-empty v-else description="请选择要评价的商品" :image-size="80" />
    </div>

    <el-empty v-else-if="!loading" description="订单不存在" />
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { ArrowLeft } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import { orderApi } from '@/api/order'
import { reviewApi } from '@/api/review'

const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()

// ============ 状态 ============
const loading = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const orderInfo = ref(null)
const orderItems = ref([])
const reviewedBooks = ref([])  // 已评价的书籍ID列表
const selectedBookId = ref(null)
const selectedBook = ref(null)
const formRef = ref()

// ============ 表单 ============
const form = reactive({
  rating: 5,
  content: ''
})

const rules = {
  rating: [
    { required: true, message: '请选择评分', trigger: 'change' }
  ],
  content: [
    { required: true, message: '请输入评价内容', trigger: 'blur' },
    { min: 5, message: '评价内容至少5个字', trigger: 'blur' },
    { max: 500, message: '评价内容不能超过500个字', trigger: 'blur' }
  ]
}

const ratingTexts = ['很差', '较差', '一般', '满意', '非常满意']
const ratingColors = ['#F56C6C', '#E6A23C', '#909399', '#67C23A', '#409EFF']

// ============ 方法 ============
const goBack = () => {
  const orderId = route.query.orderId
  if (orderId) {
    router.push(`/order/${orderId}`)
  } else {
    router.push('/orders')
  }
}

/**
 * 获取订单详情
 */
const fetchOrderDetail = async () => {
  const orderId = route.query.orderId
  if (!orderId) {
    ElMessage.error('参数错误，请从订单页面进入')
    router.push('/orders')
    return
  }

  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await orderApi.getOrderDetail(orderId)
    
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      orderInfo.value = res.data
      orderItems.value = res.data?.items || []
      
      // 检查哪些商品已评价
      await checkReviewedBooks()
      
      // 默认选中第一个未评价的商品
      const firstUnreviewed = orderItems.value.find(
        item => !hasReviewed(item.bookId)
      )
      if (firstUnreviewed) {
        selectedBookId.value = firstUnreviewed.bookId
        selectedBook.value = firstUnreviewed
      }
    } else {
      ElMessage.error('获取订单详情失败')
      router.push('/orders')
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取订单详情失败:', error)
    ElMessage.error(error.message || '获取订单详情失败')
    router.push('/orders')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

/**
 * 检查哪些商品已评价
 */
const checkReviewedBooks = async () => {
  // 实际项目中应该调用 API 查询用户已评价的商品列表
  // 这里模拟：从本地缓存或 API 获取
  try {
    // 假设有一个 API 可以查询用户已评价的书籍
    // const res = await reviewApi.getUserReviewedBooks()
    // reviewedBooks.value = res.data || []
    
    // 暂时模拟：如果订单中有商品，假设第一个已评价（演示用）
    // 实际使用时请替换为真实 API 调用
    reviewedBooks.value = []
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取已评价列表失败:', error)
    reviewedBooks.value = []
  }
}

/**
 * 判断某本书是否已评价
 */
const hasReviewed = (bookId) => {
  return reviewedBooks.value.includes(bookId)
}

/**
 * 选择商品
 */
const selectBook = (item) => {
  if (hasReviewed(item.bookId)) {
    ElMessage.warning('该商品已评价，不可重复评价')
    return
  }
  selectedBookId.value = item.bookId
  selectedBook.value = item
  // 重置表单
  resetForm()
}

/**
 * 重置表单
 */
const resetForm = () => {
  form.rating = 5
  form.content = ''
  if (formRef.value) {
    formRef.value.resetFields()
  }
}

/**
 * 提交评价
 */
const submitReview = async () => {
  if (!formRef.value) return
  
  // 检查是否选择了商品
  if (!selectedBookId.value) {
    ElMessage.warning('请选择要评价的商品')
    return
  }

  // 检查是否已评价
  if (hasReviewed(selectedBookId.value)) {
    ElMessage.warning('该商品已评价，不可重复评价')
    return
  }

  await formRef.value.validate(async (valid) => {
    if (!valid) return

    // 二次确认
    try {
      await ElMessageBox.confirm(
        `确定要对「${selectedBook.value?.bookName}」发表评价吗？`,
        '确认提交',
        {
          confirmButtonText: '确定提交',
          cancelButtonText: '取消',
          type: 'info'
        }
      )
    } catch {
      return
    }

    submitting.value = true
    try {
      const data = {
        bookId: selectedBookId.value,
        orderId: orderInfo.value.orderId,
        rating: form.rating,
        content: form.content.trim()
      }

      const res = await reviewApi.createReview(data)
      
      if (res && res.code === 200) {
        ElMessage.success('评价提交成功！')
        
        // 标记已评价
        reviewedBooks.value.push(selectedBookId.value)
        
        // 自动选择下一个未评价的商品
        const nextUnreviewed = orderItems.value.find(
          item => !hasReviewed(item.bookId)
        )
        if (nextUnreviewed) {
          selectedBookId.value = nextUnreviewed.bookId
          selectedBook.value = nextUnreviewed
          resetForm()
          ElMessage.info('继续评价下一个商品')
        } else {
          // 所有商品都已评价
          ElMessage.success('该订单所有商品已评价完毕！')
          // 延迟跳转
          setTimeout(() => {
            router.push('/orders')
          }, 1500)
        }
      } else {
        ElMessage.error(res?.message || '评价提交失败')
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('提交评价失败:', error)
      ElMessage.error(error.message || '评价提交失败')
    } finally {
      submitting.value = false
    }
  })
}

// ============ 生命周期 ============
onMounted(() => {
  fetchOrderDetail()
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="login-container">
    <div class="login-card">
      <!-- 标题 -->
      <div class="login-header">
        <h1>📚 教材商城</h1>
        <p>欢迎回来，请登录您的账号</p>
      </div>
      
      <!-- 表单 -->
      <el-form
        ref="formRef"
        :model="form"
        :rules="rules"
        @submit.prevent="handleLogin"
        size="large"
      >
        <el-form-item prop="username">
          <el-input
            v-model="form.username"
            placeholder="请输入用户名"
            prefix-icon="User"
            clearable
          />
        </el-form-item>

        <el-form-item prop="password">
          <el-input
            v-model="form.password"
            type="password"
            placeholder="请输入密码"
            prefix-icon="Lock"
            show-password
            clearable
            @keyup.enter="handleLogin"
          />
        </el-form-item>

        <el-form-item>
          <el-button
            type="primary"
            size="large"
            :loading="loading"
            @click="handleLogin"
            style="width: 100%"
          >
            {{ loading ? '登录中...' : '登 录' }}
          </el-button>
        </el-form-item>

        <div class="login-footer">
          <span>还没有账号？</span>
          <el-link type="primary" @click="goToRegister">立即注册</el-link>
          <span style="margin: 0 8px;">|</span>
          <el-link type="warning" @click="goToAdminLogin">管理员登录</el-link>
        </div>
      </el-form>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive } from 'vue'
import { useRouter, useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { useUserStore } from '@/stores/user'

const router = useRouter()
const route = useRoute()
const userStore = useUserStore()

const loading = ref(false)
const formRef = ref()

const form = reactive({
  username: '',
  password: ''
})

const rules = {
  username: [
    { required: true, message: '请输入用户名', trigger: 'blur' },
    { min: 3, max: 20, message: '用户名长度为3-20位', trigger: 'blur' }
  ],
  password: [
    { required: true, message: '请输入密码', trigger: 'blur' },
    { min: 6, max: 20, message: '密码长度为6-20位', trigger: 'blur' }
  ]
}

const handleLogin = async () => {
  if (!formRef.value) return
  
  await formRef.value.validate(async (valid) => {
    if (!valid) return
    
    loading.value = true
    try {
      await userStore.login(form)
      ElMessage.success('登录成功')
      
      // 跳转到之前访问的页面或首页
      const redirect = route.query.redirect || '/'
      router.push(redirect)
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('登录错误:', error)
      ElMessage.error(error.message || '登录失败，请检查用户名和密码')
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  })
}

const goToRegister = () => router.push('/register')
const goToAdminLogin = () => router.push('/admin/login')
</script>

<template>
  <div class="admin-books">
    <!-- 统计卡片 -->
    <el-row :gutter="20" class="stat-row">
      <el-col :span="6" v-for="stat in bookStats" :key="stat.label">
        <el-statistic :value="stat.value" :title="stat.label">
          <template #suffix>
            <span style="font-size:14px;color:#909399">{{ stat.suffix }}</span>
          </template>
        </el-statistic>
      </el-col>
    </el-row>

    <!-- 搜索区域 -->
    <el-card shadow="hover" class="search-card">
      <el-form :inline="true" :model="searchForm" class="search-form">
        <el-form-item label="关键词">
          <el-input v-model="searchForm.keyword" placeholder="书名/作者/出版社" clearable />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="分类">
          <el-select v-model="searchForm.categoryId" placeholder="全部分类" clearable>
            <el-option 
              v-for="cat in categories" 
              :key="cat.categoryId" 
              :label="cat.categoryName" 
              :value="cat.categoryId" 
            />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="状态">
          <el-select v-model="searchForm.status" placeholder="全部状态" clearable>
            <el-option label="上架" :value="1" />
            <el-option label="下架" :value="0" />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="价格区间">
          <el-input-number v-model="searchForm.minPrice" :min="0" placeholder="最低价" style="width:120px" />
          <span style="margin:0 8px">-</span>
          <el-input-number v-model="searchForm.maxPrice" :min="0" placeholder="最高价" style="width:120px" />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item>
          <el-button type="primary" @click="fetchBooks">搜索</el-button>
          <el-button @click="resetSearch">重置</el-button>
          <el-button type="success" @click="showCreateDialog">+ 新增教材</el-button>
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
    </el-card>

    <!-- 表格 -->
    <el-card shadow="hover" class="table-card">
      <div class="table-toolbar">
        <div class="toolbar-left">
          <el-button type="danger" plain :disabled="!selectedIds.length" @click="batchDelete">
            批量删除
          </el-button>
          <el-button type="success" plain :disabled="!selectedIds.length" @click="batchOnSale">
            批量上架
          </el-button>
          <el-button type="warning" plain :disabled="!selectedIds.length" @click="batchOffSale">
            批量下架
          </el-button>
        </div>
        <div class="toolbar-right">
          <span class="total-info">共 {{ total }} 本教材</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <el-table
        :data="bookList"
        style="width: 100%"
        v-loading="loading"
        @selection-change="handleSelectionChange"
      >
        <el-table-column type="selection" width="40" />
        <el-table-column label="封面" width="70">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-avatar :size="50" :src="row.coverImage || '/default-cover.jpg'" shape="square" />
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column prop="bookName" label="书名" min-width="160" />
        <el-table-column prop="author" label="作者" width="120" />
        <el-table-column prop="categoryName" label="分类" width="100" />
        <el-table-column prop="price" label="售价" width="100">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            ¥{{ row.price?.toFixed(2) }}
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column prop="stock" label="库存" width="80" />
        <el-table-column prop="sales" label="销量" width="80" />
        <el-table-column prop="status" label="状态" width="80">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-tag :type="row.status === 1 ? 'success' : 'danger'" size="small">
              {{ row.status === 1 ? '上架' : '下架' }}
            </el-tag>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column label="操作" width="280" fixed="right">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-button type="primary" link size="small" @click="viewBook(row.bookId)">
              查看
            </el-button>
            <el-button type="warning" link size="small" @click="editBook(row)">
              编辑
            </el-button>
            <el-button 
              v-if="row.status === 0" 
              type="success" 
              link 
              size="small" 
              @click="handleOnSale(row.bookId)"
            >
              上架
            </el-button>
            <el-button 
              v-else 
              type="danger" 
              link 
              size="small" 
              @click="handleOffSale(row.bookId)"
            >
              下架
            </el-button>
            <el-button type="danger" link size="small" @click="handleDelete(row.bookId)">
              删除
            </el-button>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
      </el-table>

      <!-- ✅ 分页：修复"前往第几页"跳转 -->
      <div class="pagination-wrapper">
        <el-pagination
          v-model:current-page="pageNum"
          v-model:page-size="pageSize"
          :total="total"
          :page-sizes="[10, 20, 50, 100]"
          layout="total, sizes, prev, pager, next, jumper"
          @size-change="handleSizeChange"
          @current-change="handleCurrentChange"
        />
      </div>
    </el-card>

    <!-- 创建/编辑对话框 -->
    <el-dialog
      v-model="dialogVisible"
      :title="dialogTitle"
      width="600px"
      @close="resetForm"
    >
      <el-form
        ref="formRef"
        :model="form"
        :rules="rules"
        label-width="90px"
        label-position="right"
      >
        <el-form-item label="教材名称" prop="bookName">
          <el-input v-model="form.bookName" placeholder="请输入教材名称" />
        </el-form-item>
        
        <el-row :gutter="16">
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-form-item label="作者" prop="author">
              <el-input v-model="form.author" placeholder="请输入作者" />
            </el-form-item>
          </el-col>
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-form-item label="出版社" prop="publisher">
              <el-input v-model="form.publisher" placeholder="请输入出版社" />
            </el-form-item>
          </el-col>
        </el-row>

        <el-row :gutter="16">
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-form-item label="ISBN" prop="isbn">
              <el-input v-model="form.isbn" placeholder="请输入ISBN" />
            </el-form-item>
          </el-col>
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-form-item label="分类" prop="categoryId">
              <el-select v-model="form.categoryId" placeholder="请选择分类" style="width:100%">
                <el-option 
                  v-for="cat in categories" 
                  :key="cat.categoryId" 
                  :label="cat.categoryName" 
                  :value="cat.categoryId" 
                />
              </el-select>
            </el-form-item>
          </el-col>
        </el-row>

        <el-row :gutter="16">
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-form-item label="售价" prop="price">
              <el-input-number v-model="form.price" :min="0.01" :precision="2" style="width:100%" />
            </el-form-item>
          </el-col>
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-form-item label="原价" prop="originalPrice">
              <el-input-number v-model="form.originalPrice" :min="0" :precision="2" style="width:100%" />
            </el-form-item>
          </el-col>
        </el-row>

        <el-row :gutter="16">
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-form-item label="库存" prop="stock">
              <el-input-number v-model="form.stock" :min="0" style="width:100%" />
            </el-form-item>
          </el-col>
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-form-item label="状态" prop="status">
              <el-select v-model="form.status" placeholder="请选择状态" style="width:100%">
                <el-option label="上架" :value="1" />
                <el-option label="下架" :value="0" />
              </el-select>
            </el-form-item>
          </el-col>
        </el-row>

        <el-form-item label="封面URL" prop="coverImage">
          <el-input v-model="form.coverImage" placeholder="请输入封面图片URL" />
        </el-form-item>

        <el-form-item label="简介" prop="description">
          <el-input v-model="form.description" type="textarea" :rows="3" placeholder="请输入教材简介" />
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>

      <template #footer>
        <el-button @click="dialogVisible = false">取消</el-button>
        <el-button type="primary" @click="submitForm" :loading="submitting">
          {{ isEdit ? '保存修改' : '确认添加' }}
        </el-button>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { adminApi } from '@/api/admin'

const router = useRouter()
const loading = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const bookList = ref([])
const categories = ref([])
const total = ref(0)
const pageNum = ref(1)
const pageSize = ref(10)
const selectedIds = ref([])
const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const isEdit = ref(false)
const formRef = ref()

const searchForm = reactive({
  keyword: '',
  categoryId: null,
  status: null,
  minPrice: null,
  maxPrice: null
})

const form = reactive({
  bookId: null,
  bookName: '',
  author: '',
  publisher: '',
  isbn: '',
  categoryId: null,
  price: null,
  originalPrice: null,
  stock: 0,
  coverImage: '',
  description: '',
  status: 1
})

const dialogTitle = computed(() => isEdit.value ? '编辑教材' : '新增教材')

const rules = {
  bookName: [{ required: true, message: '请输入教材名称', trigger: 'blur' }],
  categoryId: [{ required: true, message: '请选择分类', trigger: 'change' }],
  price: [{ required: true, message: '请输入售价', trigger: 'blur' }],
  status: [{ required: true, message: '请选择状态', trigger: 'change' }]
}

// 统计信息
const bookStats = ref([
  { label: '教材总数', value: 0, suffix: '本' },
  { label: '上架教材', value: 0, suffix: '本' },
  { label: '总库存', value: 0, suffix: '件' },
  { label: '总销量', value: 0, suffix: '件' }
])

// 获取分类列表
const fetchCategories = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await adminApi.getCategories()
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      categories.value = res.data || []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取分类失败:', error)
  }
}

// 获取教材列表
const fetchBooks = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const params = {
      pageNum: pageNum.value,
      pageSize: pageSize.value,
      ...searchForm
    }
    // 移除空值
    Object.keys(params).forEach(key => {
      if (params[key] === null || params[key] === '') {
        delete params[key]
      }
    })
    
    const res = await adminApi.getBooks(params)
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      const pageData = res.data
      bookList.value = pageData?.list || []
      total.value = pageData?.total || 0
      updateStats()
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取教材列表失败:', error)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

// 更新统计
const updateStats = () => {
  const list = bookList.value
  bookStats.value = [
    { label: '教材总数', value: total.value, suffix: '本' },
    { label: '上架教材', value: list.filter(b => b.status === 1).length, suffix: '本' },
    { label: '总库存', value: list.reduce((sum, b) => sum + (b.stock || 0), 0), suffix: '件' },
    { label: '总销量', value: list.reduce((sum, b) => sum + (b.sales || 0), 0), suffix: '件' }
  ]
}

const resetSearch = () => {
  Object.assign(searchForm, {
    keyword: '',
    categoryId: null,
    status: null,
    minPrice: null,
    maxPrice: null
  })
  pageNum.value = 1
  fetchBooks()
}

const handleSelectionChange = (rows) => {
  selectedIds.value = rows.map(r => r.bookId)
}

// ✅ 分页事件处理
const handleSizeChange = (val) => {
  pageSize.value = val
  pageNum.value = 1
  fetchBooks()
}

const handleCurrentChange = (val) => {
  pageNum.value = val
  fetchBooks()
}

// ========== 上架/下架 ==========
const handleOnSale = async (bookId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要上架该教材吗？', '提示', { type: 'info' })
    await adminApi.onSale(bookId)
    ElMessage.success('上架成功')
    fetchBooks()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('上架失败')
    }
  }
}

const handleOffSale = async (bookId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要下架该教材吗？', '提示', { type: 'warning' })
    await adminApi.offSale(bookId)
    ElMessage.success('下架成功')
    fetchBooks()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('下架失败')
    }
  }
}

// ========== 批量操作 ==========
const batchOnSale = async () => {
  if (!selectedIds.value.length) return
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(`确定要批量上架 ${selectedIds.value.length} 本教材吗？`, '提示', { type: 'info' })
    await adminApi.batchOnSale(selectedIds.value)
    ElMessage.success('批量上架成功')
    selectedIds.value = []
    fetchBooks()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('批量上架失败')
    }
  }
}

const batchOffSale = async () => {
  if (!selectedIds.value.length) return
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(`确定要批量下架 ${selectedIds.value.length} 本教材吗？`, '提示', { type: 'warning' })
    await adminApi.batchOffSale(selectedIds.value)
    ElMessage.success('批量下架成功')
    selectedIds.value = []
    fetchBooks()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('批量下架失败')
    }
  }
}

const batchDelete = async () => {
  if (!selectedIds.value.length) return
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(`确定要删除 ${selectedIds.value.length} 本教材吗？此操作不可恢复！`, '危险操作', { type: 'error' })
    await adminApi.batchDeleteBooks(selectedIds.value)
    ElMessage.success('批量删除成功')
    selectedIds.value = []
    fetchBooks()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('批量删除失败')
    }
  }
}

// ========== 删除 ==========
const handleDelete = async (bookId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要删除该教材吗？此操作不可恢复！', '危险操作', { type: 'error' })
    await adminApi.deleteBook(bookId)
    ElMessage.success('删除成功')
    fetchBooks()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('删除失败')
    }
  }
}

// ========== 查看 ==========
// ✅ 修改：新标签页打开前台详情，不跳出管理后台
const viewBook = (bookId) => {
  window.open(`/book/${bookId}`, '_blank')
}

// ========== 创建/编辑 ==========
const showCreateDialog = () => {
  isEdit.value = false
  form.bookId = null
  resetForm()
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const editBook = (row) => {
  isEdit.value = true
  Object.assign(form, {
    bookId: row.bookId,
    bookName: row.bookName,
    author: row.author || '',
    publisher: row.publisher || '',
    isbn: row.isbn || '',
    categoryId: row.categoryId,
    price: row.price,
    originalPrice: row.originalPrice || null,
    stock: row.stock || 0,
    coverImage: row.coverImage || '',
    description: row.description || '',
    status: row.status
  })
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const resetForm = () => {
  form.bookId = null
  form.bookName = ''
  form.author = ''
  form.publisher = ''
  form.isbn = ''
  form.categoryId = null
  form.price = null
  form.originalPrice = null
  form.stock = 0
  form.coverImage = ''
  form.description = ''
  form.status = 1
  if (formRef.value) {
    formRef.value.resetFields()
  }
}

const submitForm = async () => {
  if (!formRef.value) return
  
  await formRef.value.validate(async (valid) => {
    if (!valid) return
    
    submitting.value = true
    try {
      const data = { ...form }
      delete data.bookId
      
      if (isEdit.value) {
        await adminApi.updateBook(form.bookId, data)
        ElMessage.success('更新成功')
      } else {
        await adminApi.createBook(data)
        ElMessage.success('添加成功')
      }
      dialogVisible.value = false
      fetchBooks()
    } catch (error) {
      ElMessage.error(isEdit.value ? '更新失败' : '添加失败')
    } finally {
      submitting.value = false
    }
  })
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await fetchCategories()
  fetchBooks()
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.admin-books {
  max-width: 1400px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}

.stat-row {
  margin-bottom: 20px;
}

.stat-row .el-col {

}

}

  justify-content: flex-end;

  .search-form .el-form-item {
    width: 100%;

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\frontend\src\views\admin\AdminCategories.vue ====================

<template>
  <div class="admin-categories">
    <el-card shadow="hover">
      <template #header>
        <div class="card-header">
          <span>📂 分类管理</span>
          <div>
            <el-button type="primary" @click="showCreateDialog">+ 新增分类</el-button>
            <el-button 
              type="danger" 
              plain 
              :disabled="!selectedIds.length" 
              @click="batchDelete"
            >
              批量删除
            </el-button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </template>

      <el-table
        :data="categoryList"
        v-loading="loading"
        @selection-change="handleSelectionChange"
      >
        <el-table-column type="selection" width="40" />
        <el-table-column prop="categoryId" label="ID" width="80" />
        <el-table-column prop="categoryName" label="分类名称" />
        <el-table-column prop="sortOrder" label="排序" width="100" />
        <el-table-column prop="bookCount" label="教材数量" width="100" />
        <el-table-column label="操作" width="250">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-button type="primary" link size="small" @click="editCategory(row)">
              编辑
            </el-button>
            <el-button type="danger" link size="small" @click="handleDelete(row.categoryId)">
              删除
            </el-button>
            <el-button 
              type="warning" 
              link 
              size="small" 
              @click="showSortDialog(row)"
            >
              调整排序
            </el-button>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
      </el-table>
    </el-card>

    <!-- 创建/编辑对话框 -->
    <el-dialog
      v-model="dialogVisible"
      :title="dialogTitle"
      width="420px"
      @close="resetForm"
    >
      <el-form ref="formRef" :model="form" :rules="rules" label-width="80px">
        <el-form-item label="分类名称" prop="categoryName">
          <el-input v-model="form.categoryName" placeholder="请输入分类名称" />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="排序" prop="sortOrder">
          <el-input-number v-model="form.sortOrder" :min="0" style="width:100%" />
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
      <template #footer>
        <el-button @click="dialogVisible = false">取消</el-button>
        <el-button type="primary" @click="submitForm" :loading="submitting">
          {{ isEdit ? '保存修改' : '确认添加' }}
        </el-button>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>

    <!-- 排序对话框 -->
    <el-dialog v-model="sortDialogVisible" title="调整排序" width="400px">
      <el-form label-width="80px">
        <el-form-item label="分类名称">
          <span>{{ sortCategory?.categoryName }}</span>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="当前排序">
          <span>{{ sortCategory?.sortOrder }}</span>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="新排序">
          <el-input-number v-model="newSortOrder" :min="0" style="width:100%" />
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
      <template #footer>
        <el-button @click="sortDialogVisible = false">取消</el-button>
        <el-button type="primary" @click="submitSort" :loading="sortLoading">
          确认修改
        </el-button>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { adminApi } from '@/api/admin'

const loading = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const sortLoading = ref(false)
const categoryList = ref([])
const selectedIds = ref([])
const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const sortDialogVisible = ref(false)
const isEdit = ref(false)
const formRef = ref()
const sortCategory = ref(null)
const newSortOrder = ref(0)

const form = reactive({
  categoryId: null,
  categoryName: '',
  sortOrder: 0
})

const dialogTitle = computed(() => isEdit.value ? '编辑分类' : '新增分类')

const rules = {
  categoryName: [{ required: true, message: '请输入分类名称', trigger: 'blur' }],
  sortOrder: [{ required: true, message: '请输入排序', trigger: 'blur' }]
}

const fetchCategories = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await adminApi.getCategories()
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      categoryList.value = res.data || []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取分类列表失败:', error)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const handleSelectionChange = (rows) => {
  selectedIds.value = rows.map(r => r.categoryId)
}

const showCreateDialog = () => {
  isEdit.value = false
  form.categoryId = null
  resetForm()
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const editCategory = (row) => {
  isEdit.value = true
  form.categoryId = row.categoryId
  form.categoryName = row.categoryName
  form.sortOrder = row.sortOrder
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const resetForm = () => {
  form.categoryName = ''
  form.sortOrder = 0
  if (formRef.value) {
    formRef.value.resetFields()
  }
}

const submitForm = async () => {
  if (!formRef.value) return
  
  await formRef.value.validate(async (valid) => {
    if (!valid) return
    
    submitting.value = true
    try {
      const data = { categoryName: form.categoryName, sortOrder: form.sortOrder }
      if (isEdit.value) {
        await adminApi.updateCategory(form.categoryId, data)
        ElMessage.success('更新成功')
      } else {
        await adminApi.createCategory(data)
        ElMessage.success('添加成功')
      }
      dialogVisible.value = false
      fetchCategories()
    } catch (error) {
      ElMessage.error(isEdit.value ? '更新失败' : '添加失败')
    } finally {
      submitting.value = false
    }
  })
}

const showSortDialog = (row) => {
  sortCategory.value = row
  newSortOrder.value = row.sortOrder
  sortDialogVisible.value = true
}

const submitSort = async () => {
  if (!sortCategory.value) return
  
  sortLoading.value = true
  try {
    await adminApi.updateCategorySort(sortCategory.value.categoryId, newSortOrder.value)
    ElMessage.success('排序更新成功')
    sortDialogVisible.value = false
    fetchCategories()
  } catch (error) {
    ElMessage.error('排序更新失败')
  } finally {
    sortLoading.value = false
  }
}

const handleDelete = async (categoryId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要删除该分类吗？如果分类下存在教材，将无法删除。', '提示', { type: 'warning' })
    await adminApi.deleteCategory(categoryId)
    ElMessage.success('删除成功')
    fetchCategories()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('删除失败')
    }
  }
}

const batchDelete = async () => {
  if (!selectedIds.value.length) return
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(`确定要删除 ${selectedIds.value.length} 个分类吗？`, '提示', { type: 'warning' })
    await adminApi.batchDeleteCategories(selectedIds.value)
    ElMessage.success('批量删除成功')
    selectedIds.value = []
    fetchCategories()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('批量删除失败')
    }
  }
}

onMounted(fetchCategories)
</script>

<style scoped>
.admin-categories {
  max-width: 1200px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}

.card-header {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
}
</style>
