<template>
  <div class="admin-dashboard">
    <!-- 统计卡片 -->
    <el-row :gutter="20" class="stat-row">
      <el-col :span="6" v-for="stat in statistics" :key="stat.label">
        <el-card shadow="hover" class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-icon" :style="{ background: stat.color }">
            <el-icon><component :is="stat.icon" /></el-icon>
          </div>
          <div class="stat-content">
            <div class="stat-number">{{ stat.value }}</div>
            <div class="stat-label">{{ stat.label }}</div>
            <div class="stat-change" v-if="stat.change !== undefined">
              <span :class="stat.change >= 0 ? 'up' : 'down'">
                {{ stat.change >= 0 ? '↑' : '↓' }} {{ Math.abs(stat.change) }}%
              </span>
              较昨日
            </div>
          </div>
        </el-card>
      </el-col>
    </el-row>

    <!-- 图表区域 -->
    <el-row :gutter="20">
      <el-col :span="16">
        <el-card shadow="hover" class="chart-card">
          <template #header>
            <div class="chart-header">
              <span>📈 销售趋势</span>
              <el-radio-group v-model="trendType" size="small" @change="fetchTrend">
                <el-radio-button value="day">日</el-radio-button>
                <el-radio-button value="week">周</el-radio-button>
                <el-radio-button value="month">月</el-radio-button>
              </el-radio-group>
            </div>
          </template>
          <div ref="trendChartRef" class="chart-container"></div>
        </el-card>
      </el-col>
      <el-col :span="8">
        <el-card shadow="hover" class="chart-card">
          <template #header>
            <span>📊 订单分布</span>
          </template>
          <div ref="pieChartRef" class="chart-container"></div>
        </el-card>
      </el-col>
    </el-row>

    <!-- 最近订单 -->
    <el-card shadow="hover" class="table-card">
      <template #header>
        <div class="table-header">
          <span>📋 最近订单</span>
          <el-button type="primary" link @click="goToOrders">查看全部 →</el-button>
        </div>
      </template>
      <el-table :data="recentOrders" style="width: 100%" v-loading="orderLoading">
        <el-table-column prop="orderId" label="订单号" width="160" />
        <el-table-column prop="userId" label="用户ID" width="80" />
        <el-table-column prop="totalAmount" label="金额" width="120">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            ¥{{ row.totalAmount?.toFixed(2) }}
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column prop="orderStatus" label="状态" width="100">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-tag :type="getStatusType(row.orderStatus)" size="small">
              {{ getStatusText(row.orderStatus) }}
            </el-tag>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column prop="createTime" label="下单时间" width="180" />
        <el-table-column label="操作" width="100">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-button type="primary" link @click="goToOrderDetail(row.orderId)">
              查看
            </el-button>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
      </el-table>
    </el-card>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, nextTick } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import * as echarts from 'echarts'
import { adminApi } from '@/api/admin'

const router = useRouter()
const trendType = ref('day')
const trendChartRef = ref(null)
const pieChartRef = ref(null)
let trendChart = null
let pieChart = null

const orderLoading = ref(false)
const statistics = ref([])
const recentOrders = ref([])

// 状态映射
const statusMap = {
  0: { text: '待付款', type: 'warning' },
  1: { text: '待发货', type: 'info' },
  2: { text: '待收货', type: 'primary' },
  3: { text: '已完成', type: 'success' },
  4: { text: '已取消', type: 'danger' }
}

const getStatusText = (status) => statusMap[status]?.text || '未知'
const getStatusType = (status) => statusMap[status]?.type || 'info'

// 获取统计数据
const fetchStatistics = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await adminApi.getDashboardStatistics()
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      const data = res.data
      statistics.value = [
        { 
          label: '用户总数', 
          value: data.totalUsers || 0, 
          icon: 'User', 
          color: '#409EFF',
          change: data.userGrowth
        },
        { 
          label: '教材总数', 
          value: data.totalBooks || 0, 
          icon: 'Notebook', 
          color: '#67C23A',
          change: data.bookGrowth
        },
        { 
          label: '订单总数', 
          value: data.totalOrders || 0, 
          icon: 'List', 
          color: '#E6A23C',
          change: data.orderGrowth
        },
        { 
          label: '总销售额', 
          value: `¥${(data.totalSales || 0).toFixed(2)}`, 
          icon: 'Money', 
          color: '#F56C6C',
          change: data.salesGrowth
        }
      ]
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取统计数据失败:', error)
  }
}

// 获取趋势数据
const fetchTrend = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await adminApi.getDashboardTrend(trendType.value)
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      const data = res.data
      renderTrendChart(data)
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取趋势数据失败:', error)
  }
}

// 渲染趋势图
const renderTrendChart = (data) => {
  if (!trendChartRef.value) return
  
  if (!trendChart) {
    trendChart = echarts.init(trendChartRef.value)
  }
  
  const option = {
    tooltip: {
      trigger: 'axis',
      axisPointer: { type: 'cross' }
    },
    legend: {
      data: ['订单数', '销售额', '新增用户'],
      bottom: 0
    },
    grid: {
      left: '3%',
      right: '4%',
      bottom: '20%',
      top: '5%',
      containLabel: true
    },
    xAxis: {
      type: 'category',
      boundaryGap: false,
      data: data.dates || []
    },
    yAxis: [
      {
        type: 'value',
        name: '数量',
        splitLine: { lineStyle: { type: 'dashed' } }
      },
      {
        type: 'value',
        name: '金额 (元)',
        splitLine: { show: false }
      }
    ],
    series: [
      {
        name: '订单数',
        type: 'line',
        data: data.orderCounts || [],
        smooth: true,
        lineStyle: { color: '#409EFF' },
        areaStyle: { color: 'rgba(64, 158, 255, 0.1)' }
      },
      {
        name: '销售额',
        type: 'line',
        yAxisIndex: 1,
        data: data.salesAmounts || [],
        smooth: true,
        lineStyle: { color: '#F56C6C' },
        areaStyle: { color: 'rgba(245, 108, 108, 0.1)' }
      },
      {
        name: '新增用户',
        type: 'bar',
        data: data.userRegistrations || [],
        itemStyle: { color: '#67C23A' }
      }
    ]
  }
  
  trendChart.setOption(option)
  trendChart.resize()
}

// 获取订单分布
const fetchOrderDistribution = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await adminApi.getOrderStatusCount()
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      const data = res.data || {}
      const pieData = Object.entries(data).map(([key, value]) => ({
        name: getStatusText(Number(key)),
        value: value
      }))
      renderPieChart(pieData)
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取订单分布失败:', error)
  }
}

// 渲染饼图
const renderPieChart = (data) => {
  if (!pieChartRef.value) return
  
  if (!pieChart) {
    pieChart = echarts.init(pieChartRef.value)
  }
  
  const option = {
    tooltip: {
      trigger: 'item',
      formatter: '{a} <br/>{b}: {c} ({d}%)'
    },
    legend: {
      orient: 'vertical',
      right: '5%',
      top: 'center',
      itemWidth: 12,
      itemHeight: 12
    },
    series: [
      {
        name: '订单状态',
        type: 'pie',
        radius: ['40%', '70%'],
        avoidLabelOverlap: false,
        itemStyle: {
          borderRadius: 4,
          borderColor: '#fff',
          borderWidth: 2
        },
        label: {
          show: false
        },
        emphasis: {
          label: {
            show: true,
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: 'bold'
          }
        },
        data: data.length > 0 ? data : [{ name: '暂无数据', value: 1 }]
      }
    ]
  }
  
  pieChart.setOption(option)
  pieChart.resize()
}

// 获取最近订单
const fetchRecentOrders = async () => {
  orderLoading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await adminApi.getOrders({ pageNum: 1, pageSize: 5 })
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      const pageData = res.data
      recentOrders.value = pageData?.list || []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取最近订单失败:', error)
  } finally {
    orderLoading.value = false
  }
}

const goToOrders = () => {
  router.push('/admin/orders')
}

const goToOrderDetail = (orderId) => {
  router.push(`/admin/orders?orderId=${orderId}`)
}

// 窗口变化自适应
const handleResize = () => {
  trendChart?.resize()
  pieChart?.resize()
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await fetchStatistics()
  await fetchRecentOrders()
  await fetchOrderDistribution()
  await nextTick()
  await fetchTrend()
  
  window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize)
})
</script>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
// ✅ 新增：导入 UserFilled 图标
import { DataLine, Notebook, Menu, List, User, UserFilled, Setting, ArrowDown } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import { adminApi } from '@/api/admin'

const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const isCollapse = ref(false)
const adminInfo = ref(null)

const activeMenu = computed(() => route.path)
const currentRoute = computed(() => route)

const toggleCollapse = () => {
  isCollapse.value = !isCollapse.value
}

const fetchAdminInfo = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await adminApi.getAdminInfo()
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      adminInfo.value = res.data
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取管理员信息失败:', error)
  }
}

const handleCommand = (command) => {
  if (command === 'logout') {
    localStorage.removeItem('admin_token')
    localStorage.removeItem('admin_info')
    ElMessage.success('退出成功')
    router.push('/admin/login')
  } else if (command === 'profile') {
    router.push('/admin/profile')
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchAdminInfo()
  // 从 localStorage 读取管理员信息作为备用
  const info = localStorage.getItem('admin_info')
  if (info) {
    try {
      adminInfo.value = JSON.parse(info)
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('解析管理员信息失败:', e)
    }
  }
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="admin-members">
    <el-card shadow="hover">
      <template #header>
        <div class="card-header">
          <span>👥 普通用户管理</span>
          <span class="total-info">共 {{ total }} 位用户</span>
        </div>
      </template>

      <!-- 搜索栏 -->
      <el-form :inline="true" :model="searchForm" class="search-form">
        <el-form-item label="用户名">
          <el-input
            v-model="searchForm.keyword"
            placeholder="搜索用户名/手机号/邮箱"
            clearable
            style="width: 220px"
            @keyup.enter="handleSearch"
          />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="状态">
          <el-select v-model="searchForm.status" placeholder="全部" clearable style="width: 120px">
            <el-option label="正常" :value="1" />
            <el-option label="禁用" :value="0" />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item>
          <el-button type="primary" @click="handleSearch">搜索</el-button>
          <el-button @click="resetSearch">重置</el-button>
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>

      <!-- 表格 -->
      <el-table :data="userList" v-loading="loading" style="width: 100%">
        <el-table-column prop="userId" label="ID" width="80" />
        <el-table-column label="头像" width="80">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-avatar :size="40" :src="row.avatar || ''">
              {{ (row.username || 'U').charAt(0).toUpperCase() }}
            </el-avatar>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column prop="username" label="用户名" min-width="120" />
        <el-table-column prop="phone" label="手机号" width="140" />
        <el-table-column prop="email" label="邮箱" min-width="180" />
        <el-table-column prop="balance" label="余额" width="120">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <span class="balance">¥{{ formatMoney(row.balance) }}</span>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column prop="status" label="状态" width="100">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-tag :type="row.status === 1 ? 'success' : 'danger'" size="small">
              {{ row.status === 1 ? '正常' : '禁用' }}
            </el-tag>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column prop="createTime" label="注册时间" width="180" />
        <el-table-column label="操作" width="180" fixed="right">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-button
              v-if="row.status === 0"
              type="success"
              link
              size="small"
              @click="handleStatus(row.userId, 1)"
            >
              启用
            </el-button>
            <el-button
              v-else
              type="warning"
              link
              size="small"
              @click="handleStatus(row.userId, 0)"
            >
              禁用
            </el-button>
            <el-button
              type="danger"
              link
              size="small"
              @click="handleDelete(row.userId, row.username)"
            >
              删除
            </el-button>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
      </el-table>

      <!-- 分页 -->
      <div class="pagination-wrapper">
        <el-pagination
          v-model:current-page="pageNum"
          v-model:page-size="pageSize"
          :total="total"
          :page-sizes="[10, 20, 50, 100]"
          layout="total, sizes, prev, pager, next, jumper"
          @size-change="handleSizeChange"
          @current-change="handleCurrentChange"
        />
      </div>
    </el-card>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { adminApi } from '@/api/admin'

const loading = ref(false)
const userList = ref([])
const total = ref(0)
const pageNum = ref(1)
const pageSize = ref(10)

const searchForm = reactive({
  keyword: '',
  status: null
})

// 格式化金额
const formatMoney = (val) => {
  if (val === undefined || val === null || isNaN(val)) return '0.00'
  return Number(val).toFixed(2)
}

// 获取用户列表
const fetchUsers = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const params = {
      pageNum: pageNum.value,
      pageSize: pageSize.value
    }
    if (searchForm.keyword) params.keyword = searchForm.keyword
    if (searchForm.status !== null) params.status = searchForm.status

    const res = await adminApi.getMemberList(params)
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      userList.value = res.data?.list || []
      total.value = res.data?.total || 0
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取用户列表失败:', error)
    ElMessage.error('获取用户列表失败')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

// 搜索
const handleSearch = () => {
  pageNum.value = 1
  fetchUsers()
}

// 重置
const resetSearch = () => {
  searchForm.keyword = ''
  searchForm.status = null
  pageNum.value = 1
  fetchUsers()
}

// 分页
const handleSizeChange = (val) => {
  pageSize.value = val
  pageNum.value = 1
  fetchUsers()
}

const handleCurrentChange = (val) => {
  pageNum.value = val
  fetchUsers()
}

// 启用/禁用
const handleStatus = async (userId, status) => {
  const tip = status === 1 ? '启用' : '禁用'
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(`确定要${tip}该用户吗？`, '提示', { type: 'warning' })
    await adminApi.updateMemberStatus(userId, status)
    ElMessage.success(`${tip}成功`)
    fetchUsers()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error(`${tip}失败`)
    }
  }
}

// 删除
const handleDelete = async (userId, username) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(
      `确定要删除用户「${username}」吗？此操作不可恢复！`,
      '危险操作',
      { type: 'error' }
    )
    await adminApi.deleteMember(userId)
    ElMessage.success('删除成功')
    fetchUsers()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('删除失败')
    }
  }
}

onMounted(fetchUsers)
</script>

.admin-members {
  max-width: 1400px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}

.card-header {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
}

.total-info {
  font-size: 14px;
  color: #909399;
}

.search-form {
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.balance {
  color: #f56c6c;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.pagination-wrapper {
  margin-top: 16px;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: flex-end;
}
</style>

<template>
  <div class="admin-orders">
    <!-- 统计卡片 -->
    <el-row :gutter="20" class="stat-row">
      <el-col :span="4" v-for="stat in orderStats" :key="stat.label">
        <el-statistic :value="stat.value" :title="stat.label">
          <template #suffix>
            <span style="font-size:14px;color:#909399">单</span>
          </template>
        </el-statistic>
      </el-col>
    </el-row>

    <!-- 搜索区域 -->
    <el-card shadow="hover" class="search-card">
      <el-form :inline="true" :model="searchForm" class="search-form">
        <el-form-item label="订单号">
          <el-input v-model="searchForm.orderId" placeholder="输入订单号" clearable />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="用户ID">
          <el-input-number v-model="searchForm.userId" :min="1" placeholder="用户ID" style="width:140px" />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="状态">
          <el-select v-model="searchForm.status" placeholder="全部状态" clearable>
            <el-option label="待付款" :value="0" />
            <el-option label="待发货" :value="1" />
            <el-option label="待收货" :value="2" />
            <el-option label="已完成" :value="3" />
            <el-option label="已取消" :value="4" />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="时间范围">
          <el-date-picker
            v-model="searchForm.dateRange"
            type="datetimerange"
            range-separator="至"
            start-placeholder="开始时间"
            end-placeholder="结束时间"
            value-format="YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss"
          />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item>
          <el-button type="primary" @click="fetchOrders">搜索</el-button>
          <el-button @click="resetSearch">重置</el-button>
          <el-button type="success" @click="exportOrders">导出数据</el-button>
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
    </el-card>

    <!-- 表格 -->
    <el-card shadow="hover" class="table-card">
      <div class="table-toolbar">
        <div class="toolbar-left">
          <el-button type="success" plain :disabled="!selectedIds.length" @click="batchDeliver">
            批量发货
          </el-button>
          <el-button type="danger" plain :disabled="!selectedIds.length" @click="batchCancel">
            批量取消
          </el-button>
        </div>
        <div class="toolbar-right">
          <span class="total-info">共 {{ total }} 笔订单</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <el-table
        :data="orderList"
        style="width: 100%"
        v-loading="loading"
        @selection-change="handleSelectionChange"
      >
        <el-table-column type="selection" width="40" />
        <el-table-column prop="orderId" label="订单号" width="160" />
        <el-table-column prop="userId" label="用户ID" width="80" />
        <el-table-column prop="totalAmount" label="金额" width="100">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            ¥{{ row.totalAmount?.toFixed(2) }}
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column prop="orderStatus" label="状态" width="100">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-tag :type="getStatusType(row.orderStatus)" size="small">
              {{ getStatusText(row.orderStatus) }}
            </el-tag>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column prop="receiverName" label="收货人" width="80" />
        <el-table-column prop="receiverPhone" label="电话" width="120" />
        <el-table-column prop="createTime" label="下单时间" width="170" />
        <el-table-column label="操作" width="200" fixed="right">
          <template #default="{ row }">
            <el-button type="primary" link size="small" @click="viewOrder(row.orderId)">
              详情
            </el-button>
            <el-button 
              v-if="row.orderStatus === 1" 
              type="success" 
              link 
              size="small" 
              @click="handleDeliver(row.orderId)"
            >
              发货
            </el-button>
            <el-button 
              v-if="row.orderStatus === 0 || row.orderStatus === 1" 
              type="danger" 
              link 
              size="small" 
              @click="handleCancel(row.orderId)"
            >
              取消
            </el-button>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
      </el-table>

      <!-- 分页 -->
      <div class="pagination-wrapper">
        <el-pagination
          v-model:page-num="pageNum"
          v-model:page-size="pageSize"
          :total="total"
          :page-sizes="[10, 20, 50]"
          layout="total, sizes, prev, pager, next, jumper"
          @size-change="fetchOrders"
          @current-change="fetchOrders"
        />
      </div>
    </el-card>
  </div>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { adminApi } from '@/api/admin'

const router = useRouter()
const loading = ref(false)
const orderList = ref([])
const total = ref(0)
const pageNum = ref(1)
const pageSize = ref(10)
const selectedIds = ref([])

const searchForm = reactive({
  orderId: '',
  userId: null,
  status: null,
  dateRange: null
})

const statusMap = {
  0: { text: '待付款', type: 'warning' },
  1: { text: '待发货', type: 'info' },
  2: { text: '待收货', type: 'primary' },
  3: { text: '已完成', type: 'success' },
  4: { text: '已取消', type: 'danger' }
}

const getStatusText = (status) => statusMap[status]?.text || '未知'
const getStatusType = (status) => statusMap[status]?.type || 'info'

// 统计信息
const orderStats = ref([
  { label: '总订单', value: 0 },
  { label: '待付款', value: 0 },
  { label: '待发货', value: 0 },
  { label: '待收货', value: 0 },
  { label: '已完成', value: 0 },
  { label: '已取消', value: 0 }
])

const fetchOrders = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const params = {
      pageNum: pageNum.value,
      pageSize: pageSize.value,
      userId: searchForm.userId || undefined,
      status: searchForm.status !== null ? searchForm.status : undefined,
      startTime: searchForm.dateRange?.[0] || undefined,
      endTime: searchForm.dateRange?.[1] || undefined
    }
    // 移除空值
    Object.keys(params).forEach(key => {
      if (params[key] === undefined || params[key] === null || params[key] === '') {
        delete params[key]
      }
    })
    
    const res = await adminApi.getOrders(params)
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      const pageData = res.data
      orderList.value = pageData?.list || []
      total.value = pageData?.total || 0
      updateStats()
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取订单列表失败:', error)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const updateStats = () => {
  const list = orderList.value
  orderStats.value = [
    { label: '总订单', value: total.value },
    { label: '待付款', value: list.filter(o => o.orderStatus === 0).length },
    { label: '待发货', value: list.filter(o => o.orderStatus === 1).length },
    { label: '待收货', value: list.filter(o => o.orderStatus === 2).length },
    { label: '已完成', value: list.filter(o => o.orderStatus === 3).length },
    { label: '已取消', value: list.filter(o => o.orderStatus === 4).length }
  ]
}

const resetSearch = () => {
  searchForm.orderId = ''
  searchForm.userId = null
  searchForm.status = null
  searchForm.dateRange = null
  pageNum.value = 1
  fetchOrders()
}

const handleSelectionChange = (rows) => {
  selectedIds.value = rows.map(r => r.orderId)
}

// ========== 发货 ==========
const handleDeliver = async (orderId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要发货该订单吗？', '提示', { type: 'info' })
    await adminApi.deliverOrder(orderId)
    ElMessage.success('发货成功')
    fetchOrders()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('发货失败')
    }
  }
}

/**
 * 批量发货 - 只筛选"待发货"状态的订单
 */
const batchDeliver = async () => {
  if (!selectedIds.value.length) return

  // ✅ 关键修复：只筛选状态为 1（待发货）的订单
  const deliverableIds = orderList.value
    .filter(row => selectedIds.value.includes(row.orderId) && row.orderStatus === 1)
    .map(row => row.orderId)

  if (!deliverableIds.length) {
    ElMessage.warning('选中的订单中没有可发货的（只有"待发货"状态的订单才能发货）')
    return
  }

  // 提示用户跳过的订单数量
  const skipped = selectedIds.value.length - deliverableIds.length
  const tip = skipped > 0
    ? `确定要批量发货 ${deliverableIds.length} 个订单吗？（跳过 ${skipped} 个状态不符的订单）`
    : `确定要批量发货 ${deliverableIds.length} 个订单吗？`

  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(tip, '提示', { type: 'info' })
    await adminApi.batchDeliverOrders(deliverableIds)
    ElMessage.success('批量发货成功')
    selectedIds.value = []
    fetchOrders()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('批量发货失败')
    }
  }
}

// ========== 取消 ==========
const handleCancel = async (orderId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要取消该订单吗？', '提示', { type: 'warning' })
    await adminApi.cancelOrderByAdmin(orderId)
    ElMessage.success('取消成功')
    fetchOrders()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('取消失败')
    }
  }
}

/**
 * 批量取消 - 只筛选状态为 0（待付款）或 1（待发货）的订单
 */
const batchCancel = async () => {
  if (!selectedIds.value.length) return

  // ✅ 关键修复：只筛选可取消的订单（0-待付款 或 1-待发货）
  const cancellableIds = orderList.value
    .filter(row => selectedIds.value.includes(row.orderId) 
      && (row.orderStatus === 0 || row.orderStatus === 1))
    .map(row => row.orderId)

  if (!cancellableIds.length) {
    ElMessage.warning('选中的订单中没有可取消的（只有"待付款"或"待发货"状态的订单才能取消）')
    return
  }

  // 提示用户跳过的订单数量
  const skipped = selectedIds.value.length - cancellableIds.length
  const tip = skipped > 0
    ? `确定要批量取消 ${cancellableIds.length} 个订单吗？（跳过 ${skipped} 个状态不符的订单）`
    : `确定要批量取消 ${cancellableIds.length} 个订单吗？`

  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(tip, '提示', { type: 'warning' })
    await adminApi.batchCancelOrders(cancellableIds)
    ElMessage.success('批量取消成功')
    selectedIds.value = []
    fetchOrders()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('批量取消失败')
    }
  }
}

// ========== 查看详情 ==========
const viewOrder = (orderId) => {
  router.push(`/order/${orderId}`)
}

// ========== 导出 ==========
const exportOrders = async () => {
  try {
    const params = {
      status: searchForm.status !== null ? searchForm.status : undefined,
      startTime: searchForm.dateRange?.[0] || undefined,
      endTime: searchForm.dateRange?.[1] || undefined,
      format: 'csv'
    }
    Object.keys(params).forEach(key => {
      if (params[key] === undefined || params[key] === null) {
        delete params[key]
      }
    })
    
    const res = await adminApi.exportOrders(params)
    // 创建下载链接
    const blob = new Blob([res], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' })
    const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob)
    const a = document.createElement('a')
    a.href = url
    a.download = `订单数据_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.csv`
    a.click()
    window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
    ElMessage.success('导出成功')
  } catch (error) {
    ElMessage.error('导出失败')
  }
}

onMounted(fetchOrders)
</script>

            <el-form-item label="头像URL" prop="avatar">

            <el-form-item label="旧密码" prop="oldPassword">

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { adminApi } from '@/api/admin'

const router = useRouter()
const saving = ref(false)
const pwdLoading = ref(false)
const formRef = ref()
const pwdFormRef = ref()

const form = reactive({
  adminId: null,
  username: '',
  realName: '',
  role: '',
  roleDesc: '',
  phone: '',
  email: '',
  avatar: '',
  status: 1
})

const pwdForm = reactive({
  oldPassword: '',
  newPassword: '',
  confirmPassword: ''
})

const rules = {
  realName: [{ required: true, message: '请输入真实姓名', trigger: 'blur' }],
  phone: [{ pattern: /^1[3-9]\d{9}$/, message: '手机号格式不正确', trigger: 'blur' }],
  email: [{ type: 'email', message: '邮箱格式不正确', trigger: 'blur' }]
}

const validateConfirm = (rule, value, callback) => {
  if (value !== pwdForm.newPassword) {
    callback(new Error('两次输入的密码不一致'))
  } else {
    callback()
  }
}

const pwdRules = {
  oldPassword: [
    { required: true, message: '请输入旧密码', trigger: 'blur' },
    { min: 6, max: 20, message: '密码长度为6-20位', trigger: 'blur' }
  ],
  newPassword: [
    { required: true, message: '请输入新密码', trigger: 'blur' },
    { min: 6, max: 20, message: '密码长度为6-20位', trigger: 'blur' }
  ],
  confirmPassword: [
    { required: true, message: '请确认密码', trigger: 'blur' },
    { validator: validateConfirm, trigger: 'blur' }
  ]
}

const loadProfile = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await adminApi.getAdminInfo()
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      const data = res.data
      form.adminId = data.adminId
      form.username = data.username
      form.realName = data.realName || ''
      form.role = data.role
      form.roleDesc = data.roleDesc || data.role
      form.phone = data.phone || ''
      form.email = data.email || ''
      form.avatar = data.avatar || ''
      form.status = data.status
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取个人信息失败:', error)
  }
}

const saveProfile = async () => {
  if (!formRef.value) return
  
  await formRef.value.validate(async (valid) => {
    if (!valid) return
    
    saving.value = true
    try {
      const data = {
        realName: form.realName,
        phone: form.phone,
        email: form.email,
        avatar: form.avatar
      }
      await adminApi.updateProfile(data)
      ElMessage.success('保存成功')
      // 更新本地存储
      const info = localStorage.getItem('admin_info')
      if (info) {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(info)
          parsed.realName = form.realName
          parsed.phone = form.phone
          parsed.email = form.email
          parsed.avatar = form.avatar
          localStorage.setItem('admin_info', JSON.stringify(parsed))
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('更新本地存储失败:', e)
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      ElMessage.error('保存失败')
    } finally {
      saving.value = false
    }
  })
}

const updatePassword = async () => {
  if (!pwdFormRef.value) return
  
  await pwdFormRef.value.validate(async (valid) => {
    if (!valid) return
    
    pwdLoading.value = true
    try {
      const data = {
        oldPassword: pwdForm.oldPassword,
        newPassword: pwdForm.newPassword
      }
      await adminApi.updatePassword(data)
      ElMessage.success('密码修改成功，请重新登录')
      // 清除管理员 token 并跳转到登录页
      localStorage.removeItem('admin_token')
      localStorage.removeItem('admin_info')
      setTimeout(() => {
        router.push('/admin/login')
      }, 1500)
    } catch (error) {
      ElMessage.error('密码修改失败')
    } finally {
      pwdLoading.value = false
    }
  })
}

onMounted(loadProfile)
</script>

<template>
  <div class="admin-users">
    <el-card shadow="hover">

            <el-button type="primary" link size="small" @click="editAdmin(row)">
              编辑

              v-if="row.status === 0" 
              type="success" 
              link 
              size="small" 
              @click="handleEnable(row.adminId)"
            >
              启用
            </el-button>
            <el-button 
              v-else 
              type="danger" 
              link 
              size="small" 
              @click="handleDisable(row.adminId)"
            >
              禁用
            </el-button>
            <el-button type="danger" link size="small" @click="handleDelete(row.adminId)">
              删除
            </el-button>
          </template>

    <!-- 创建/编辑对话框 -->

      v-model="dialogVisible"

    >

        </el-form-item>

          <el-select v-model="form.role" placeholder="请选择角色" style="width:100%">

          <el-input v-model="form.avatar" placeholder="请输入头像图片URL" />
        </el-form-item>

          <el-alert title="默认密码为 123456，请管理员登录后修改" type="info" :closable="false" />
        </el-form-item>

        <el-button @click="dialogVisible = false">取消</el-button>
        <el-button type="primary" @click="submitForm" :loading="submitting">
          {{ isEdit ? '保存修改' : '确认添加' }}
        </el-button>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { adminApi } from '@/api/admin'

const loading = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const adminList = ref([])
const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const isEdit = ref(false)
const formRef = ref()

const form = reactive({
  adminId: null,
  username: '',
  realName: '',
  role: 'admin',
  phone: '',
  email: '',
  avatar: ''
})

const dialogTitle = computed(() => isEdit.value ? '编辑管理员' : '新增管理员')

const rules = {
  username: [{ required: true, message: '请输入用户名', trigger: 'blur' }],
  realName: [{ required: true, message: '请输入真实姓名', trigger: 'blur' }],
  role: [{ required: true, message: '请选择角色', trigger: 'change' }]
}

const getRoleText = (role) => {
  const map = {
    super_admin: '超级管理员',
    admin: '管理员',
    operator: '运营人员',
    viewer: '查看者'
  }
  return map[role] || role
}

const fetchAdmins = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await adminApi.getAdminList()
    if (res && res.code === 200) {
      adminList.value = res.data || []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('获取管理员列表失败:', error)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const showCreateDialog = () => {
  isEdit.value = false
  form.adminId = null
  resetForm()
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const editAdmin = (row) => {
  isEdit.value = true
  form.adminId = row.adminId
  form.username = row.username
  form.realName = row.realName || ''
  form.role = row.role
  form.phone = row.phone || ''
  form.email = row.email || ''
  form.avatar = row.avatar || ''
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const resetForm = () => {
  form.username = ''
  form.realName = ''
  form.role = 'admin'
  form.phone = ''
  form.email = ''
  form.avatar = ''
  if (formRef.value) {
    formRef.value.resetFields()
  }
}

const submitForm = async () => {
  if (!formRef.value) return
  
  await formRef.value.validate(async (valid) => {
    if (!valid) return
    
    submitting.value = true
    try {
      const data = {
        username: form.username,
        realName: form.realName,
        role: form.role,
        phone: form.phone,
        email: form.email,
        avatar: form.avatar
      }
      
      if (isEdit.value) {
        await adminApi.updateAdmin(form.adminId, data)
        ElMessage.success('更新成功')
      } else {
        await adminApi.createAdmin(data)
        ElMessage.success('添加成功，默认密码为 123456')
      }
      dialogVisible.value = false
      fetchAdmins()
    } catch (error) {
      ElMessage.error(isEdit.value ? '更新失败' : '添加失败')
    } finally {
      submitting.value = false
    }
  })
}

const handleEnable = async (adminId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要启用该管理员吗？', '提示', { type: 'info' })
    await adminApi.enableAdmin(adminId)
    ElMessage.success('启用成功')
    fetchAdmins()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('启用失败')
    }
  }
}

const handleDisable = async (adminId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要禁用该管理员吗？', '提示', { type: 'warning' })
    await adminApi.disableAdmin(adminId)
    ElMessage.success('禁用成功')
    fetchAdmins()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('禁用失败')
    }
  }
}

const handleDelete = async (adminId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要删除该管理员吗？此操作不可恢复！', '危险操作', { type: 'error' })
    await adminApi.deleteAdmin(adminId)
    ElMessage.success('删除成功')
    fetchAdmins()
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error('删除失败')
    }
  }
}

onMounted(fetchAdmins)
</script>

package com.bookmall.gateway;

import com.bookmall.common.config.WebMvcConfig;
import com.bookmall.common.interceptor.AuthInterceptor;
import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.cloud.client.discovery.EnableDiscoveryClient;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.ComponentScan;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.FilterType;

/**
 * 网关服务启动类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@SpringBootApplication
@EnableDiscoveryClient
@EnableConfigurationProperties
@ComponentScan(
        basePackages = {"com.bookmall.gateway", "com.bookmall.common"},
        excludeFilters = {
                @ComponentScan.Filter(
                        type = FilterType.ASSIGNABLE_TYPE,
                        classes = {WebMvcConfig.class, AuthInterceptor.class}
                ),
                @ComponentScan.Filter(
                        type = FilterType.REGEX,
                        pattern = "com.bookmall.common.config.*"
                ),
                @ComponentScan.Filter(
                        type = FilterType.REGEX,
                        pattern = "com.bookmall.common.interceptor.*"
                )
        }
)
public class GatewayApplication {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SpringApplication.run(GatewayApplication.class, args);
        System.out.println("========================================");
        System.out.println("  📚 教材商城系统 - Gateway模块启动成功！");
        System.out.println("  🔗 网关地址: http://localhost:8090");
        System.out.println("========================================");
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\config\CorsConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.config;

import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.server.reactive.ServerHttpRequest;
import org.springframework.http.server.reactive.ServerHttpResponse;
import org.springframework.web.cors.reactive.CorsUtils;
import org.springframework.web.server.ServerWebExchange;
import org.springframework.web.server.WebFilter;
import org.springframework.web.server.WebFilterChain;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

/**
 * CORS跨域配置
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class CorsConfig {

    private static final String ALLOWED_ORIGINS = "*";
    private static final String ALLOWED_METHODS = "GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS";
    private static final String ALLOWED_HEADERS = "*";
    private static final String ALLOWED_CREDENTIALS = "true";
    private static final String MAX_AGE = "3600";

    @Bean
    public WebFilter corsWebFilter() {
        return (ServerWebExchange exchange, WebFilterChain chain) -> {
            ServerHttpRequest request = exchange.getRequest();
            if (CorsUtils.isCorsRequest(request)) {
                ServerHttpResponse response = exchange.getResponse();
                HttpHeaders headers = response.getHeaders();
                headers.add("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", ALLOWED_ORIGINS);
                headers.add("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", ALLOWED_METHODS);
                headers.add("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", ALLOWED_HEADERS);
                headers.add("Access-Control-Allow-Credentials", ALLOWED_CREDENTIALS);
                headers.add("Access-Control-Max-Age", MAX_AGE);
                if (request.getMethod() == HttpMethod.OPTIONS) {
                    response.setStatusCode(HttpStatus.OK);
                    return Mono.empty();
                }
            }
            return chain.filter(exchange);
        };
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\config\GatewayConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.config;

import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.route.RouteLocator;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.route.builder.RouteLocatorBuilder;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

/**
 * 网关路由配置
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class GatewayConfig {

    @Bean
    public RouteLocator customRouteLocator(RouteLocatorBuilder builder) {
        return builder.routes()
                // User服务路由
                .route("user-service", r -> r
                        .path("/api/user/**")
                        .filters(f -> f
                                .stripPrefix(1)  // 移除 /api 前缀
                                .retry(config -> config.setRetries(3)))
                        .uri("http://localhost:8081"))

                // Admin服务路由
                .route("admin-service", r -> r
                        .path("/api/admin/**")
                        .filters(f -> f
                                .stripPrefix(1)
                                .retry(config -> config.setRetries(3)))
                        .uri("http://localhost:8087"))

                // Book服务路由
                .route("book-service", r -> r
                        .path("/api/books/**")
                        .filters(f -> f
                                .stripPrefix(1)
                                .retry(config -> config.setRetries(3)))
                        .uri("http://localhost:8083"))

                // Category服务路由
                .route("category-service", r -> r
                        .path("/api/categories/**")
                        .filters(f -> f
                                .stripPrefix(1)
                                .retry(config -> config.setRetries(3)))
                        .uri("http://localhost:8082"))

                // Cart服务路由
                .route("cart-service", r -> r
                        .path("/api/cart/**")
                        .filters(f -> f
                                .stripPrefix(1)
                                .retry(config -> config.setRetries(3)))
                        .uri("http://localhost:8084"))

                // Order服务路由
                .route("order-service", r -> r
                        .path("/api/order/**")
                        .filters(f -> f
                                .stripPrefix(1)
                                .retry(config -> config.setRetries(3)))
                        .uri("http://localhost:8085"))

                // Payment服务路由
                .route("payment-service", r -> r
                        .path("/api/payment/**")
                        .filters(f -> f
                                .stripPrefix(1)
                                .retry(config -> config.setRetries(3)))
                        .uri("http://localhost:8086"))

                // Review服务路由
                .route("review-service", r -> r
                        .path("/api/review/**")
                        .filters(f -> f
                                .stripPrefix(1)
                                .retry(config -> config.setRetries(3)))
                        .uri("http://localhost:8089"))

                // Address服务路由
                .route("address-service", r -> r
                        .path("/api/address/**")
                        .filters(f -> f
                                .stripPrefix(1)
                                .retry(config -> config.setRetries(3)))
                        .uri("http://localhost:8088"))
                .build();
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\config\RateLimiterConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.config;

import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.filter.ratelimit.KeyResolver;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

/**
 * 限流配置
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class RateLimiterConfig {

    /**
     * 根据IP限流
     */
    @Bean
    public KeyResolver ipKeyResolver() {
        return exchange -> {
            String ip = exchange.getRequest().getRemoteAddress() != null
                    ? exchange.getRequest().getRemoteAddress().getAddress().getHostAddress()
                    : "unknown";
            return Mono.just(ip);
        };
    }

    /**
     * 根据用户ID限流（需要解析Token）
     * 标记为 @Primary，作为默认限流策略
     */
    @Bean
    @Primary
    public KeyResolver userKeyResolver() {
        return exchange -> {
            String token = exchange.getRequest().getHeaders().getFirst("Authorization");
            if (token != null && token.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
                return Mono.just(token.substring(7));
            }
            return Mono.just("anonymous");
        };
    }

    /**
     * 根据请求路径限流
     */
    @Bean
    public KeyResolver pathKeyResolver() {
        return exchange -> {
            String path = exchange.getRequest().getPath().value();
            return Mono.just(path);
        };
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\config\RedisConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.config;

import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.data.redis.connection.RedisConnectionFactory;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.redis.serializer.Jackson2JsonRedisSerializer;
import org.springframework.data.redis.serializer.StringRedisSerializer;

@Configuration
public class RedisConfig {

    @Bean
    public RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate(RedisConnectionFactory connectionFactory) {
        RedisTemplate<String, Object> template = new RedisTemplate<>();
        template.setConnectionFactory(connectionFactory);

        // 使用Jackson2JsonRedisSerializer来序列化和反序列化redis的value值
        Jackson2JsonRedisSerializer<Object> serializer = new Jackson2JsonRedisSerializer<>(Object.class);

        template.setKeySerializer(new StringRedisSerializer());
        template.setValueSerializer(serializer);
        template.setHashKeySerializer(new StringRedisSerializer());
        template.setHashValueSerializer(serializer);
        template.afterPropertiesSet();

        return template;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\filter\AuthGlobalFilter.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.filter;

import com.bookmall.gateway.properties.WhitelistProperties;
import com.bookmall.gateway.utils.JwtUtil;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.filter.GatewayFilterChain;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.filter.GlobalFilter;
import org.springframework.core.Ordered;
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DataBuffer;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.server.reactive.ServerHttpRequest;
import org.springframework.http.server.reactive.ServerHttpResponse;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.util.AntPathMatcher;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;
import org.springframework.web.server.ServerWebExchange;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 认证全局过滤器 - 验证JWT Token + 管理端角色校验
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AuthGlobalFilter implements GlobalFilter, Ordered {

    private final WhitelistProperties whitelistProperties;
    private final AntPathMatcher pathMatcher = new AntPathMatcher();

    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        ServerHttpRequest request = exchange.getRequest();
        String path = request.getPath().value();

        log.info("===== AuthGlobalFilter 拦截请求: {} =====", path);

        // 获取白名单列表
        List<String> whitelist = whitelistProperties.getUrls();
        log.info("当前白名单配置: {}", whitelist);

        if (whitelist != null && !whitelist.isEmpty()) {
            for (String pattern : whitelist) {
                if (pathMatcher.match(pattern, path)) {
                    log.info("✅ 白名单匹配成功，跳过认证: {} 匹配 {}", path, pattern);
                    return chain.filter(exchange);
                }
            }
        } else {
            log.warn("⚠️ 白名单配置为空，使用默认白名单");
            List<String> defaultWhitelist = whitelistProperties.getDefaultWhitelist();
            for (String pattern : defaultWhitelist) {
                if (pathMatcher.match(pattern, path)) {
                    log.info("✅ 默认白名单匹配成功，跳过认证: {} 匹配 {}", path, pattern);
                    return chain.filter(exchange);
                }
            }
        }

        log.info("❌ 不在白名单中，需要认证: {}", path);

        // 获取Token
        String token = getTokenFromRequest(request);
        if (!StringUtils.hasText(token)) {
            log.warn("❌ 请求缺少Token: {}", path);
            return unauthorizedResponse(exchange);
        }

        log.debug("Token: {}", token.substring(0, Math.min(token.length(), 20)) + "...");

        // 验证Token
        if (!JwtUtil.validateToken(token)) {
            log.warn("❌ Token无效或已过期: {}", path);
            return unauthorizedResponse(exchange);
        }

        // 从Token获取用户信息
        Long userId = JwtUtil.getUserIdFromToken(token);
        String username = JwtUtil.getUsernameFromToken(token);
        String role = JwtUtil.getRoleFromToken(token);

        if (userId == null) {
            log.warn("❌ Token解析失败: {}", path);
            return unauthorizedResponse(exchange);
        }

        // ==================== 新增：角色校验 ====================
        // 管理端接口只允许 admin 角色访问
        // 注意：/api/admin/login 已在白名单中提前放行，不会走到这里
        if (path.startsWith("/api/admin/")) {
            if (!"admin".equals(role)) {
                log.warn("⛔ 越权访问被拦截: userId={}, username={}, role={}, path={}",
                        userId, username, role, path);
                return forbiddenResponse(exchange);
            }
        }
        // =======================================================

        // 将用户信息放入请求头，传递给下游服务
        ServerHttpRequest mutatedRequest = request.mutate()
                .header("X-User-Id", String.valueOf(userId))
                .header("X-Username", username != null ? username : "")
                .header("X-User-Role", role != null ? role : "")
                .build();

        log.info("✅ 认证通过: userId={}, username={}, role={}, path={}", userId, username, role, path);

        return chain.filter(exchange.mutate().request(mutatedRequest).build());
    }

    /**
     * 从请求中获取Token
     */
    private String getTokenFromRequest(ServerHttpRequest request) {
        String bearerToken = request.getHeaders().getFirst(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);
        if (StringUtils.hasText(bearerToken) && bearerToken.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            return bearerToken.substring(7);
        }
        return null;
    }

    /**
     * 返回未授权响应（401）
     */
    private Mono<Void> unauthorizedResponse(ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        ServerHttpResponse response = exchange.getResponse();
        response.setStatusCode(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED);
        return response.setComplete();
    }

    /**
     * 返回无权限响应（403），带 JSON body
     */
    private Mono<Void> forbiddenResponse(ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        ServerHttpResponse response = exchange.getResponse();
        response.setStatusCode(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN);
        response.getHeaders().setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);

        String body = "{\"code\":403,\"message\":\"无权限访问，仅管理员可操作\",\"success\":false}";
        DataBuffer buffer = response.bufferFactory()
                .wrap(body.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        return response.writeWith(Mono.just(buffer));
    }

    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return -100;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\filter\LoggingGlobalFilter.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.filter;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.filter.GatewayFilterChain;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.filter.GlobalFilter;
import org.springframework.core.Ordered;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.server.ServerWebExchange;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * 日志全局过滤器 - 记录请求日志
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
public class LoggingGlobalFilter implements GlobalFilter, Ordered {

    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        // 声明为 final
        final String traceId = UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "").substring(0, 16);
        final long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // 获取请求信息
        final String method = exchange.getRequest().getMethod().name();
        final String path = exchange.getRequest().getPath().value();
        final String remoteAddr = exchange.getRequest().getRemoteAddress() != null ?
                exchange.getRequest().getRemoteAddress().getHostString() : "unknown";

        // 记录请求日志
        log.info("请求开始: traceId={}, method={}, path={}, remoteAddr={}",
                traceId, method, path, remoteAddr);

        // 将traceId放入请求头，传递给下游服务
        ServerWebExchange mutatedExchange = exchange.mutate()
                .request(exchange.getRequest().mutate()
                        .header("X-Trace-Id", traceId)
                        .build())
                .build();

        return chain.filter(mutatedExchange)
                .doFinally(signalType -> {
                    long duration = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;
                    int status = mutatedExchange.getResponse().getStatusCode() != null ?
                            mutatedExchange.getResponse().getStatusCode().value() : 0;
                    log.info("请求完成: traceId={}, method={}, path={}, status={}, duration={}ms",
                            traceId, method, path, status, duration);
                });
    }

    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        // 最高优先级，最先执行
        return -200;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\filter\RequestTraceFilter.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.filter;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.filter.GatewayFilter;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.filter.GatewayFilterChain;
import org.springframework.core.Ordered;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.server.ServerWebExchange;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

/**
 * 请求追踪过滤器 - 添加请求追踪信息
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
public class RequestTraceFilter implements GatewayFilter, Ordered {

    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        // 添加请求时间戳
        exchange = exchange.mutate()
                .request(exchange.getRequest().mutate()
                        .header("X-Request-Time", String.valueOf(System.currentTimeMillis()))
                        .build())
                .build();

        return chain.filter(exchange);
    }

    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return -150;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\filter\ResponseWrapperFilter.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.filter;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.filter.GatewayFilterChain;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.filter.GlobalFilter;
import org.springframework.core.Ordered;
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DataBuffer;
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DataBufferFactory;
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DataBufferUtils;
import org.springframework.http.server.reactive.ServerHttpResponse;
import org.springframework.http.server.reactive.ServerHttpResponseDecorator;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.server.ServerWebExchange;
import reactor.core.publisher.Flux;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

/**
 * 响应包装过滤器 - 用于记录响应日志
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
public class ResponseWrapperFilter implements GlobalFilter, Ordered {

    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        ServerHttpResponse originalResponse = exchange.getResponse();

        // 包装响应，用于记录响应内容（可开启）
        DataBufferFactory bufferFactory = originalResponse.bufferFactory();
        ServerHttpResponseDecorator decoratedResponse = new ServerHttpResponseDecorator(originalResponse) {
            public Mono<Void> writeWith(Flux<DataBuffer> body) {
                if (body != null) {
                    // 记录响应状态
                    log.debug("响应状态: {}", this.getStatusCode());
                }
                return super.writeWith(body);
            }
        };

        return chain.filter(exchange.mutate().response(decoratedResponse).build());
    }

    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return -50;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\handler\FallbackHandler.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.handler;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.BodyInserters;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.server.HandlerFunction;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.server.ServerRequest;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.server.ServerResponse;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

/**
 * 服务降级处理器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
public class FallbackHandler implements HandlerFunction<ServerResponse> {

    @Override
    public Mono<ServerResponse> handle(ServerRequest request) {
        log.warn("服务降级: {}", request.path());

        String response = String.format(
                "{\"code\":503,\"message\":\"服务暂不可用，请稍后再试\",\"timestamp\":%d}",
                System.currentTimeMillis()
        );

        return ServerResponse.status(HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .body(BodyInserters.fromValue(response));
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\handler\GlobalExceptionHandler.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.handler;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.boot.web.reactive.error.ErrorWebExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.cloud.gateway.support.NotFoundException;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.core.annotation.Order;
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DataBuffer;
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DataBufferFactory;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.server.reactive.ServerHttpResponse;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;
import org.springframework.web.server.ServerWebExchange;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

/**
 * 全局异常处理器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Order(-1)
@Configuration
public class GlobalExceptionHandler implements ErrorWebExceptionHandler {

    @Override
    public Mono<Void> handle(ServerWebExchange exchange, Throwable ex) {
        ServerHttpResponse response = exchange.getResponse();
        DataBufferFactory bufferFactory = response.bufferFactory();

        if (response.isCommitted()) {
            return Mono.error(ex);
        }

        response.getHeaders().setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        response.setStatusCode(getHttpStatus(ex));

        String errorMsg = getErrorMessage(ex);
        String jsonResponse = String.format(
                "{\"code\":%d,\"message\":\"%s\",\"timestamp\":%d}",
                response.getStatusCode().value(),
                errorMsg,
                System.currentTimeMillis()
        );

        DataBuffer buffer = bufferFactory.wrap(jsonResponse.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        return response.writeWith(Mono.just(buffer));
    }

    /**
     * 获取HTTP状态码
     */
    private HttpStatus getHttpStatus(Throwable ex) {
        if (ex instanceof NotFoundException) {
            return HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND;
        }
        if (ex instanceof ResponseStatusException) {
            return ((ResponseStatusException) ex).getStatus();
        }
        return HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR;
    }

    /**
     * 获取错误信息
     */
    private String getErrorMessage(Throwable ex) {
        if (ex instanceof NotFoundException) {
            return "服务不存在";
        }
        if (ex instanceof ResponseStatusException) {
            return ex.getMessage();
        }
        log.error("网关异常: ", ex);
        return "系统繁忙，请稍后再试";
    }
}

package com.bookmall.gateway.properties;

import lombok.Data;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 白名单配置属性
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Data
@Component
@ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "auth")  // 修改为 auth
public class WhitelistProperties {

    /**
     * 白名单URL列表（支持Ant风格通配符）
     */
    private List<String> whitelist = new ArrayList<>();

    /**
     * 获取白名单URL列表（兼容方法）
     */
    public List<String> getUrls() {
        if (whitelist == null || whitelist.isEmpty()) {
            return getDefaultWhitelist();
        }
        return whitelist;
    }

    /**
     * 默认白名单（当配置为空时使用）
     */
    public List<String> getDefaultWhitelist() {
        List<String> defaultList = new ArrayList<>();
        // 用户认证
        defaultList.add("/api/user/login");
        defaultList.add("/api/user/register");
        defaultList.add("/api/user/captcha");
        defaultList.add("/api/user/forgot-password");
        defaultList.add("/api/user/send-code");
        defaultList.add("/api/user/check-username");
        // 管理员认证
        defaultList.add("/api/admin/login");
        // 公开接口
        defaultList.add("/api/books");
        defaultList.add("/api/books/");
        defaultList.add("/api/books/**");
        defaultList.add("/api/categories");
        defaultList.add("/api/categories/");
        defaultList.add("/api/categories/**");
        defaultList.add("/api/review/list");
        defaultList.add("/api/review/list/**");
        defaultList.add("/api/review/detail/**");
        // 支付回调
        defaultList.add("/api/payment/callback/**");
        // Swagger
        defaultList.add("/swagger-ui/**");
        defaultList.add("/v3/api-docs/**");
        defaultList.add("/doc.html");
        defaultList.add("/webjars/**");
        // Actuator
        defaultList.add("/actuator/**");
        // 其他
        defaultList.add("/favicon.ico");
        defaultList.add("/error");
        return defaultList;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Gateway\src\main\java\com\bookmall\gateway\utils\JwtUtil.java ====================

package com.bookmall.gateway.utils;

import io.jsonwebtoken.Claims;
import io.jsonwebtoken.Jwts;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

/**
 * JWT工具类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
public class JwtUtil {

    private static final String SECRET = "book_mall_secret_key_2024_secure";

    /**
     * 从Token中获取用户ID
     */
    public static Long getUserIdFromToken(String token) {
        try {
            Claims claims = parseToken(token);
            Object userId = claims.get("userId");
            if (userId instanceof Long) {
                return (Long) userId;
            }
            if (userId instanceof Integer) {
                return ((Integer) userId).longValue();
            }
            return null;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("解析Token获取userId失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 从Token中获取用户名
     */
    public static String getUsernameFromToken(String token) {
        try {
            Claims claims = parseToken(token);
            return claims.get("username", String.class);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("解析Token获取username失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 从Token中获取角色
     */
    public static String getRoleFromToken(String token) {
        try {
            Claims claims = parseToken(token);
            return claims.get("role", String.class);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("解析Token获取role失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 验证Token是否有效
     */
    public static Boolean validateToken(String token) {
        try {
            Claims claims = parseToken(token);
            return !claims.getExpiration().before(new java.util.Date());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Token验证失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 解析Token
     */
    private static Claims parseToken(String token) {
        return Jwts.parser()
                .setSigningKey(SECRET)
                .parseClaimsJws(token)
                .getBody();
    }
}

spring:
  application:
    name: gateway-service

  # ========== 激活环境（默认dev） ==========
  profiles:
    active: dev

  # ========== Nacos 配置中心 ==========

    nacos:

      config:

        group: DEFAULT_GROUP
        refresh-enabled: true
        # 共享配置（所有微服务通用）
        shared-configs:
          - data-id: common-config.yaml
            group: DEFAULT_GROUP
            refresh: true
        # 扩展配置（按环境区分：dev / test / prod）
        extension-configs:
          - data-id: gateway-service-${spring.profiles.active}.yaml
            group: DEFAULT_GROUP
            refresh: true
      # ---------- 服务注册与发现 ----------
      discovery:
        enabled: true
        server-addr: 127.0.0.1:8848          # ✅ 与 config 保持一致
        namespace: public
        group: DEFAULT_GROUP
        ip: 127.0.0.1                        # ✅ 显式指定注册 IP，避免多网卡注册错误
        port: 8090                           # ✅ 显式指定网关端口，与 application.yml 一致

# ========== 日志配置 ==========
logging:
  level:
    com.alibaba.nacos: warn
    org.springframework.cloud.gateway: debug
    com.bookmall.gateway: debug              # ✅ 增加自己网关包的日志级别
    org.springframework.web: info
