package com.bookmall.order;

import org.mybatis.spring.annotation.MapperScan;
import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
import org.springframework.cloud.client.discovery.EnableDiscoveryClient;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.EnableFeignClients;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.ComponentScan;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.EnableScheduling;

/**
 * 订单服务启动类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@SpringBootApplication
@EnableDiscoveryClient
@EnableFeignClients
@EnableScheduling
@MapperScan("com.bookmall.order.mapper")
@ComponentScan(basePackages = {"com.bookmall.order", "com.bookmall.common"})
public class OrderApplication {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SpringApplication.run(OrderApplication.class, args);
        System.out.println("========================================");
        System.out.println("  📚 教材商城系统 - Order模块启动成功！");
        System.out.println("  🔗 访问地址: http://localhost:8085");
        System.out.println("  📖 API文档: http://localhost:8085/swagger-ui.html");
        System.out.println("  💾 Druid监控: http://localhost:8085/druid");
        System.out.println("========================================");
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Order\src\main\java\com\bookmall\order\config\OrderConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.order.config;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.DbType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.MybatisPlusInterceptor;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.inner.PaginationInnerInterceptor;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

/**
 * Order模块配置类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class OrderConfig {

    /**
     * MyBatis-Plus 分页插件
     */
    @Bean
    public MybatisPlusInterceptor mybatisPlusInterceptor() {
        MybatisPlusInterceptor interceptor = new MybatisPlusInterceptor();
        interceptor.addInnerInterceptor(new PaginationInnerInterceptor(DbType.MYSQL));
        return interceptor;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Order\src\main\java\com\bookmall\order\controller\OrderController.java ====================

package com.bookmall.order.controller;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.PageResult;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.order.dto.OrderCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.order.dto.OrderDTO;
import com.bookmall.order.service.OrderService;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Parameter;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.format.annotation.DateTimeFormat;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.validation.Valid;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 订单控制器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/order")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "订单接口", description = "订单管理")
public class OrderController {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    private Long getUserId(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String userIdHeader = request.getHeader("X-User-Id");
        if (userIdHeader != null && !userIdHeader.isEmpty()) {
            try {
                return Long.parseLong(userIdHeader);
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                log.warn("解析 userId 失败: {}", userIdHeader);
            }
        }
        Object userIdAttr = request.getAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID);
        if (userIdAttr instanceof Long) {
            return (Long) userIdAttr;
        }
        throw new RuntimeException("用户未登录");
    }

    // ==================== 用户端 ====================

    @PostMapping
    @Operation(summary = "创建订单")
    public Result<OrderDTO> createOrder(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @Valid @RequestBody OrderCreateDTO orderCreateDTO) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        OrderDTO order = orderService.createOrderFromCart(userId, orderCreateDTO);
        return Result.success("订单创建成功", order);
    }

    @PostMapping("/direct")
    @Operation(summary = "立即购买")
    public Result<OrderDTO> directBuy(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @RequestParam @NotNull Long bookId,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer quantity,
            @RequestParam @NotNull Long addressId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String remark) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        OrderDTO order = orderService.createOrderDirect(userId, bookId, quantity, addressId, remark);
        return Result.success("订单创建成功", order);
    }

    @GetMapping
    @Operation(summary = "获取用户订单列表")
    public Result<PageResult<OrderDTO>> getUserOrders(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        Page<OrderDTO> page = orderService.getUserOrders(userId, status, pageNum, pageSize);
        return Result.success(PageResult.of(page));
    }

    @GetMapping("/{orderId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取订单详情")
    public Result<OrderDTO> getOrderDetail(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        OrderDTO order = orderService.getOrderDetail(userId, orderId);
        return Result.success(order);
    }

    @PostMapping("/{orderId}/pay")
    @Operation(summary = "支付订单")
    public Result<Void> payOrder(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        orderService.payOrder(userId, orderId);
        return Result.success("支付成功");
    }

    @PutMapping("/{orderId}/cancel")
    @Operation(summary = "取消订单")
    public Result<Void> cancelOrder(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        orderService.cancelOrder(userId, orderId);
        return Result.success("取消成功");
    }

    @PutMapping("/{orderId}/receive")
    @Operation(summary = "确认收货")
    public Result<Void> confirmReceive(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        orderService.confirmReceive(userId, orderId);
        return Result.success("确认收货成功");
    }

    @GetMapping("/count/status")
    @Operation(summary = "统计用户各状态订单数量")
    public Result<List<Map<String, Object>>> countUserOrdersByStatus(HttpServletRequest request) {
        Long userId = getUserId(request);
        List<Map<String, Object>> counts = orderService.countUserOrdersByStatus(userId);
        return Result.success(counts);
    }

    // ==================== 管理员端 ====================

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders")
    @Operation(summary = "获取所有订单列表", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<PageResult<OrderDTO>> getAllOrders(
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long userId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime startTime,
            @RequestParam(required = false) @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime endTime,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {
        Page<OrderDTO> page = orderService.getAllOrders(userId, status, startTime, endTime, pageNum, pageSize);
        return Result.success(PageResult.of(page));
    }

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取订单详情（管理员）", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<OrderDTO> getOrderDetailByAdmin(@PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId) {
        OrderDTO order = orderService.getOrderDetailByAdmin(orderId);
        return Result.success(order);
    }

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}/deliver")
    @Operation(summary = "发货", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> deliverOrder(@PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId) {
        orderService.deliverOrder(orderId);
        return Result.success("发货成功");
    }

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}/cancel")
    @Operation(summary = "取消订单", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> cancelOrderByAdmin(@PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId) {
        orderService.cancelOrderByAdmin(orderId);
        return Result.success("取消成功");
    }

    // ===== 补充接口（Feign 调用需要） =====

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/batch/deliver")
    @Operation(summary = "批量发货", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> batchDeliverOrders(@RequestBody List<String> orderIds) {
        for (String orderId : orderIds) {
            orderService.deliverOrder(orderId);
        }
        return Result.success("批量发货成功");
    }

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/batch/cancel")
    @Operation(summary = "批量取消订单", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> batchCancelOrders(@RequestBody List<String> orderIds) {
        for (String orderId : orderIds) {
            orderService.cancelOrderByAdmin(orderId);
        }
        return Result.success("批量取消成功");
    }

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}/remark")
    @Operation(summary = "修改订单备注", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> updateOrderRemark(
            @PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId,
            @RequestParam String remark) {
        orderService.updateOrderRemark(orderId, remark);
        return Result.success("备注修改成功");
    }

    @PutMapping("/admin/orders/{orderId}/receiver")
    @Operation(summary = "修改收货信息", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> updateReceiverInfo(
            @PathVariable @NotBlank String orderId,
            @RequestBody Map<String, String> receiverInfo) {
        orderService.updateReceiverInfo(orderId, receiverInfo);
        return Result.success("收货信息修改成功");
    }

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics")
    @Operation(summary = "获取订单统计信息", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Map<String, Object>> getOrderStatistics() {
        return Result.success(orderService.getOrderStatisticsMap());
    }

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics/status")
    @Operation(summary = "获取各状态订单数量统计", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Map<Integer, Long>> countByStatus() {
        return Result.success(orderService.countByStatusMap());
    }

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics/user/{userId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取用户订单统计", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Map<String, Object>> getUserOrderStatistics(@PathVariable Long userId) {
        return Result.success(orderService.getUserOrderStatistics(userId));
    }

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics/today")
    @Operation(summary = "获取今日订单统计", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Map<String, Object>> getTodayStatistics() {
        return Result.success(orderService.getTodayStatistics());
    }

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/statistics/range")
    @Operation(summary = "获取指定日期范围内的订单统计", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Map<String, Object>> getRangeStatistics(
            @RequestParam @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime startTime,
            @RequestParam @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime endTime) {
        return Result.success(orderService.getRangeStatistics(startTime, endTime));
    }

    @GetMapping("/admin/orders/export")
    @Operation(summary = "导出订单数据", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<String> exportOrders(
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime startTime,
            @RequestParam(required = false) @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime endTime,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "csv") String format) {
        return Result.success("导出功能待实现");
    }

    // ==================== 内部调用接口 ====================

package com.bookmall.order.dto;

import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.media.Schema;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.Data;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 订单响应DTO
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Data
@Builder
@Schema(description = "订单信息")
public class OrderDTO {

    @Schema(description = "订单号", example = "202609040001")
    private String orderId;

    @Schema(description = "用户ID", example = "1")
    private Long userId;

    @Schema(description = "用户名", example = "testuser")
    private String username;

    @Schema(description = "订单总金额", example = "158.00")
    private BigDecimal totalAmount;

    @Schema(description = "运费", example = "0.00")
    private BigDecimal shippingFee;

    @Schema(description = "订单状态：0-待付款 1-待发货 2-待收货 3-已完成 4-已取消", example = "0")
    private Integer orderStatus;

    @Schema(description = "订单状态描述", example = "待付款")
    private String statusDesc;

    @Schema(description = "支付方式：0-余额", example = "0")
    private Integer payType;

    @Schema(description = "收货人姓名", example = "张三")
    private String receiverName;

    @Schema(description = "收货人电话", example = "13800138000")
    private String receiverPhone;

    @Schema(description = "收货地址", example = "江西省南昌市青山湖区江西财经大学南区12栋301")
    private String receiverAddress;

    @Schema(description = "订单备注", example = "请尽快发货")
    private String remark;

    @Schema(description = "支付时间")
    private LocalDateTime payTime;

    @Schema(description = "发货时间")
    private LocalDateTime deliverTime;

    @Schema(description = "收货时间")
    private LocalDateTime receiveTime;

    @Schema(description = "创建时间")
    private LocalDateTime createTime;

    @Schema(description = "订单商品列表")
    private List<OrderItemDTO> items;
}

package com.bookmall.order.entity;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableField;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableId;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableName;
import lombok.Data;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * 订单实体类 - 对应 tb_order 表
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Data
@TableName("tb_order")
public class Order {

    @TableId("order_id")
    private String orderId;

    @TableField("user_id")
    private Long userId;

    @TableField("total_amount")
    private BigDecimal totalAmount;

    @TableField("shipping_fee")
    private BigDecimal shippingFee;

    @TableField("order_status")
    private Integer orderStatus;

    @TableField("pay_type")
    private Integer payType;

    @TableField("receiver_name")
    private String receiverName;

    @TableField("receiver_phone")
    private String receiverPhone;

    @TableField("receiver_address")
    private String receiverAddress;

    @TableField("remark")
    private String remark;

    @TableField("pay_time")
    private LocalDateTime payTime;

    @TableField("deliver_time")
    private LocalDateTime deliverTime;

    @TableField("receive_time")
    private LocalDateTime receiveTime;

    @TableField("create_time")
    private LocalDateTime createTime;

    // ==================== 非数据库字段 ====================

    @TableField(exist = false)
    private String username;

    @TableField(exist = false)
    private String statusDesc;

    @TableField(exist = false)
    private java.util.List<OrderItem> items;
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Order\src\main\java\com\bookmall\order\enums\OrderStatus.java ====================

package com.bookmall.order.enums;

import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Getter;

/**
 * 订单状态枚举
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Getter
@AllArgsConstructor
public enum OrderStatus {

    PENDING(0, "待付款"),
    PAID(1, "待发货"),
    SHIPPED(2, "待收货"),
    COMPLETED(3, "已完成"),
    CANCELLED(4, "已取消");

    private final int code;
    private final String desc;

    public static OrderStatus fromCode(int code) {
        for (OrderStatus status : values()) {
            if (status.code == code) {
                return status;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    public static String getDesc(int code) {
        OrderStatus status = fromCode(code);
        return status != null ? status.desc : "未知";
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Order\src\main\java\com\bookmall\order\handler\OrderExceptionHandler.java ====================

package com.bookmall.order.handler;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import feign.FeignException;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;

/**
 * 订单模块异常处理器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestControllerAdvice
public class OrderExceptionHandler {

    /**
     * Feign调用异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(FeignException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleFeignException(FeignException e) {
        log.error("Feign调用失败: status={}, message={}", e.status(), e.getMessage());
        if (e.status() == 404) {
            return Result.error("服务不可用，请稍后再试");
        }
        if (e.status() >= 500) {
            return Result.error("服务异常，请稍后再试");
        }
        return Result.error("请求失败，请稍后再试");
    }

    /**
     * 业务异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(BusinessException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleBusinessException(BusinessException e) {
        log.warn("业务异常: code={}, message={}", e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
        return Result.error(e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
    }

    /**
     * 系统异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public Result<Void> handleException(Exception e) {
        log.error("系统异常: ", e);
        return Result.error(ExceptionEnum.SYSTEM_ERROR.getMessage());
    }
}

package com.bookmall.order.mapper;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.bookmall.order.entity.OrderItem;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 订单详情数据访问层
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Mapper
public interface OrderItemMapper extends BaseMapper<OrderItem> {

    /**
     * 根据订单号查询订单详情列表
     */
    @Select("SELECT item_id, order_id, book_id, book_name, book_cover, " +
            "price, quantity, total_price " +
            "FROM tb_order_item WHERE order_id = #{orderId}")
    List<OrderItem> selectByOrderId(@Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 批量插入订单详情
     */
    int batchInsert(@Param("items") List<OrderItem> items);

    /**
     * 根据订单号删除订单详情
     */
    @Select("DELETE FROM tb_order_item WHERE order_id = #{orderId}")
    int deleteByOrderId(@Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 统计某个教材的销量
     */
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(quantity), 0) FROM tb_order_item " +
            "WHERE book_id = #{bookId}")
    Integer sumSalesByBookId(@Param("bookId") Long bookId);

    /**
     * 统计某个用户购买某教材的总数（用于评价检测）
     */
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(quantity), 0) FROM tb_order_item oi " +
            "INNER JOIN tb_order o ON oi.order_id = o.order_id " +
            "WHERE oi.book_id = #{bookId} AND o.user_id = #{userId} " +
            "AND o.order_status IN (1, 2, 3)")
    Integer countUserPurchased(@Param("userId") Long userId,
                               @Param("bookId") Long bookId);
}

package com.bookmall.order.mapper;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.order.entity.Order;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Update;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 订单数据访问层
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Mapper
public interface OrderMapper extends BaseMapper<Order> {

    /**
     * 分页查询用户订单列表
     */
    Page<Order> selectUserOrderPage(Page<Order> page,
                                    @Param("userId") Long userId,
                                    @Param("status") Integer status);

    /**
     * 分页查询所有订单（管理员）
     */
    Page<Order> selectAllOrderPage(Page<Order> page,
                                   @Param("userId") Long userId,
                                   @Param("status") Integer status,
                                   @Param("startTime") LocalDateTime startTime,
                                   @Param("endTime") LocalDateTime endTime);

    /**
     * 查询订单详情（含商品列表）
     */
    Order selectOrderDetail(@Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 根据用户ID和订单号查询
     */
    @Select("SELECT order_id, user_id, total_amount, shipping_fee, order_status, " +
            "pay_type, receiver_name, receiver_phone, receiver_address, remark, " +
            "pay_time, deliver_time, receive_time, create_time " +
            "FROM tb_order WHERE order_id = #{orderId} AND user_id = #{userId}")
    Order selectByOrderIdAndUserId(@Param("orderId") String orderId,
                                   @Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 更新订单状态
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_order SET order_status = #{status} WHERE order_id = #{orderId}")
    int updateStatus(@Param("orderId") String orderId, @Param("status") Integer status);

    /**
     * 支付订单
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_order SET order_status = 1, pay_time = NOW() " +
            "WHERE order_id = #{orderId} AND order_status = 0")
    int payOrder(@Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 发货
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_order SET order_status = 2, deliver_time = NOW() " +
            "WHERE order_id = #{orderId} AND order_status = 1")
    int deliverOrder(@Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 确认收货
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_order SET order_status = 3, receive_time = NOW() " +
            "WHERE order_id = #{orderId} AND order_status = 2")
    int receiveOrder(@Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 取消订单
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_order SET order_status = 4 " +
            "WHERE order_id = #{orderId} AND order_status IN (0, 1)")
    int cancelOrder(@Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 统计用户各状态订单数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT order_status, COUNT(*) AS count FROM tb_order " +
            "WHERE user_id = #{userId} GROUP BY order_status")
    List<java.util.Map<String, Object>> countByStatusForUser(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 统计所有订单（管理员）
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_order")
    Long countTotal();

    /**
     * 统计各状态订单数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT order_status, COUNT(*) AS count FROM tb_order GROUP BY order_status")
    List<java.util.Map<String, Object>> countByStatus();

    /**
     * 统计总销售额
     */
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(total_amount), 0) FROM tb_order " +
            "WHERE order_status IN (1, 2, 3)")
    BigDecimal sumTotalSales();

    /**
     * 统计今日订单数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_order WHERE DATE(create_time) = CURDATE()")
    Long countTodayOrders();

    /**
     * 统计今日销售额
     */
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(total_amount), 0) FROM tb_order " +
            "WHERE DATE(create_time) = CURDATE() AND order_status IN (1, 2, 3)")
    BigDecimal sumTodaySales();

    /**
     * 统计指定时间段订单数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_order " +
            "WHERE create_time BETWEEN #{startTime} AND #{endTime}")
    Long countByDateRange(@Param("startTime") LocalDateTime startTime,
                          @Param("endTime") LocalDateTime endTime);

    /**
     * 统计指定时间段销售额
     */
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(total_amount), 0) FROM tb_order " +
            "WHERE create_time BETWEEN #{startTime} AND #{endTime} " +
            "AND order_status IN (1, 2, 3)")
    BigDecimal sumSalesByDateRange(@Param("startTime") LocalDateTime startTime,
                                   @Param("endTime") LocalDateTime endTime);

    /**
     * 获取超时未支付订单（30分钟前创建且未支付）
     */
    @Select("SELECT order_id FROM tb_order " +
            "WHERE order_status = 0 AND create_time < DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 30 MINUTE)")
    List<String> findTimeoutPendingOrders();
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Order\src\main\java\com\bookmall\order\service\impl\OrderServiceImpl.java ====================

package com.bookmall.order.service.impl;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.RedisUtil;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.SnowflakeIdWorker;
import com.bookmall.order.dto.*;
import com.bookmall.order.entity.Order;
import com.bookmall.order.entity.OrderItem;
import com.bookmall.order.enums.OrderStatus;
import com.bookmall.order.feign.*;
import com.bookmall.order.mapper.OrderItemMapper;
import com.bookmall.order.mapper.OrderMapper;
import com.bookmall.order.service.OrderService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * 订单服务实现类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class OrderServiceImpl implements OrderService {

    private final OrderMapper orderMapper;
    private final OrderItemMapper orderItemMapper;
    private final SnowflakeIdWorker snowflakeIdWorker;
    private final RedisUtil redisUtil;

    // Feign Clients
    private final BookFeignClient bookFeignClient;
    private final UserFeignClient userFeignClient;
    private final CartFeignClient cartFeignClient;
    private final AddressFeignClient addressFeignClient;

    // Redis Key前缀 - 订单锁
    private static final String REDIS_ORDER_LOCK_KEY = Constants.REDIS_ORDER_LOCK;

    // ==================== 用户端 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDTO createOrderFromCart(Long userId, OrderCreateDTO request) {
        // 1. 获取Redis分布式锁（防止重复提交）
        String lockKey = REDIS_ORDER_LOCK_KEY + userId;
        Boolean locked = redisUtil.setIfAbsent(lockKey, "1", 10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        if (locked == null || !locked) {
            throw new BusinessException("请勿重复提交订单");
        }

        try {
            // 2. 验证用户
            validateUser(userId);

            // 3. 获取收货地址
            Map<String, Object> address = getAddress(userId, request.getAddressId());

            // 4. 获取购物车商品
            List<Map<String, Object>> cartItems = getCartItems(userId, request.getCartIds());

            if (cartItems == null || cartItems.isEmpty()) {
                throw new BusinessException("购物车为空，请选择商品");
            }

            // 5. 创建订单
            return createOrderFromItems(userId, cartItems, address, request.getRemark());
        } finally {
            // 释放锁
            redisUtil.delete(lockKey);
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDTO createOrderDirect(Long userId, Long bookId, Integer quantity, Long addressId, String remark) {
        // 1. 验证用户
        validateUser(userId);

        // 2. 验证教材
        Result<Object> bookResult = bookFeignClient.getBookDetail(bookId);
        if (bookResult == null || !bookResult.isSuccess() || bookResult.getData() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.BOOK_NOT_EXIST);
        }
        Map<String, Object> book = (Map<String, Object>) bookResult.getData();

        // 验证库存
        Integer stock = (Integer) book.get("stock");
        if (stock == null || stock < quantity) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.STOCK_INSUFFICIENT);
        }

        // 验证是否上架
        Integer status = (Integer) book.get("status");
        if (status == null || status != Constants.BOOK_STATUS_ON) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.BOOK_OFF_SALE);
        }

        // 3. 获取收货地址
        Map<String, Object> address = getAddress(userId, addressId);

        // 4. 构建购物车项格式（Java 8 兼容）
        List<Map<String, Object>> cartItems = new ArrayList<>();
        Map<String, Object> item = new HashMap<>();
        item.put("bookId", bookId);
        item.put("bookName", book.get("bookName"));
        item.put("bookCover", book.get("coverImage"));
        item.put("price", book.get("price"));
        item.put("quantity", quantity);
        item.put("stock", stock);
        cartItems.add(item);

        // 5. 创建订单
        return createOrderFromItems(userId, cartItems, address, remark);
    }

    /**
     * 从商品列表创建订单
     */
    private OrderDTO createOrderFromItems(Long userId, List<Map<String, Object>> items,
                                          Map<String, Object> address, String remark) {
        // 1. 生成订单号
        String orderId = generateOrderId();

        // 2. 计算总金额
        BigDecimal totalAmount = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        List<OrderItem> orderItems = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Map<String, Object> item : items) {
            Long bookId = ((Number) item.get("bookId")).longValue();
            Integer quantity = (Integer) item.get("quantity");
            BigDecimal price = new BigDecimal(item.get("price").toString());
            BigDecimal subtotal = price.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(quantity));
            totalAmount = totalAmount.add(subtotal);

            OrderItem orderItem = new OrderItem();
            orderItem.setOrderId(orderId);
            orderItem.setBookId(bookId);
            orderItem.setBookName((String) item.get("bookName"));
            orderItem.setBookCover((String) item.get("bookCover"));
            orderItem.setPrice(price);
            orderItem.setQuantity(quantity);
            orderItem.setTotalPrice(subtotal);
            orderItems.add(orderItem);

            // 扣减库存
            Result<Void> deductResult = bookFeignClient.deductStock(bookId, quantity);
            if (deductResult == null || !deductResult.isSuccess()) {
                throw new BusinessException("库存扣减失败：" +
                        (deductResult != null ? deductResult.getMessage() : "服务不可用"));
            }

            // 增加销量
            bookFeignClient.addSales(bookId, quantity);
        }

        // 3. 创建订单
        Order order = new Order();
        order.setOrderId(orderId);
        order.setUserId(userId);
        order.setTotalAmount(totalAmount);
        order.setShippingFee(BigDecimal.ZERO);
        order.setOrderStatus(OrderStatus.PENDING.getCode());
        order.setPayType(Constants.PAY_TYPE_BALANCE);
        order.setReceiverName((String) address.get("receiverName"));
        order.setReceiverPhone((String) address.get("receiverPhone"));
        order.setReceiverAddress((String) address.get("fullAddress"));
        order.setRemark(remark);

        orderMapper.insert(order);

        // 4. 插入订单详情
        orderItemMapper.batchInsert(orderItems);

        // 5. 清空购物车（从购物车创建时）
        List<Long> cartIds = items.stream()
                .map(item -> (Long) item.get("cartId"))
                .filter(id -> id != null)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        if (!cartIds.isEmpty()) {
            cartFeignClient.batchDeleteCartItems(userId, cartIds);
        }

        log.info("订单创建成功: orderId={}, userId={}, totalAmount={}",
                orderId, userId, totalAmount);

        return convertToDTO(order, orderItems);
    }

    @Override
    public Page<OrderDTO> getUserOrders(Long userId, Integer status, Integer pageNum, Integer pageSize) {
        Page<Order> page = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize);
        Page<Order> orderPage = orderMapper.selectUserOrderPage(page, userId, status);

        Page<OrderDTO> dtoPage = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize, orderPage.getTotal());
        List<OrderDTO> dtoList = orderPage.getRecords().stream()
                .map(order -> {
                    List<OrderItem> items = orderItemMapper.selectByOrderId(order.getOrderId());
                    return convertToDTO(order, items);
                })
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        dtoPage.setRecords(dtoList);

        return dtoPage;
    }

    @Override
    public OrderDTO getOrderDetail(Long userId, String orderId) {
        // 验证订单是否属于该用户
        if (!belongsToUser(orderId, userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        Order order = orderMapper.selectOrderDetail(orderId);
        if (order == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        List<OrderItem> items = orderItemMapper.selectByOrderId(orderId);
        return convertToDTO(order, items);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void payOrder(Long userId, String orderId) {
        // 1. 验证订单
        if (!belongsToUser(orderId, userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        Order order = orderMapper.selectByOrderIdAndUserId(orderId, userId);
        if (order == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        // 2. 验证订单状态
        if (order.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING.getCode()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_CANNOT_PAY);
        }

        // 3. 扣减余额
        Result<Void> balanceResult = userFeignClient.deductBalance(userId, order.getTotalAmount());
        if (balanceResult == null || !balanceResult.isSuccess()) {
            throw new BusinessException(balanceResult != null ? balanceResult.getMessage() : "余额扣减失败");
        }

        // 4. 更新订单状态
        int result = orderMapper.payOrder(orderId);
        if (result <= 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PAY_ERROR);
        }

        log.info("订单支付成功: orderId={}, userId={}, amount={}",
                orderId, userId, order.getTotalAmount());
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void cancelOrder(Long userId, String orderId) {
        if (!belongsToUser(orderId, userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        Order order = orderMapper.selectByOrderIdAndUserId(orderId, userId);
        if (order == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        // 只有待付款和待发货可以取消
        if (order.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING.getCode() &&
                order.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.PAID.getCode()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_CANNOT_CANCEL);
        }

        // 如果已支付，需要退款
        if (order.getOrderStatus() == OrderStatus.PAID.getCode()) {
            // 退还余额
            Result<Void> refundResult = userFeignClient.addBalance(userId, order.getTotalAmount());
            if (refundResult == null || !refundResult.isSuccess()) {
                log.warn("退款失败: orderId={}, userId={}", orderId, userId);
                throw new BusinessException("退款失败，请稍后重试");
            }
        }

        // 更新状态
        int result = orderMapper.cancelOrder(orderId);
        if (result <= 0) {
            throw new BusinessException("取消订单失败");
        }

        log.info("订单取消成功: orderId={}, userId={}", orderId, userId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void confirmReceive(Long userId, String orderId) {
        if (!belongsToUser(orderId, userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        Order order = orderMapper.selectByOrderIdAndUserId(orderId, userId);
        if (order == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        if (order.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.SHIPPED.getCode()) {
            throw new BusinessException("订单状态不正确，无法确认收货");
        }

        int result = orderMapper.receiveOrder(orderId);
        if (result <= 0) {
            throw new BusinessException("确认收货失败");
        }

        log.info("确认收货成功: orderId={}, userId={}", orderId, userId);
    }

    // ==================== 管理员端 ====================

    @Override
    public Page<OrderDTO> getAllOrders(Long userId, Integer status, LocalDateTime startTime,
                                       LocalDateTime endTime, Integer pageNum, Integer pageSize) {
        Page<Order> page = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize);
        Page<Order> orderPage = orderMapper.selectAllOrderPage(page, userId, status, startTime, endTime);

        Page<OrderDTO> dtoPage = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize, orderPage.getTotal());
        List<OrderDTO> dtoList = orderPage.getRecords().stream()
                .map(order -> {
                    List<OrderItem> items = orderItemMapper.selectByOrderId(order.getOrderId());
                    return convertToDTO(order, items);
                })
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        dtoPage.setRecords(dtoList);

        return dtoPage;
    }

    /**
     * 获取订单详情（管理员，不校验 userId）
     */
    @Override
    public OrderDTO getOrderDetailByAdmin(String orderId) {
        Order order = orderMapper.selectOrderDetail(orderId);
        if (order == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }
        List<OrderItem> items = orderItemMapper.selectByOrderId(orderId);
        return convertToDTO(order, items);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deliverOrder(String orderId) {
        Order order = getOrderEntity(orderId);
        if (order.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.PAID.getCode()) {
            throw new BusinessException("订单状态不正确，无法发货");
        }

        int result = orderMapper.deliverOrder(orderId);
        if (result <= 0) {
            throw new BusinessException("发货失败");
        }

        log.info("发货成功: orderId={}", orderId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void cancelOrderByAdmin(String orderId) {
        Order order = getOrderEntity(orderId);
        if (order.getOrderStatus() == OrderStatus.COMPLETED.getCode()) {
            throw new BusinessException("已完成订单无法取消");
        }

        // 如果已支付，需要退款
        if (order.getOrderStatus() == OrderStatus.PAID.getCode() ||
                order.getOrderStatus() == OrderStatus.SHIPPED.getCode()) {
            Result<Void> refundResult = userFeignClient.addBalance(order.getUserId(), order.getTotalAmount());
            if (refundResult == null || !refundResult.isSuccess()) {
                log.warn("管理员退款失败: orderId={}", orderId);
                throw new BusinessException("退款失败，请稍后重试");
            }
        }

        int result = orderMapper.cancelOrder(orderId);
        if (result <= 0) {
            throw new BusinessException("取消订单失败");
        }

        log.info("管理员取消订单成功: orderId={}", orderId);
    }

    // ==================== 统计 ====================

    @Override
    public OrderStatisticsVO getOrderStatistics() {
        Long totalOrders = orderMapper.countTotal();
        List<Map<String, Object>> statusCounts = orderMapper.countByStatus();

        Long pending = 0L, paid = 0L, shipped = 0L, completed = 0L, cancelled = 0L;
        for (Map<String, Object> map : statusCounts) {
            Integer status = (Integer) map.get("order_status");
            Long count = (Long) map.get("count");
            if (status != null && count != null) {
                switch (status) {
                    case 0: pending = count; break;
                    case 1: paid = count; break;
                    case 2: shipped = count; break;
                    case 3: completed = count; break;
                    case 4: cancelled = count; break;
                    default: break;
                }
            }
        }

        BigDecimal totalSales = orderMapper.sumTotalSales();
        if (totalSales == null) {
            totalSales = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        }

        Long todayOrders = orderMapper.countTodayOrders();
        if (todayOrders == null) {
            todayOrders = 0L;
        }

        BigDecimal todaySales = orderMapper.sumTodaySales();
        if (todaySales == null) {
            todaySales = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        }

        Long paidCompleted = paid + shipped + completed;
        BigDecimal avgAmount = paidCompleted > 0 ?
                totalSales.divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(paidCompleted), 2, BigDecimal.ROUND_HALF_UP) :
                BigDecimal.ZERO;

        return OrderStatisticsVO.builder()
                .totalOrders(totalOrders != null ? totalOrders : 0L)
                .pendingOrders(pending)
                .paidOrders(paid)
                .shippedOrders(shipped)
                .completedOrders(completed)
                .cancelledOrders(cancelled)
                .totalSales(totalSales)
                .avgAmount(avgAmount)
                .todayOrders(todayOrders)
                .todaySales(todaySales)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    public List<Map<String, Object>> countUserOrdersByStatus(Long userId) {
        List<Map<String, Object>> result = orderMapper.countByStatusForUser(userId);
        return result != null ? result : new ArrayList<>();
    }

    // ==================== 定时任务 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void cancelTimeoutOrders() {
        List<String> timeoutOrderIds = orderMapper.findTimeoutPendingOrders();
        if (timeoutOrderIds == null || timeoutOrderIds.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        int successCount = 0;
        for (String orderId : timeoutOrderIds) {
            try {
                int result = orderMapper.cancelOrder(orderId);
                if (result > 0) {
                    successCount++;
                    log.info("定时任务取消超时订单: orderId={}", orderId);
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("取消超时订单失败: orderId={}, error={}", orderId, e.getMessage(), e);
            }
        }

        log.info("定时任务取消超时订单完成，共处理 {} 个订单，成功取消 {} 个",
                timeoutOrderIds.size(), successCount);
    }

    // ==================== 内部调用 ====================

    @Override
    public Order getOrderEntity(String orderId) {
        Order order = orderMapper.selectById(orderId);
        if (order == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
        }
        return order;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean belongsToUser(String orderId, Long userId) {
        Order order = orderMapper.selectById(orderId);
        return order != null && order.getUserId().equals(userId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void paySuccess(String orderId) {
        // 1. 获取订单
        Order order = getOrderEntity(orderId);

        // 2. 验证订单状态（只有待付款才能支付成功）
        if (order.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING.getCode()) {
            throw new BusinessException("订单状态不正确，无法支付");
        }

        // 3. 更新订单状态为已支付/待发货
        int result = orderMapper.payOrder(orderId);
        if (result <= 0) {
            throw new BusinessException("订单支付状态更新失败");
        }

        log.info("订单支付成功（回调）: orderId={}, userId={}, amount={}",
                orderId, order.getUserId(), order.getTotalAmount());
    }

    // ==================== 私有方法 ====================

    /**
     * 验证用户
     */
    private void validateUser(Long userId) {
        Result<Boolean> result = userFeignClient.checkUserExists(userId);
        if (result == null || !result.isSuccess() || result.getData() == null || !result.getData()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.USER_NOT_EXIST);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 获取收货地址
     */
    private Map<String, Object> getAddress(Long userId, Long addressId) {
        Result<Object> result = addressFeignClient.getAddressById(userId, addressId);
        if (result == null || !result.isSuccess() || result.getData() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException("收货地址不存在");
        }
        return (Map<String, Object>) result.getData();
    }

    /**
     * 获取购物车商品列表
     */
    private List<Map<String, Object>> getCartItems(Long userId, List<Long> cartIds) {
        Result<List<Object>> result = cartFeignClient.getCartList(userId);
        if (result == null || !result.isSuccess() || result.getData() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException("获取购物车失败");
        }

        List<Object> allItems = result.getData();
        if (allItems.isEmpty()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.CART_EMPTY);
        }

        List<Map<String, Object>> items = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Object obj : allItems) {
            Map<String, Object> item = (Map<String, Object>) obj;
            Long cartId = ((Number) item.get("cartId")).longValue();

            // 如果指定了cartIds，只取选中的
            if (cartIds != null && !cartIds.isEmpty()) {
                if (!cartIds.contains(cartId)) {
                    continue;
                }
            }

            // 验证库存
            Integer stock = (Integer) item.get("stock");
            Integer quantity = (Integer) item.get("quantity");
            if (stock == null || quantity == null || stock < quantity) {
                throw new BusinessException("商品【" + item.get("bookName") + "】库存不足");
            }

            // 验证是否上架
            Boolean onSale = (Boolean) item.get("onSale");
            if (onSale == null || !onSale) {
                throw new BusinessException("商品【" + item.get("bookName") + "】已下架");
            }

            // 添加cartId到item中，用于后续删除
            item.put("cartId", cartId);
            items.add(item);
        }

        if (items.isEmpty()) {
            throw new BusinessException("没有选中的商品");
        }

        return items;
    }

    /**
     * 生成订单号
     * 格式：yyyyMMddHHmmss + 6位雪花ID取模
     */
    private String generateOrderId() {
        String timestamp = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMddHHmmss"));
        String seq = String.format("%06d", snowflakeIdWorker.nextId() % 1000000);
        return timestamp + seq;
    }

    /**
     * 实体转DTO
     */
    private OrderDTO convertToDTO(Order order, List<OrderItem> items) {
        if (order == null) {
            return null;
        }

        List<OrderItemDTO> itemDTOs = items != null ? items.stream()
                .map(this::convertItemToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList()) : new ArrayList<>();

        return OrderDTO.builder()
                .orderId(order.getOrderId())
                .userId(order.getUserId())
                .username(order.getUsername())
                .totalAmount(order.getTotalAmount())
                .shippingFee(order.getShippingFee())
                .orderStatus(order.getOrderStatus())
                .statusDesc(order.getStatusDesc() != null ? order.getStatusDesc() :
                        OrderStatus.getDesc(order.getOrderStatus()))
                .payType(order.getPayType())
                .receiverName(order.getReceiverName())
                .receiverPhone(order.getReceiverPhone())
                .receiverAddress(order.getReceiverAddress())
                .remark(order.getRemark())
                .payTime(order.getPayTime())
                .deliverTime(order.getDeliverTime())
                .receiveTime(order.getReceiveTime())
                .createTime(order.getCreateTime())
                .items(itemDTOs)
                .build();
    }

    /**
     * 订单详情实体转DTO
     */
    private OrderItemDTO convertItemToDTO(OrderItem item) {
        if (item == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return OrderItemDTO.builder()
                .itemId(item.getItemId())
                .orderId(item.getOrderId())
                .bookId(item.getBookId())
                .bookName(item.getBookName())
                .bookCover(item.getBookCover())
                .price(item.getPrice())
                .quantity(item.getQuantity())
                .totalPrice(item.getTotalPrice())
                .build();
    }

    // ==================== 管理员补充操作 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updateOrderRemark(String orderId, String remark) {
        Order order = getOrderEntity(orderId);
        order.setRemark(remark);
        orderMapper.updateById(order);
        log.info("订单备注修改成功: orderId={}, remark={}", orderId, remark);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updateReceiverInfo(String orderId, Map<String, String> receiverInfo) {
        Order order = getOrderEntity(orderId);
        if (receiverInfo.containsKey("receiverName")) {
            order.setReceiverName(receiverInfo.get("receiverName"));
        }
        if (receiverInfo.containsKey("receiverPhone")) {
            order.setReceiverPhone(receiverInfo.get("receiverPhone"));
        }
        if (receiverInfo.containsKey("receiverAddress")) {
            order.setReceiverAddress(receiverInfo.get("receiverAddress"));
        }
        orderMapper.updateById(order);
        log.info("订单收货信息修改成功: orderId={}", orderId);
    }

    // ==================== Feign 调用需要的统计数据 ====================

    @Override
    public Map<String, Object> getOrderStatisticsMap() {
        Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();

        Long totalOrders = orderMapper.countTotal();
        List<Map<String, Object>> statusCounts = orderMapper.countByStatus();

        long pending = 0, paid = 0, shipped = 0, completed = 0, cancelled = 0;
        for (Map<String, Object> map : statusCounts) {
            Integer status = (Integer) map.get("order_status");
            Long count = (Long) map.get("count");
            if (status != null && count != null) {
                switch (status) {
                    case 0: pending = count; break;
                    case 1: paid = count; break;
                    case 2: shipped = count; break;
                    case 3: completed = count; break;
                    case 4: cancelled = count; break;
                    default: break;
                }
            }
        }

        BigDecimal totalSales = orderMapper.sumTotalSales();
        if (totalSales == null) {
            totalSales = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        }

        Long todayOrders = orderMapper.countTodayOrders();
        if (todayOrders == null) {
            todayOrders = 0L;
        }

        BigDecimal todaySales = orderMapper.sumTodaySales();
        if (todaySales == null) {
            todaySales = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        }

        result.put("totalOrders", totalOrders != null ? totalOrders : 0L);
        result.put("pendingOrders", pending);
        result.put("paidOrders", paid);
        result.put("shippedOrders", shipped);
        result.put("completedOrders", completed);
        result.put("cancelledOrders", cancelled);
        result.put("totalSales", totalSales);
        result.put("todayOrders", todayOrders);
        result.put("todaySales", todaySales);
        return result;
    }

    @Override
    public Map<Integer, Long> countByStatusMap() {
        Map<Integer, Long> result = new HashMap<>();
        List<Map<String, Object>> statusCounts = orderMapper.countByStatus();
        for (Map<String, Object> map : statusCounts) {
            Integer status = (Integer) map.get("order_status");
            Long count = (Long) map.get("count");
            if (status != null && count != null) {
                result.put(status, count);
            }
        }
        // 保证所有状态都有值
        for (int i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
            result.putIfAbsent(i, 0L);
        }
        return result;
    }

    @Override
    public Map<String, Object> getUserOrderStatistics(Long userId) {
        Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
        List<Map<String, Object>> counts = orderMapper.countByStatusForUser(userId);

        long pending = 0, paid = 0, shipped = 0, completed = 0, cancelled = 0;
        for (Map<String, Object> map : counts) {
            Integer status = (Integer) map.get("order_status");
            Long count = (Long) map.get("count");
            if (status != null && count != null) {
                switch (status) {
                    case 0: pending = count; break;
                    case 1: paid = count; break;
                    case 2: shipped = count; break;
                    case 3: completed = count; break;
                    case 4: cancelled = count; break;
                    default: break;
                }
            }
        }

        result.put("userId", userId);
        result.put("pendingOrders", pending);
        result.put("paidOrders", paid);
        result.put("shippedOrders", shipped);
        result.put("completedOrders", completed);
        result.put("cancelledOrders", cancelled);
        result.put("totalOrders", pending + paid + shipped + completed + cancelled);
        return result;
    }

    @Override
    public Map<String, Object> getTodayStatistics() {
        Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
        Long todayOrders = orderMapper.countTodayOrders();
        BigDecimal todaySales = orderMapper.sumTodaySales();
        result.put("todayOrders", todayOrders != null ? todayOrders : 0L);
        result.put("todaySales", todaySales != null ? todaySales : BigDecimal.ZERO);
        return result;
    }

    @Override
    public Map<String, Object> getRangeStatistics(LocalDateTime startTime, LocalDateTime endTime) {
        Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
        Long totalOrders = orderMapper.countByDateRange(startTime, endTime);
        BigDecimal totalSales = orderMapper.sumSalesByDateRange(startTime, endTime);
        result.put("startTime", startTime);
        result.put("endTime", endTime);
        result.put("totalOrders", totalOrders != null ? totalOrders : 0L);
        result.put("totalSales", totalSales != null ? totalSales : BigDecimal.ZERO);
        return result;
    }

}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Order\src\main\resources\mapper\OrderItemMapper.xml ====================

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">
<mapper namespace="com.bookmall.order.mapper.OrderItemMapper">

    <!-- 结果映射 -->
    <resultMap id="BaseResultMap" type="com.bookmall.order.entity.OrderItem">
        <id column="item_id" property="itemId"/>
        <result column="order_id" property="orderId"/>
        <result column="book_id" property="bookId"/>
        <result column="book_name" property="bookName"/>
        <result column="book_cover" property="bookCover"/>
        <result column="price" property="price"/>
        <result column="quantity" property="quantity"/>
        <result column="total_price" property="totalPrice"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 通用查询字段（带表别名） -->
    <sql id="Base_Column_List">
        oi.item_id,
        oi.order_id,
        oi.book_id,
        oi.book_name,
        oi.book_cover,
        oi.price,
        oi.quantity,
        oi.total_price
    </sql>

    <!-- 批量插入订单详情 -->
    <insert id="batchInsert">
        INSERT INTO tb_order_item (order_id, book_id, book_name, book_cover, price, quantity, total_price)
        VALUES
        <foreach collection="items" item="item" separator=",">
            (#{item.orderId}, #{item.bookId}, #{item.bookName}, #{item.bookCover},
            #{item.price}, #{item.quantity}, #{item.totalPrice})
        </foreach>
    </insert>

    <!-- 根据订单ID查询订单详情列表 -->
    <select id="selectByOrderId" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_order_item oi
        WHERE oi.order_id = #{orderId}
    </select>

    <!-- 根据订单ID列表批量查询 -->
    <select id="selectByOrderIds" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_order_item oi
        WHERE oi.order_id IN
        <foreach collection="orderIds" item="orderId" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{orderId}
        </foreach>
    </select>

    <!-- 根据订单ID删除订单详情 -->
    <delete id="deleteByOrderId">
        DELETE FROM tb_order_item
        WHERE order_id = #{orderId}
    </delete>

</mapper>

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">
<mapper namespace="com.bookmall.order.mapper.OrderMapper">

    <!-- 结果映射 -->
    <resultMap id="BaseResultMap" type="com.bookmall.order.entity.Order">
        <id column="order_id" property="orderId"/>
        <result column="user_id" property="userId"/>
        <result column="total_amount" property="totalAmount"/>
        <result column="shipping_fee" property="shippingFee"/>
        <result column="order_status" property="orderStatus"/>
        <result column="pay_type" property="payType"/>
        <result column="receiver_name" property="receiverName"/>
        <result column="receiver_phone" property="receiverPhone"/>
        <result column="receiver_address" property="receiverAddress"/>
        <result column="remark" property="remark"/>
        <result column="pay_time" property="payTime"/>
        <result column="deliver_time" property="deliverTime"/>
        <result column="receive_time" property="receiveTime"/>
        <result column="create_time" property="createTime"/>
        <result column="username" property="username"/>
        <result column="status_desc" property="statusDesc"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 通用查询字段（带表别名） -->
    <sql id="Base_Column_List">
        o.order_id,
        o.user_id,
        o.total_amount,
        o.shipping_fee,
        o.order_status,
        o.pay_type,
        o.receiver_name,
        o.receiver_phone,
        o.receiver_address,
        o.remark,
        o.pay_time,
        o.deliver_time,
        o.receive_time,
        o.create_time
    </sql>

    <!-- 分页查询用户订单列表 -->
    <select id="selectUserOrderPage" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>,
        u.username,
        CASE o.order_status
        WHEN 0 THEN '待付款'
        WHEN 1 THEN '待发货'
        WHEN 2 THEN '待收货'
        WHEN 3 THEN '已完成'
        WHEN 4 THEN '已取消'
        ELSE '未知'
        END AS status_desc
        FROM tb_order o
        LEFT JOIN tb_user u ON o.user_id = u.user_id
        WHERE o.user_id = #{userId}
        <if test="status != null">
            AND o.order_status = #{status}
        </if>
        ORDER BY o.create_time DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 分页查询所有订单（管理员） -->
    <select id="selectAllOrderPage" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>,
        u.username,
        CASE o.order_status
        WHEN 0 THEN '待付款'
        WHEN 1 THEN '待发货'
        WHEN 2 THEN '待收货'
        WHEN 3 THEN '已完成'
        WHEN 4 THEN '已取消'
        ELSE '未知'
        END AS status_desc
        FROM tb_order o
        LEFT JOIN tb_user u ON o.user_id = u.user_id
        <where>
            <if test="userId != null">
                AND o.user_id = #{userId}
            </if>
            <if test="status != null">
                AND o.order_status = #{status}
            </if>
            <if test="startTime != null">
                AND o.create_time >= #{startTime}
            </if>
            <if test="endTime != null">
                AND o.create_time &lt;= #{endTime}
            </if>
        </where>
        ORDER BY o.create_time DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 查询订单详情（含商品列表） -->
    <select id="selectOrderDetail" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>,
        u.username,
        CASE o.order_status
        WHEN 0 THEN '待付款'
        WHEN 1 THEN '待发货'
        WHEN 2 THEN '待收货'
        WHEN 3 THEN '已完成'
        WHEN 4 THEN '已取消'
        ELSE '未知'
        END AS status_desc
        FROM tb_order o
        LEFT JOIN tb_user u ON o.user_id = u.user_id
        WHERE o.order_id = #{orderId}
    </select>

</mapper>

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Payment\src\main\java\com\bookmall\payment\config\PaymentConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.payment.config;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.DbType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.MybatisPlusInterceptor;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.inner.PaginationInnerInterceptor;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

/**
 * Payment模块配置类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class PaymentConfig {

    /**
     * MyBatis-Plus 分页插件
     */
    @Bean
    public MybatisPlusInterceptor mybatisPlusInterceptor() {
        MybatisPlusInterceptor interceptor = new MybatisPlusInterceptor();
        interceptor.addInnerInterceptor(new PaginationInnerInterceptor(DbType.MYSQL));
        return interceptor;
    }
}

package com.bookmall.payment.controller;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.PageResult;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.payment.dto.PaymentCallbackDTO;
import com.bookmall.payment.dto.PaymentCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.payment.dto.PaymentDTO;
import com.bookmall.payment.dto.PaymentStatisticsVO;
import com.bookmall.payment.service.PaymentService;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Parameter;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.format.annotation.DateTimeFormat;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.validation.Valid;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * 支付控制器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/payment")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "支付接口", description = "支付管理")
public class PaymentController {

    private final PaymentService paymentService;

    // ==================== 用户端 ====================

    /**
     * 创建支付
     */
    @PostMapping
    @Operation(summary = "创建支付")
    public Result<PaymentDTO> createPayment(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId,
            @Valid @RequestBody PaymentCreateDTO request) {
        PaymentDTO payment = paymentService.createPayment(userId, request);
        return Result.success("支付创建成功", payment);
    }

    /**
     * 获取支付详情
     */
    @GetMapping("/{paymentNo}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取支付详情")
    public Result<PaymentDTO> getPaymentDetail(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId,
            @Parameter(description = "支付单号", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotBlank String paymentNo) {
        PaymentDTO payment = paymentService.getPaymentDetail(userId, paymentNo);
        return Result.success(payment);
    }

    /**
     * 获取用户支付记录列表
     */
    @GetMapping
    @Operation(summary = "获取用户支付记录列表")
    public Result<PageResult<PaymentDTO>> getUserPayments(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {
        Page<PaymentDTO> page = paymentService.getUserPayments(userId, status, pageNum, pageSize);
        return Result.success(PageResult.of(page));
    }

    /**
     * 取消支付
     */
    @PutMapping("/{paymentNo}/cancel")
    @Operation(summary = "取消支付")
    public Result<Void> cancelPayment(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId,
            @Parameter(description = "支付单号", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotBlank String paymentNo) {
        paymentService.cancelPayment(userId, paymentNo);
        return Result.success("取消成功");
    }

    /**
     * 支付回调接口（外部系统调用）
     */
    @PostMapping("/callback")
    @Operation(summary = "支付回调", description = "第三方支付回调接口")
    public Result<Void> paymentCallback(@Valid @RequestBody PaymentCallbackDTO callback) {
        paymentService.handlePaymentCallback(callback);
        return Result.success("回调处理成功");
    }

    // ==================== 管理员端 ====================

    /**
     * 获取所有支付记录（管理员）
     */
    @GetMapping("/admin/list")
    @Operation(summary = "获取所有支付记录", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<PageResult<PaymentDTO>> getAllPayments(
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long userId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime startTime,
            @RequestParam(required = false) @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") LocalDateTime endTime,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {
        Page<PaymentDTO> page = paymentService.getAllPayments(userId, status, startTime, endTime, pageNum, pageSize);
        return Result.success(PageResult.of(page));
    }

    /**
     * 退款（管理员）
     */
    @PostMapping("/admin/{paymentNo}/refund")
    @Operation(summary = "退款", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> refund(
            @Parameter(description = "支付单号", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotBlank String paymentNo,
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId) {
        paymentService.refund(userId, paymentNo);
        return Result.success("退款成功");
    }

    /**
     * 获取支付统计信息（管理员）
     */
    @GetMapping("/admin/statistics")
    @Operation(summary = "获取支付统计信息", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<PaymentStatisticsVO> getPaymentStatistics() {
        PaymentStatisticsVO statistics = paymentService.getPaymentStatistics();
        return Result.success(statistics);
    }
}

package com.bookmall.payment.entity;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.*;
import lombok.Data;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * 支付实体类 - 对应 tb_payment 表
 * 注意：需要先在数据库中创建此表
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Data
@TableName("tb_payment")
public class Payment {

    @TableId(value = "payment_id", type = IdType.AUTO)
    private Long paymentId;

    @TableField("payment_no")
    private String paymentNo;

    @TableField("order_id")
    private String orderId;

    @TableField("user_id")
    private Long userId;

    @TableField("amount")
    private BigDecimal amount;

    @TableField("pay_type")
    private Integer payType;

    @TableField("status")
    private Integer status;

    @TableField("transaction_id")
    private String transactionId;

    @TableField("callback_data")
    private String callbackData;

    @TableField("pay_time")
    private LocalDateTime payTime;

    @TableField("expire_time")
    private LocalDateTime expireTime;

    @TableField(value = "create_time", fill = FieldFill.INSERT)
    private LocalDateTime createTime;

    @TableField(value = "update_time", fill = FieldFill.INSERT_UPDATE)
    private LocalDateTime updateTime;

    // ==================== 非数据库字段 ====================

    @TableField(exist = false)
    private String statusDesc;

    @TableField(exist = false)
    private String payTypeDesc;

    @TableField(exist = false)
    private String username;
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Payment\src\main\java\com\bookmall\payment\enums\PaymentStatus.java ====================

package com.bookmall.payment.enums;

import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Getter;

/**
 * 支付状态枚举
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Getter
@AllArgsConstructor
public enum PaymentStatus {

    PENDING(0, "待支付"),
    PROCESSING(1, "处理中"),
    SUCCESS(2, "支付成功"),
    FAILED(3, "支付失败"),
    CANCELLED(4, "已取消"),
    REFUNDING(5, "退款中"),
    REFUNDED(6, "已退款");

    private final int code;
    private final String desc;

    public static PaymentStatus fromCode(int code) {
        for (PaymentStatus status : values()) {
            if (status.code == code) {
                return status;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    public static String getDesc(int code) {
        PaymentStatus status = fromCode(code);
        return status != null ? status.desc : "未知";
    }

    /**
     * 判断是否已支付成功
     */
    public static boolean isSuccess(int code) {
        return code == SUCCESS.code;
    }

    /**
     * 判断是否可退款
     */
    public static boolean isRefundable(int code) {
        return code == SUCCESS.code;
    }
}

package com.bookmall.payment.feign;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

/**
 * 订单服务 Feign 客户端
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@FeignClient(name = "order-service", path = "/api/order")
public interface OrderFeignClient {

    /**
     * 获取订单详情
     */
    @GetMapping("/{orderId}")
    Result<Object> getOrderDetail(@PathVariable("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 支付订单
     */
    @PostMapping("/{orderId}/pay")
    Result<Void> payOrder(@PathVariable("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 更新订单状态为已支付
     */
    @PostMapping("/{orderId}/pay-success")
    Result<Void> paySuccess(@PathVariable("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 检查订单是否属于用户
     */
    @GetMapping("/belongs/{orderId}/{userId}")
    Result<Boolean> belongsToUser(@PathVariable("orderId") String orderId,
                                  @PathVariable("userId") Long userId);
}

package com.bookmall.payment.feign;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

/**
 * 用户服务 Feign 客户端
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@FeignClient(name = "user-service", path = "/user")  // ✅ 修改为 /user
public interface UserFeignClient {

    /**
     * 检查用户是否存在
     */
    @GetMapping("/exists/{userId}")
    Result<Boolean> checkUserExists(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 获取用户余额
     */
    @GetMapping("/balance/{userId}")
    Result<BigDecimal> getUserBalance(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 扣减余额
     */
    @PostMapping("/balance/deduct")
    Result<Void> deductBalance(@RequestParam("userId") Long userId,
                               @RequestParam("amount") BigDecimal amount);

    /**
     * 增加余额（退款）
     */
    @PostMapping("/balance/add")
    Result<Void> addBalance(@RequestParam("userId") Long userId,
                            @RequestParam("amount") BigDecimal amount);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Payment\src\main\java\com\bookmall\payment\handler\PaymentExceptionHandler.java ====================

package com.bookmall.payment.handler;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import feign.FeignException;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;

/**
 * 支付模块异常处理器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestControllerAdvice
public class PaymentExceptionHandler {

    /**
     * Feign调用异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(FeignException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleFeignException(FeignException e) {
        log.error("Feign调用失败: status={}, message={}", e.status(), e.getMessage());
        if (e.status() == 404) {
            return Result.error("服务不可用，请稍后再试");
        }
        if (e.status() >= 500) {
            return Result.error("服务异常，请稍后再试");
        }
        return Result.error("请求失败，请稍后再试");
    }

    /**
     * 业务异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(BusinessException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleBusinessException(BusinessException e) {
        log.warn("业务异常: code={}, message={}", e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
        return Result.error(e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
    }

    /**
     * 系统异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public Result<Void> handleException(Exception e) {
        log.error("系统异常: ", e);
        return Result.error(ExceptionEnum.SYSTEM_ERROR.getMessage());
    }
}

package com.bookmall.payment.mapper;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.payment.entity.Payment;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Update;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 支付数据访问层
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Mapper
public interface PaymentMapper extends BaseMapper<Payment> {

    /**
     * 根据支付单号查询
     */
    @Select("SELECT payment_id, payment_no, order_id, user_id, amount, pay_type, " +
            "status, transaction_id, callback_data, pay_time, expire_time, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_payment WHERE payment_no = #{paymentNo}")
    Payment selectByPaymentNo(@Param("paymentNo") String paymentNo);

    /**
     * 根据订单号查询
     */
    @Select("SELECT payment_id, payment_no, order_id, user_id, amount, pay_type, " +
            "status, transaction_id, callback_data, pay_time, expire_time, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_payment WHERE order_id = #{orderId}")
    Payment selectByOrderId(@Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 分页查询用户支付记录
     */
    Page<Payment> selectUserPaymentPage(Page<Payment> page,
                                        @Param("userId") Long userId,
                                        @Param("status") Integer status);

    /**
     * 分页查询所有支付记录（管理员）
     */
    Page<Payment> selectAllPaymentPage(Page<Payment> page,
                                       @Param("userId") Long userId,
                                       @Param("status") Integer status,
                                       @Param("startTime") LocalDateTime startTime,
                                       @Param("endTime") LocalDateTime endTime);

    /**
     * 更新支付状态
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_payment SET status = #{status} WHERE payment_no = #{paymentNo}")
    int updateStatus(@Param("paymentNo") String paymentNo,
                     @Param("status") Integer status);

    /**
     * 支付成功回调更新
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_payment SET status = 2, transaction_id = #{transactionId}, " +
            "callback_data = #{callbackData}, pay_time = NOW() " +
            "WHERE payment_no = #{paymentNo} AND status = 0")
    int paySuccess(@Param("paymentNo") String paymentNo,
                   @Param("transactionId") String transactionId,
                   @Param("callbackData") String callbackData);

    /**
     * 支付失败回调更新
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_payment SET status = 3, callback_data = #{callbackData} " +
            "WHERE payment_no = #{paymentNo} AND status = 0")
    int payFailed(@Param("paymentNo") String paymentNo,
                  @Param("callbackData") String callbackData);

    /**
     * 取消支付
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_payment SET status = 4 WHERE payment_no = #{paymentNo} AND status = 0")
    int cancelPayment(@Param("paymentNo") String paymentNo);

    /**
     * 开始退款
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_payment SET status = 5 WHERE payment_no = #{paymentNo} AND status = 2")
    int startRefund(@Param("paymentNo") String paymentNo);

    /**
     * 退款完成
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_payment SET status = 6 WHERE payment_no = #{paymentNo} AND status = 5")
    int refundComplete(@Param("paymentNo") String paymentNo);

    /**
     * 统计总支付笔数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_payment")
    Long countTotal();

    /**
     * 统计成功支付笔数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_payment WHERE status = 2")
    Long countSuccess();

    /**
     * 统计总支付金额
     */
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(amount), 0) FROM tb_payment")
    BigDecimal sumTotalAmount();

    /**
     * 统计成功支付金额
     */
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(amount), 0) FROM tb_payment WHERE status = 2")
    BigDecimal sumSuccessAmount();

    /**
     * 统计今日支付笔数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_payment WHERE DATE(create_time) = CURDATE()")
    Long countTodayPayments();

    /**
     * 统计今日支付金额
     */
    @Select("SELECT IFNULL(SUM(amount), 0) FROM tb_payment " +
            "WHERE DATE(create_time) = CURDATE() AND status = 2")
    BigDecimal sumTodayAmount();

    /**
     * 按支付方式统计
     */
    @Select("SELECT pay_type, COUNT(*) AS count, IFNULL(SUM(amount), 0) AS amount " +
            "FROM tb_payment WHERE status = 2 GROUP BY pay_type")
    List<Map<String, Object>> statsByPayType();

    /**
     * 获取超时未支付记录（30分钟前创建且状态为待支付）
     */
    @Select("SELECT payment_no FROM tb_payment " +
            "WHERE status = 0 AND create_time < DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 30 MINUTE)")
    List<String> findTimeoutPendingPayments();
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Payment\src\main\java\com\bookmall\payment\schedule\PaymentTimeoutTask.java ====================

package com.bookmall.payment.schedule;

import com.bookmall.payment.service.PaymentService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * 支付超时定时任务
 * 每5分钟执行一次，取消30分钟未支付的支付记录
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PaymentTimeoutTask {

    private final PaymentService paymentService;

    /**
     * 每5分钟执行一次
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 */5 * * * ?")
    public void cancelTimeoutPayments() {
        log.info("开始执行超时支付取消任务");
        try {
            paymentService.cancelTimeoutPayments();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("超时支付取消任务执行失败: {}", e.getMessage(), e);
        }
        log.info("超时支付取消任务执行完成");
    }
}

package com.bookmall.payment.service;
