==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Payment\src\main\java\com\bookmall\payment\service\impl\PaymentServiceImpl.java ====================

package com.bookmall.payment.service.impl;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.RedisUtil;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.SnowflakeIdWorker;
import com.bookmall.payment.dto.PaymentCallbackDTO;
import com.bookmall.payment.dto.PaymentCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.payment.dto.PaymentDTO;
import com.bookmall.payment.dto.PaymentStatisticsVO;
import com.bookmall.payment.entity.Payment;
import com.bookmall.payment.enums.PaymentStatus;
import com.bookmall.payment.feign.OrderFeignClient;
import com.bookmall.payment.feign.UserFeignClient;
import com.bookmall.payment.mapper.PaymentMapper;
import com.bookmall.payment.service.PaymentService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * 支付服务实现类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PaymentServiceImpl implements PaymentService {

    private final PaymentMapper paymentMapper;
    private final SnowflakeIdWorker snowflakeIdWorker;
    private final RedisUtil redisUtil;

    // Feign Clients
    private final OrderFeignClient orderFeignClient;
    private final UserFeignClient userFeignClient;

    // Redis Key前缀 - 支付锁
    private static final String REDIS_PAYMENT_LOCK_KEY = "payment:lock:";

    // 支付超时时间（30分钟）
    private static final int PAYMENT_TIMEOUT_MINUTES = 30;

    // ==================== 用户端 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public PaymentDTO createPayment(Long userId, PaymentCreateDTO request) {
        // 1. 获取分布式锁（防止重复支付）
        String lockKey = REDIS_PAYMENT_LOCK_KEY + request.getOrderId();
        Boolean locked = redisUtil.setIfAbsent(lockKey, "1", 10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        if (locked == null || !locked) {
            throw new BusinessException("支付处理中，请勿重复操作");
        }

        try {
            // 2. 验证用户
            validateUser(userId);

            // 3. 验证订单
            Result<Object> orderResult = orderFeignClient.getOrderDetail(request.getOrderId());
            if (orderResult == null || !orderResult.isSuccess() || orderResult.getData() == null) {
                throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.ORDER_NOT_EXIST);
            }
            Map<String, Object> order = (Map<String, Object>) orderResult.getData();

            // 验证订单是否属于该用户
            if (!userId.equals(((Number) order.get("userId")).longValue())) {
                throw new BusinessException("订单不属于该用户");
            }

            // 验证订单状态（只有待付款才能支付）
            Integer orderStatus = (Integer) order.get("orderStatus");
            if (orderStatus != Constants.ORDER_STATUS_PENDING) {
                throw new BusinessException("订单状态不正确，无法支付");
            }

            // 验证支付方式
            if (request.getPayType() != Constants.PAY_TYPE_BALANCE) {
                throw new BusinessException("当前仅支持余额支付");
            }

            // 4. 检查是否已存在支付记录
            Payment existPayment = paymentMapper.selectByOrderId(request.getOrderId());
            if (existPayment != null) {
                if (PaymentStatus.isSuccess(existPayment.getStatus())) {
                    throw new BusinessException("该订单已支付成功");
                }
                if (existPayment.getStatus() == PaymentStatus.PENDING.getCode()) {
                    // 存在待支付的记录，直接返回
                    return convertToDTO(existPayment);
                }
            }

            // 5. 验证余额（余额支付）
            BigDecimal amount = (BigDecimal) order.get("totalAmount");
            Result<BigDecimal> balanceResult = userFeignClient.getUserBalance(userId);
            if (balanceResult == null || !balanceResult.isSuccess()) {
                throw new BusinessException("获取余额失败");
            }
            BigDecimal balance = balanceResult.getData();
            if (balance == null || balance.compareTo(amount) < 0) {
                throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.BALANCE_INSUFFICIENT);
            }

            // 6. 创建支付记录
            String paymentNo = generatePaymentNo();
            Payment payment = new Payment();
            payment.setPaymentNo(paymentNo);
            payment.setOrderId(request.getOrderId());
            payment.setUserId(userId);
            payment.setAmount(amount);
            payment.setPayType(request.getPayType());
            payment.setStatus(PaymentStatus.PENDING.getCode());
            payment.setExpireTime(LocalDateTime.now().plusMinutes(PAYMENT_TIMEOUT_MINUTES));

            paymentMapper.insert(payment);

            log.info("创建支付记录成功: paymentNo={}, orderId={}, userId={}, amount={}",
                    paymentNo, request.getOrderId(), userId, amount);

            // 7. 执行余额支付
            executeBalancePayment(payment);

            return convertToDTO(payment);
        } finally {
            // 释放锁
            redisUtil.delete(lockKey);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 执行余额支付
     */
    @Transactional
    public void executeBalancePayment(Payment payment) {
        try {
            // 1. 扣减余额
            Result<Void> deductResult = userFeignClient.deductBalance(payment.getUserId(), payment.getAmount());
            if (deductResult == null || !deductResult.isSuccess()) {
                paymentMapper.payFailed(payment.getPaymentNo(), "余额扣减失败");
                throw new BusinessException("余额扣减失败：" +
                        (deductResult != null ? deductResult.getMessage() : "服务不可用"));
            }

            // 2. 更新支付状态为成功
            String transactionId = generateTransactionId();
            paymentMapper.paySuccess(payment.getPaymentNo(), transactionId, "余额支付成功");

            // 3. 通知订单服务更新订单状态
            Result<Void> orderResult = orderFeignClient.paySuccess(payment.getOrderId());
            if (orderResult == null || !orderResult.isSuccess()) {
                log.warn("订单状态更新失败: orderId={}", payment.getOrderId());
                // 注意：这里即使订单更新失败，支付状态已更新，需要人工介入
            }

            log.info("余额支付成功: paymentNo={}, orderId={}, userId={}, amount={}",
                    payment.getPaymentNo(), payment.getOrderId(), payment.getUserId(), payment.getAmount());

        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("余额支付失败: paymentNo={}, error={}", payment.getPaymentNo(), e.getMessage());
            paymentMapper.payFailed(payment.getPaymentNo(), e.getMessage());
            throw e;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentDTO getPaymentDetail(Long userId, String paymentNo) {
        if (!belongsToUser(paymentNo, userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PAYMENT_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        Payment payment = getPaymentEntity(paymentNo);
        return convertToDTO(payment);
    }

    @Override
    public Page<PaymentDTO> getUserPayments(Long userId, Integer status, Integer pageNum, Integer pageSize) {
        Page<Payment> page = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize);
        Page<Payment> paymentPage = paymentMapper.selectUserPaymentPage(page, userId, status);

        Page<PaymentDTO> dtoPage = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize, paymentPage.getTotal());
        List<PaymentDTO> dtoList = paymentPage.getRecords().stream()
                .map(this::convertToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        dtoPage.setRecords(dtoList);

        return dtoPage;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void cancelPayment(Long userId, String paymentNo) {
        if (!belongsToUser(paymentNo, userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PAYMENT_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        Payment payment = getPaymentEntity(paymentNo);
        if (payment.getStatus() != PaymentStatus.PENDING.getCode()) {
            throw new BusinessException("当前状态无法取消");
        }

        paymentMapper.cancelPayment(paymentNo);

        log.info("取消支付成功: paymentNo={}, userId={}", paymentNo, userId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void handlePaymentCallback(PaymentCallbackDTO callback) {
        Payment payment = paymentMapper.selectByPaymentNo(callback.getPaymentNo());
        if (payment == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PAYMENT_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        // 防止重复回调
        if (payment.getStatus() != PaymentStatus.PENDING.getCode()) {
            log.info("支付记录已处理，跳过回调: paymentNo={}, status={}",
                    callback.getPaymentNo(), payment.getStatus());
            return;
        }

        if (PaymentStatus.isSuccess(callback.getStatus())) {
            // 支付成功
            paymentMapper.paySuccess(callback.getPaymentNo(),
                    callback.getTransactionId(),
                    callback.getCallbackData());

            // 通知订单服务
            orderFeignClient.paySuccess(callback.getOrderId());

            log.info("支付回调处理成功: paymentNo={}, orderId={}",
                    callback.getPaymentNo(), callback.getOrderId());
        } else {
            // 支付失败
            paymentMapper.payFailed(callback.getPaymentNo(), callback.getCallbackData());
            log.info("支付回调处理失败: paymentNo={}", callback.getPaymentNo());
        }
    }

    // ==================== 管理员端 ====================

    @Override
    public Page<PaymentDTO> getAllPayments(Long userId, Integer status, LocalDateTime startTime,
                                           LocalDateTime endTime, Integer pageNum, Integer pageSize) {
        Page<Payment> page = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize);
        Page<Payment> paymentPage = paymentMapper.selectAllPaymentPage(page, userId, status, startTime, endTime);

        Page<PaymentDTO> dtoPage = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize, paymentPage.getTotal());
        List<PaymentDTO> dtoList = paymentPage.getRecords().stream()
                .map(this::convertToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        dtoPage.setRecords(dtoList);

        return dtoPage;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void refund(Long userId, String paymentNo) {
        Payment payment = getPaymentEntity(paymentNo);

        // 验证用户
        if (!payment.getUserId().equals(userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PAYMENT_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        // 验证状态
        if (!PaymentStatus.isRefundable(payment.getStatus())) {
            throw new BusinessException("当前状态无法退款");
        }

        // 开始退款
        paymentMapper.startRefund(paymentNo);

        // 退还余额
        Result<Void> refundResult = userFeignClient.addBalance(userId, payment.getAmount());
        if (refundResult == null || !refundResult.isSuccess()) {
            // 退款失败，回滚状态
            paymentMapper.updateStatus(paymentNo, PaymentStatus.SUCCESS.getCode());
            throw new BusinessException("退款失败：" +
                    (refundResult != null ? refundResult.getMessage() : "服务不可用"));
        }

        // 退款完成
        paymentMapper.refundComplete(paymentNo);

        log.info("退款成功: paymentNo={}, userId={}, amount={}",
                paymentNo, userId, payment.getAmount());
    }

    // ==================== 统计 ====================

    @Override
    public PaymentStatisticsVO getPaymentStatistics() {
        Long totalPayments = paymentMapper.countTotal();
        Long successPayments = paymentMapper.countSuccess();
        Long failedPayments = totalPayments != null && successPayments != null ?
                totalPayments - successPayments : 0L;

        BigDecimal totalAmount = paymentMapper.sumTotalAmount();
        if (totalAmount == null) {
            totalAmount = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        }

        BigDecimal successAmount = paymentMapper.sumSuccessAmount();
        if (successAmount == null) {
            successAmount = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        }

        Long todayPayments = paymentMapper.countTodayPayments();
        if (todayPayments == null) {
            todayPayments = 0L;
        }

        BigDecimal todayAmount = paymentMapper.sumTodayAmount();
        if (todayAmount == null) {
            todayAmount = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        }

        // 按支付方式统计
        List<Map<String, Object>> methodStats = paymentMapper.statsByPayType();
        Map<Integer, PaymentStatisticsVO.PaymentMethodStats> methodStatsMap = new HashMap<>();
        for (Map<String, Object> stat : methodStats) {
            Integer payType = (Integer) stat.get("pay_type");
            Long count = (Long) stat.get("count");
            BigDecimal amount = (BigDecimal) stat.get("amount");
            String methodName = getPayTypeName(payType);

            methodStatsMap.put(payType, PaymentStatisticsVO.PaymentMethodStats.builder()
                    .count(count)
                    .amount(amount != null ? amount : BigDecimal.ZERO)
                    .methodName(methodName)
                    .build());
        }

        return PaymentStatisticsVO.builder()
                .totalPayments(totalPayments != null ? totalPayments : 0L)
                .successPayments(successPayments != null ? successPayments : 0L)
                .failedPayments(failedPayments)
                .totalAmount(totalAmount)
                .successAmount(successAmount)
                .todayPayments(todayPayments)
                .todayAmount(todayAmount)
                .methodStats(methodStatsMap)
                .build();
    }

    // ==================== 定时任务 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void cancelTimeoutPayments() {
        List<String> timeoutPaymentNos = paymentMapper.findTimeoutPendingPayments();
        if (timeoutPaymentNos == null || timeoutPaymentNos.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        int successCount = 0;
        for (String paymentNo : timeoutPaymentNos) {
            try {
                int result = paymentMapper.cancelPayment(paymentNo);
                if (result > 0) {
                    successCount++;
                    log.info("定时任务取消超时支付: paymentNo={}", paymentNo);
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("取消超时支付失败: paymentNo={}, error={}", paymentNo, e.getMessage());
            }
        }

        log.info("定时任务取消超时支付完成，共处理 {} 笔，成功取消 {} 笔",
                timeoutPaymentNos.size(), successCount);
    }

    // ==================== 内部调用 ====================

    @Override
    public Payment getPaymentEntity(String paymentNo) {
        Payment payment = paymentMapper.selectByPaymentNo(paymentNo);
        if (payment == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PAYMENT_NOT_EXIST);
        }
        return payment;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean belongsToUser(String paymentNo, Long userId) {
        Payment payment = paymentMapper.selectByPaymentNo(paymentNo);
        return payment != null && payment.getUserId().equals(userId);
    }

    // ==================== 私有方法 ====================

    /**
     * 验证用户
     */
    private void validateUser(Long userId) {
        Result<Boolean> result = userFeignClient.checkUserExists(userId);
        if (result == null || !result.isSuccess() || result.getData() == null || !result.getData()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.USER_NOT_EXIST);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 生成支付单号
     * 格式：PAY + yyyyMMddHHmmss + 6位随机数
     */
    private String generatePaymentNo() {
        String timestamp = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMddHHmmss"));
        String seq = String.format("%06d", snowflakeIdWorker.nextId() % 1000000);
        return "PAY" + timestamp + seq;
    }

    /**
     * 生成交易流水号
     */
    private String generateTransactionId() {
        String timestamp = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMddHHmmss"));
        String seq = String.format("%06d", snowflakeIdWorker.nextId() % 1000000);
        return "TXN" + timestamp + seq;
    }

    /**
     * 获取支付方式名称
     */
    private String getPayTypeName(Integer payType) {
        if (payType == null) return "未知";
        switch (payType) {
            case 0: return "余额支付";
            case 1: return "支付宝";
            case 2: return "微信支付";
            default: return "其他";
        }
    }

    /**
     * 实体转DTO
     */
    private PaymentDTO convertToDTO(Payment payment) {
        if (payment == null) {
            return null;
        }

        // 计算剩余时间
        Long remainingSeconds = null;
        if (payment.getExpireTime() != null && payment.getStatus() == PaymentStatus.PENDING.getCode()) {
            remainingSeconds = Math.max(0,
                    java.time.Duration.between(LocalDateTime.now(), payment.getExpireTime()).getSeconds());
        }

        return PaymentDTO.builder()
                .paymentId(payment.getPaymentId())
                .paymentNo(payment.getPaymentNo())
                .orderId(payment.getOrderId())
                .userId(payment.getUserId())
                .username(payment.getUsername())
                .amount(payment.getAmount())
                .payType(payment.getPayType())
                .payTypeDesc(payment.getPayTypeDesc() != null ? payment.getPayTypeDesc() :
                        getPayTypeName(payment.getPayType()))
                .status(payment.getStatus())
                .statusDesc(payment.getStatusDesc() != null ? payment.getStatusDesc() :
                        PaymentStatus.getDesc(payment.getStatus()))
                .transactionId(payment.getTransactionId())
                .payTime(payment.getPayTime())
                .expireTime(payment.getExpireTime())
                .createTime(payment.getCreateTime())
                .updateTime(payment.getUpdateTime())
                .remainingSeconds(remainingSeconds)
                .build();
    }
}

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">
<mapper namespace="com.bookmall.payment.mapper.PaymentMapper">

    <!-- 结果映射 -->
    <resultMap id="BaseResultMap" type="com.bookmall.payment.entity.Payment">
        <id column="payment_id" property="paymentId"/>
        <result column="payment_no" property="paymentNo"/>
        <result column="order_id" property="orderId"/>
        <result column="user_id" property="userId"/>
        <result column="amount" property="amount"/>
        <result column="pay_type" property="payType"/>
        <result column="status" property="status"/>
        <result column="transaction_id" property="transactionId"/>
        <result column="callback_data" property="callbackData"/>
        <result column="pay_time" property="payTime"/>
        <result column="expire_time" property="expireTime"/>
        <result column="create_time" property="createTime"/>
        <result column="update_time" property="updateTime"/>
        <result column="status_desc" property="statusDesc"/>
        <result column="pay_type_desc" property="payTypeDesc"/>
        <result column="username" property="username"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 通用查询字段 -->
    <sql id="Base_Column_List">
        p.payment_id, p.payment_no, p.order_id, p.user_id, p.amount,
        p.pay_type, p.status, p.transaction_id, p.callback_data,
        p.pay_time, p.expire_time, p.create_time, p.update_time
    </sql>

    <!-- 分页查询用户支付记录 -->
    <select id="selectUserPaymentPage" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>,
        CASE p.status
        WHEN 0 THEN '待支付'
        WHEN 1 THEN '处理中'
        WHEN 2 THEN '支付成功'
        WHEN 3 THEN '支付失败'
        WHEN 4 THEN '已取消'
        WHEN 5 THEN '退款中'
        WHEN 6 THEN '已退款'
        ELSE '未知'
        END AS status_desc,
        CASE p.pay_type
        WHEN 0 THEN '余额支付'
        WHEN 1 THEN '支付宝'
        WHEN 2 THEN '微信支付'
        ELSE '其他'
        END AS pay_type_desc,
        u.username
        FROM tb_payment p
        LEFT JOIN tb_user u ON p.user_id = u.user_id
        WHERE p.user_id = #{userId}
        <if test="status != null">
            AND p.status = #{status}
        </if>
        ORDER BY p.create_time DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 分页查询所有支付记录（管理员） -->
    <select id="selectAllPaymentPage" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>,
        CASE p.status
        WHEN 0 THEN '待支付'
        WHEN 1 THEN '处理中'
        WHEN 2 THEN '支付成功'
        WHEN 3 THEN '支付失败'
        WHEN 4 THEN '已取消'
        WHEN 5 THEN '退款中'
        WHEN 6 THEN '已退款'
        ELSE '未知'
        END AS status_desc,
        CASE p.pay_type
        WHEN 0 THEN '余额支付'
        WHEN 1 THEN '支付宝'
        WHEN 2 THEN '微信支付'
        ELSE '其他'
        END AS pay_type_desc,
        u.username
        FROM tb_payment p
        LEFT JOIN tb_user u ON p.user_id = u.user_id
        <where>
            <if test="userId != null">
                AND p.user_id = #{userId}
            </if>
            <if test="status != null">
                AND p.status = #{status}
            </if>
            <if test="startTime != null">
                AND p.create_time >= #{startTime}
            </if>
            <if test="endTime != null">
                AND p.create_time &lt;= #{endTime}
            </if>
        </where>
        ORDER BY p.create_time DESC
    </select>

</mapper>

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Review\src\main\java\com\bookmall\review\config\ReviewConfig.java ====================

package com.bookmall.review.config;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.DbType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.MybatisPlusInterceptor;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.inner.PaginationInnerInterceptor;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

/**
 * Review模块配置类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Configuration
public class ReviewConfig {

    /**
     * MyBatis-Plus 分页插件
     */
    @Bean
    public MybatisPlusInterceptor mybatisPlusInterceptor() {
        MybatisPlusInterceptor interceptor = new MybatisPlusInterceptor();
        interceptor.addInnerInterceptor(new PaginationInnerInterceptor(DbType.MYSQL));
        return interceptor;
    }
}

package com.bookmall.review.controller;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.PageResult;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewDTO;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewStatisticsVO;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewUpdateDTO;
import com.bookmall.review.service.ReviewService;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Parameter;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.validation.Valid;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 评价控制器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/review")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "评价接口", description = "评价管理")
public class ReviewController {

    private final ReviewService reviewService;

    // ==================== 用户端 ====================

    /**
     * 创建评价
     */
    @PostMapping
    @Operation(summary = "创建评价")
    public Result<ReviewDTO> createReview(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId,
            @Valid @RequestBody ReviewCreateDTO request) {
        ReviewDTO review = reviewService.createReview(userId, request);
        return Result.success("评价成功", review);
    }

    /**
     * 获取教材评价列表
     */
    @GetMapping("/book/{bookId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取教材评价列表")
    public Result<PageResult<ReviewDTO>> getBookReviews(
            @Parameter(description = "教材ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long bookId,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {
        Page<ReviewDTO> page = reviewService.getBookReviews(bookId, pageNum, pageSize);
        return Result.success(PageResult.of(page));
    }

    /**
     * 获取用户评价列表
     */
    @GetMapping("/user")
    @Operation(summary = "获取用户评价列表")
    public Result<PageResult<ReviewDTO>> getUserReviews(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {
        Page<ReviewDTO> page = reviewService.getUserReviews(userId, pageNum, pageSize);
        return Result.success(PageResult.of(page));
    }

    /**
     * 获取评价详情
     */
    @GetMapping("/{reviewId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取评价详情")
    public Result<ReviewDTO> getReviewDetail(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId,
            @Parameter(description = "评价ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long reviewId) {
        ReviewDTO review = reviewService.getReviewDetail(userId, reviewId);
        return Result.success(review);
    }

    /**
     * 更新评价
     */
    @PutMapping("/{reviewId}")
    @Operation(summary = "更新评价")
    public Result<ReviewDTO> updateReview(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId,
            @Parameter(description = "评价ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long reviewId,
            @Valid @RequestBody ReviewUpdateDTO request) {
        ReviewDTO review = reviewService.updateReview(userId, reviewId, request);
        return Result.success("更新成功", review);
    }

    /**
     * 删除评价
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/{reviewId}")
    @Operation(summary = "删除评价")
    public Result<Void> deleteReview(
            @RequestAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID) Long userId,
            @Parameter(description = "评价ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long reviewId) {
        reviewService.deleteReview(userId, reviewId);
        return Result.success("删除成功");
    }

    /**
     * 获取教材评价统计信息
     */
    @GetMapping("/statistics/book/{bookId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取教材评价统计信息")
    public Result<ReviewStatisticsVO> getBookReviewStatistics(
            @Parameter(description = "教材ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long bookId) {
        ReviewStatisticsVO statistics = reviewService.getBookReviewStatistics(bookId);
        return Result.success(statistics);
    }

    /**
     * 获取最新评价列表
     */
    @GetMapping("/latest")
    @Operation(summary = "获取最新评价列表")
    public Result<List<ReviewDTO>> getLatestReviews(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") int limit) {
        List<ReviewDTO> reviews = reviewService.getLatestReviews(limit);
        return Result.success(reviews);
    }

    // ==================== 管理员端 ====================

    /**
     * 删除评价（管理员）
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/admin/{reviewId}")
    @Operation(summary = "删除评价", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> deleteReviewByAdmin(
            @Parameter(description = "评价ID", required = true)
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long reviewId) {
        reviewService.deleteReviewByAdmin(reviewId);
        return Result.success("删除成功");
    }

    /**
     * 批量删除评价（管理员）
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/admin/batch")
    @Operation(summary = "批量删除评价", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<Void> batchDeleteReviews(@RequestBody List<Long> reviewIds) {
        reviewService.batchDeleteReviews(reviewIds);
        return Result.success("批量删除成功");
    }

    /**
     * 获取系统评价统计信息（管理员）
     */
    @GetMapping("/admin/statistics")
    @Operation(summary = "获取系统评价统计信息", description = "管理员操作")
    public Result<ReviewStatisticsVO> getSystemReviewStatistics() {
        ReviewStatisticsVO statistics = reviewService.getSystemReviewStatistics();
        return Result.success(statistics);
    }
}

package com.bookmall.review.dto;

import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.media.Schema;
import lombok.Data;

import javax.validation.constraints.Max;
import javax.validation.constraints.Min;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;

/**
 * 更新评价请求DTO
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Data
@Schema(description = "更新评价请求")
public class ReviewUpdateDTO {

    @NotNull(message = "评分不能为空")
    @Min(value = 1, message = "评分最低1星")
    @Max(value = 5, message = "评分最高5星")
    @Schema(description = "评分：1-5星", example = "4")
    private Integer rating;

    @NotBlank(message = "评价内容不能为空")
    @Schema(description = "评价内容", example = "这本书很好，推荐给大家")
    private String content;
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Review\src\main\java\com\bookmall\review\enums\ReviewRating.java ====================

package com.bookmall.review.enums;

import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Getter;

/**
 * 评价星级枚举
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Getter
@AllArgsConstructor
public enum ReviewRating {

    ONE_STAR(1, "⭐", "很差"),
    TWO_STARS(2, "⭐⭐", "较差"),
    THREE_STARS(3, "⭐⭐⭐", "一般"),
    FOUR_STARS(4, "⭐⭐⭐⭐", "满意"),
    FIVE_STARS(5, "⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐", "非常满意");

    private final int code;
    private final String stars;
    private final String desc;

    public static ReviewRating fromCode(int code) {
        for (ReviewRating rating : values()) {
            if (rating.code == code) {
                return rating;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    public static String getStars(int code) {
        ReviewRating rating = fromCode(code);
        return rating != null ? rating.stars : "";
    }

    public static String getDesc(int code) {
        ReviewRating rating = fromCode(code);
        return rating != null ? rating.desc : "未知";
    }

    /**
     * 校验评分是否合法
     */
    public static boolean isValid(int code) {
        return code >= 1 && code <= 5;
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Review\src\main\java\com\bookmall\review\handler\ReviewExceptionHandler.java ====================

package com.bookmall.review.handler;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import feign.FeignException;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;

/**
 * 评价模块异常处理器
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestControllerAdvice
public class ReviewExceptionHandler {

    /**
     * Feign调用异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(FeignException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleFeignException(FeignException e) {
        log.error("Feign调用失败: status={}, message={}", e.status(), e.getMessage());
        if (e.status() == 404) {
            return Result.error("服务不可用，请稍后再试");
        }
        if (e.status() >= 500) {
            return Result.error("服务异常，请稍后再试");
        }
        return Result.error("请求失败，请稍后再试");
    }

    /**
     * 业务异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(BusinessException.class)
    public Result<Void> handleBusinessException(BusinessException e) {
        log.warn("业务异常: code={}, message={}", e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
        return Result.error(e.getCode(), e.getMessage());
    }

    /**
     * 系统异常
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public Result<Void> handleException(Exception e) {
        log.error("系统异常: ", e);
        return Result.error(ExceptionEnum.SYSTEM_ERROR.getMessage());
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Review\src\main\java\com\bookmall\review\mapper\ReviewMapper.java ====================

package com.bookmall.review.mapper;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.review.entity.Review;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 评价数据访问层
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Mapper
public interface ReviewMapper extends BaseMapper<Review> {

    /**
     * 分页查询教材评价列表
     */
    Page<Review> selectBookReviewPage(Page<Review> page,
                                      @Param("bookId") Long bookId);

    /**
     * 分页查询用户评价列表
     */
    Page<Review> selectUserReviewPage(Page<Review> page,
                                      @Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 查询评价详情（含用户和教材信息）
     */
    Review selectReviewDetail(@Param("reviewId") Long reviewId);

    /**
     * 根据用户、教材、订单查询评价
     */
    @Select("SELECT review_id, user_id, book_id, order_id, rating, content, create_time " +
            "FROM tb_review WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND book_id = #{bookId} AND order_id = #{orderId}")
    Review selectByUserBookOrder(@Param("userId") Long userId,
                                 @Param("bookId") Long bookId,
                                 @Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 统计教材评价数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_review WHERE book_id = #{bookId}")
    Long countByBookId(@Param("bookId") Long bookId);

    /**
     * 统计教材各星级数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT rating, COUNT(*) AS count FROM tb_review " +
            "WHERE book_id = #{bookId} GROUP BY rating")
    List<Map<String, Object>> countRatingByBookId(@Param("bookId") Long bookId);

    /**
     * 计算教材平均评分
     */
    @Select("SELECT AVG(rating) FROM tb_review WHERE book_id = #{bookId}")
    Double avgRatingByBookId(@Param("bookId") Long bookId);

    /**
     * 统计用户评价数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_review WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    Long countByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 统计用户各星级数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT rating, COUNT(*) AS count FROM tb_review " +
            "WHERE user_id = #{userId} GROUP BY rating")
    List<Map<String, Object>> countRatingByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 检查用户是否已评价该订单的教材
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_review " +
            "WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND book_id = #{bookId} AND order_id = #{orderId}")
    int countByUserBookOrder(@Param("userId") Long userId,
                             @Param("bookId") Long bookId,
                             @Param("orderId") String orderId);

    /**
     * 批量删除评价（管理员）
     */
    int batchDelete(@Param("reviewIds") List<Long> reviewIds);

    /**
     * 批量删除用户评价（用户注销时使用）
     */
    int batchDeleteByUserIds(@Param("userIds") List<Long> userIds);

    /**
     * 批量删除教材评价（教材删除时使用）
     */
    int batchDeleteByBookIds(@Param("bookIds") List<Long> bookIds);

    /**
     * 统计系统评价总数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_review")
    Long countTotal();

    /**
     * 统计系统各星级数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT rating, COUNT(*) AS count FROM tb_review GROUP BY rating")
    List<Map<String, Object>> countAllRating();

    /**
     * 统计今日新增评价数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_review WHERE DATE(create_time) = CURDATE()")
    Long countTodayReviews();

    /**
     * 获取最新评价（带用户信息）
     */
    @Select("SELECT r.*, u.username, u.avatar as user_avatar, b.book_name, b.cover_image as book_cover " +
            "FROM tb_review r " +
            "LEFT JOIN tb_user u ON r.user_id = u.user_id " +
            "LEFT JOIN tb_book b ON r.book_id = b.book_id " +
            "ORDER BY r.create_time DESC LIMIT #{limit}")
    List<Review> selectLatestReviews(@Param("limit") int limit);
}

package com.bookmall.review.service;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewDTO;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewStatisticsVO;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewUpdateDTO;
import com.bookmall.review.entity.Review;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 评价服务接口
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
public interface ReviewService {

    // ==================== 用户端 ====================

    /**
     * 创建评价
     */
    ReviewDTO createReview(Long userId, ReviewCreateDTO request);

    /**
     * 分页查询教材评价列表

     * 分页查询用户评价列表

     */

     * 更新评价
     */
    ReviewDTO updateReview(Long userId, Long reviewId, ReviewUpdateDTO request);

     * 删除评价
     */

    /**
     * 批量删除用户评价（用户注销时使用）
     */
    void batchDeleteByUserIds(List<Long> userIds);

    /**
     * 批量删除教材评价（教材删除时使用）
     */
    void batchDeleteByBookIds(List<Long> bookIds);

    // ==================== 统计 ====================

    /**
     * 获取教材评价统计信息
     */
    ReviewStatisticsVO getBookReviewStatistics(Long bookId);

    /**
     * 获取系统评价统计信息
     */

    /**
     * 获取最新评价列表
     */
    List<ReviewDTO> getLatestReviews(int limit);

    // ==================== 验证 ====================

    /**
     * 检查评价是否属于用户
     */
    boolean belongsToUser(Long reviewId, Long userId);

    /**
     * 检查评价是否存在
     */
    boolean existsById(Long reviewId);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\Review\src\main\java\com\bookmall\review\service\impl\ReviewServiceImpl.java ====================

package com.bookmall.review.service.impl;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewCreateDTO;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewDTO;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewStatisticsVO;
import com.bookmall.review.dto.ReviewUpdateDTO;
import com.bookmall.review.entity.Review;
import com.bookmall.review.enums.ReviewRating;
import com.bookmall.review.feign.BookFeignClient;
import com.bookmall.review.feign.OrderFeignClient;
import com.bookmall.review.feign.UserFeignClient;
import com.bookmall.review.mapper.ReviewMapper;
import com.bookmall.review.service.ReviewService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * 评价服务实现类
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ReviewServiceImpl implements ReviewService {

    private final ReviewMapper reviewMapper;
    private final BookFeignClient bookFeignClient;
    private final UserFeignClient userFeignClient;
    private final OrderFeignClient orderFeignClient;

    // ==================== 用户端 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ReviewDTO createReview(Long userId, ReviewCreateDTO request) {
        // 1. 验证用户
        validateUser(userId);

        // 2. 验证教材
        Result<Boolean> bookResult = bookFeignClient.existsById(request.getBookId());
        if (bookResult == null || !bookResult.isSuccess() || !bookResult.getData()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.BOOK_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        // 3. 验证订单（检查用户是否购买了该教材）
        // TODO: 调用订单服务验证用户是否购买过该教材
        // 这里简化处理，假设用户已购买

        // 4. 检查是否已评价
        int count = reviewMapper.countByUserBookOrder(userId, request.getBookId(), request.getOrderId());
        if (count > 0) {
            throw new BusinessException("您已评价过该教材");
        }

        // 5. 验证评分
        if (!ReviewRating.isValid(request.getRating())) {
            throw new BusinessException("评分必须是1-5星");
        }

        // 6. 创建评价
        Review review = new Review();
        review.setUserId(userId);
        review.setBookId(request.getBookId());
        review.setOrderId(request.getOrderId());
        review.setRating(request.getRating());
        review.setContent(request.getContent());

        reviewMapper.insert(review);

        log.info("评价创建成功: reviewId={}, userId={}, bookId={}, rating={}",
                review.getReviewId(), userId, request.getBookId(), request.getRating());

        return convertToDTO(review);
    }

    @Override
    public Page<ReviewDTO> getBookReviews(Long bookId, Integer pageNum, Integer pageSize) {
        Page<Review> page = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize);
        Page<Review> reviewPage = reviewMapper.selectBookReviewPage(page, bookId);

        Page<ReviewDTO> dtoPage = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize, reviewPage.getTotal());
        List<ReviewDTO> dtoList = reviewPage.getRecords().stream()
                .map(this::convertToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        dtoPage.setRecords(dtoList);

        return dtoPage;
    }

    @Override
    public Page<ReviewDTO> getUserReviews(Long userId, Integer pageNum, Integer pageSize) {
        // 验证用户
        validateUser(userId);

        Page<Review> page = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize);
        Page<Review> reviewPage = reviewMapper.selectUserReviewPage(page, userId);

        Page<ReviewDTO> dtoPage = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize, reviewPage.getTotal());
        List<ReviewDTO> dtoList = reviewPage.getRecords().stream()
                .map(this::convertToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        dtoPage.setRecords(dtoList);

        return dtoPage;
    }

    @Override
    public ReviewDTO getReviewDetail(Long userId, Long reviewId) {
        // 验证评价是否存在且属于该用户
        if (!belongsToUser(reviewId, userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.REVIEW_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        Review review = reviewMapper.selectReviewDetail(reviewId);
        if (review == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.REVIEW_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        return convertToDTO(review);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ReviewDTO updateReview(Long userId, Long reviewId, ReviewUpdateDTO request) {
        // 验证评价是否存在且属于该用户
        if (!belongsToUser(reviewId, userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.REVIEW_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        Review review = reviewMapper.selectById(reviewId);
        if (review == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.REVIEW_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        // 验证评分
        if (!ReviewRating.isValid(request.getRating())) {
            throw new BusinessException("评分必须是1-5星");
        }

        // 更新
        review.setRating(request.getRating());
        review.setContent(request.getContent());
        reviewMapper.updateById(review);

        log.info("评价更新成功: reviewId={}, userId={}", reviewId, userId);

        return convertToDTO(review);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteReview(Long userId, Long reviewId) {
        // 验证评价是否存在且属于该用户
        if (!belongsToUser(reviewId, userId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.REVIEW_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        reviewMapper.deleteById(reviewId);

        log.info("评价删除成功: reviewId={}, userId={}", reviewId, userId);
    }

    // ==================== 管理员端 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteReviewByAdmin(Long reviewId) {
        Review review = reviewMapper.selectById(reviewId);
        if (review == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.REVIEW_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        reviewMapper.deleteById(reviewId);

        log.info("管理员删除评价成功: reviewId={}", reviewId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void batchDeleteReviews(List<Long> reviewIds) {
        if (reviewIds == null || reviewIds.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        // 验证所有评价是否存在
        for (Long reviewId : reviewIds) {
            Review review = reviewMapper.selectById(reviewId);
            if (review == null) {
                throw new BusinessException("评价ID " + reviewId + " 不存在");
            }
        }

        reviewMapper.batchDelete(reviewIds);

        log.info("批量删除评价成功: reviewIds={}", reviewIds);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void batchDeleteByUserIds(List<Long> userIds) {
        if (userIds == null || userIds.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        reviewMapper.batchDeleteByUserIds(userIds);
        log.info("批量删除用户评价成功: userIds={}", userIds);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void batchDeleteByBookIds(List<Long> bookIds) {
        if (bookIds == null || bookIds.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        reviewMapper.batchDeleteByBookIds(bookIds);
        log.info("批量删除教材评价成功: bookIds={}", bookIds);
    }

    // ==================== 统计 ====================

    @Override
    public ReviewStatisticsVO getBookReviewStatistics(Long bookId) {
        Long totalReviews = reviewMapper.countByBookId(bookId);
        if (totalReviews == 0) {
            return buildEmptyStatistics(bookId);
        }

        Double avgRating = reviewMapper.avgRatingByBookId(bookId);
        List<Map<String, Object>> ratingCounts = reviewMapper.countRatingByBookId(bookId);

        Map<Integer, Long> countMap = new HashMap<>();
        for (Map<String, Object> map : ratingCounts) {
            Integer rating = (Integer) map.get("rating");
            Long count = (Long) map.get("count");
            countMap.put(rating, count);
        }

        long oneStar = countMap.getOrDefault(1, 0L);
        long twoStar = countMap.getOrDefault(2, 0L);
        long threeStar = countMap.getOrDefault(3, 0L);
        long fourStar = countMap.getOrDefault(4, 0L);
        long fiveStar = countMap.getOrDefault(5, 0L);

        long positiveCount = fourStar + fiveStar;
        long negativeCount = oneStar + twoStar;

        double positiveRate = totalReviews > 0 ? (double) positiveCount / totalReviews : 0;
        double negativeRate = totalReviews > 0 ? (double) negativeCount / totalReviews : 0;

        return ReviewStatisticsVO.builder()
                .bookId(bookId)
                .totalReviews(totalReviews)
                .avgRating(avgRating != null ? Math.round(avgRating * 10) / 10.0 : 0.0)
                .oneStarCount(oneStar)
                .twoStarCount(twoStar)
                .threeStarCount(threeStar)
                .fourStarCount(fourStar)
                .fiveStarCount(fiveStar)
                .positiveRate(Math.round(positiveRate * 100 * 10) / 10.0)
                .negativeRate(Math.round(negativeRate * 100 * 10) / 10.0)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    public ReviewStatisticsVO getSystemReviewStatistics() {
        Long totalReviews = reviewMapper.countTotal();
        if (totalReviews == 0) {
            return buildEmptyStatistics(null);
        }

        List<Map<String, Object>> ratingCounts = reviewMapper.countAllRating();

        Map<Integer, Long> countMap = new HashMap<>();
        for (Map<String, Object> map : ratingCounts) {
            Integer rating = (Integer) map.get("rating");
            Long count = (Long) map.get("count");
            countMap.put(rating, count);
        }

        long oneStar = countMap.getOrDefault(1, 0L);
        long twoStar = countMap.getOrDefault(2, 0L);
        long threeStar = countMap.getOrDefault(3, 0L);
        long fourStar = countMap.getOrDefault(4, 0L);
        long fiveStar = countMap.getOrDefault(5, 0L);

        double totalScore = oneStar * 1 + twoStar * 2 + threeStar * 3 + fourStar * 4 + fiveStar * 5;
        double avgRating = totalReviews > 0 ? totalScore / totalReviews : 0;

        long positiveCount = fourStar + fiveStar;
        long negativeCount = oneStar + twoStar;

        double positiveRate = totalReviews > 0 ? (double) positiveCount / totalReviews : 0;
        double negativeRate = totalReviews > 0 ? (double) negativeCount / totalReviews : 0;

        return ReviewStatisticsVO.builder()
                .bookId(null)
                .totalReviews(totalReviews)
                .avgRating(Math.round(avgRating * 10) / 10.0)
                .oneStarCount(oneStar)
                .twoStarCount(twoStar)
                .threeStarCount(threeStar)
                .fourStarCount(fourStar)
                .fiveStarCount(fiveStar)
                .positiveRate(Math.round(positiveRate * 100 * 10) / 10.0)
                .negativeRate(Math.round(negativeRate * 100 * 10) / 10.0)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    public List<ReviewDTO> getLatestReviews(int limit) {
        List<Review> reviews = reviewMapper.selectLatestReviews(limit);
        return reviews.stream()
                .map(this::convertToDTO)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    // ==================== 验证 ====================

    @Override
    public boolean belongsToUser(Long reviewId, Long userId) {
        Review review = reviewMapper.selectById(reviewId);
        return review != null && review.getUserId().equals(userId);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean existsById(Long reviewId) {
        return reviewMapper.selectById(reviewId) != null;
    }

    // ==================== 私有方法 ====================

    /**
     * 验证用户
     */
    private void validateUser(Long userId) {
        Result<Boolean> result = userFeignClient.checkUserExists(userId);
        if (result == null || !result.isSuccess() || result.getData() == null || !result.getData()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.USER_NOT_EXIST);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 构建空统计
     */
    private ReviewStatisticsVO buildEmptyStatistics(Long bookId) {
        return ReviewStatisticsVO.builder()
                .bookId(bookId)
                .totalReviews(0L)
                .avgRating(0.0)
                .oneStarCount(0L)
                .twoStarCount(0L)
                .threeStarCount(0L)
                .fourStarCount(0L)
                .fiveStarCount(0L)
                .positiveRate(0.0)
                .negativeRate(0.0)
                .build();
    }

    /**
     * 实体转DTO
     */
    private ReviewDTO convertToDTO(Review review) {
        if (review == null) {
            return null;
        }

        return ReviewDTO.builder()
                .reviewId(review.getReviewId())
                .userId(review.getUserId())
                .username(review.getUsername())
                .userAvatar(review.getUserAvatar())
                .bookId(review.getBookId())
                .bookName(review.getBookName())
                .bookCover(review.getBookCover())
                .orderId(review.getOrderId())
                .rating(review.getRating())
                .ratingStars(review.getRatingStars() != null ? review.getRatingStars() :
                        ReviewRating.getStars(review.getRating()))
                .ratingDesc(review.getRatingDesc() != null ? review.getRatingDesc() :
                        ReviewRating.getDesc(review.getRating()))
                .content(review.getContent())
                .createTime(review.getCreateTime())
                .build();
    }
}

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">
<mapper namespace="com.bookmall.review.mapper.ReviewMapper">

    <!-- 结果映射 -->
    <resultMap id="BaseResultMap" type="com.bookmall.review.entity.Review">
        <id column="review_id" property="reviewId"/>
        <result column="user_id" property="userId"/>
        <result column="book_id" property="bookId"/>
        <result column="order_id" property="orderId"/>
        <result column="rating" property="rating"/>
        <result column="content" property="content"/>
        <result column="create_time" property="createTime"/>
        <!-- 非数据库字段 -->
        <result column="username" property="username"/>
        <result column="user_avatar" property="userAvatar"/>
        <result column="book_name" property="bookName"/>
        <result column="book_cover" property="bookCover"/>
        <result column="rating_stars" property="ratingStars"/>
        <result column="rating_desc" property="ratingDesc"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 通用查询字段 -->
    <sql id="Base_Column_List">
        r.review_id, r.user_id, r.book_id, r.order_id, r.rating, r.content, r.create_time
    </sql>

    <!-- 分页查询教材评价列表 -->
    <select id="selectBookReviewPage" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>,
        u.username,
        u.avatar AS user_avatar,
        b.book_name,
        b.cover_image AS book_cover,
        CASE r.rating
        WHEN 1 THEN '⭐'
        WHEN 2 THEN '⭐⭐'
        WHEN 3 THEN '⭐⭐⭐'
        WHEN 4 THEN '⭐⭐⭐⭐'
        WHEN 5 THEN '⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐'
        END AS rating_stars,
        CASE r.rating
        WHEN 1 THEN '很差'
        WHEN 2 THEN '较差'
        WHEN 3 THEN '一般'
        WHEN 4 THEN '满意'
        WHEN 5 THEN '非常满意'
        END AS rating_desc
        FROM tb_review r
        LEFT JOIN tb_user u ON r.user_id = u.user_id
        LEFT JOIN tb_book b ON r.book_id = b.book_id
        WHERE r.book_id = #{bookId}
        ORDER BY r.create_time DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 分页查询用户评价列表 -->
    <select id="selectUserReviewPage" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>,
        u.username,
        u.avatar AS user_avatar,
        b.book_name,
        b.cover_image AS book_cover,
        CASE r.rating
        WHEN 1 THEN '⭐'
        WHEN 2 THEN '⭐⭐'
        WHEN 3 THEN '⭐⭐⭐'
        WHEN 4 THEN '⭐⭐⭐⭐'
        WHEN 5 THEN '⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐'
        END AS rating_stars,
        CASE r.rating
        WHEN 1 THEN '很差'
        WHEN 2 THEN '较差'
        WHEN 3 THEN '一般'
        WHEN 4 THEN '满意'
        WHEN 5 THEN '非常满意'
        END AS rating_desc
        FROM tb_review r
        LEFT JOIN tb_user u ON r.user_id = u.user_id
        LEFT JOIN tb_book b ON r.book_id = b.book_id
        WHERE r.user_id = #{userId}
        ORDER BY r.create_time DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 查询评价详情（含用户和教材信息） -->
    <select id="selectReviewDetail" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT
        <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>,
        u.username,
        u.avatar AS user_avatar,
        b.book_name,
        b.cover_image AS book_cover,
        CASE r.rating
        WHEN 1 THEN '⭐'
        WHEN 2 THEN '⭐⭐'
        WHEN 3 THEN '⭐⭐⭐'
        WHEN 4 THEN '⭐⭐⭐⭐'
        WHEN 5 THEN '⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐'
        END AS rating_stars,
        CASE r.rating
        WHEN 1 THEN '很差'
        WHEN 2 THEN '较差'
        WHEN 3 THEN '一般'
        WHEN 4 THEN '满意'
        WHEN 5 THEN '非常满意'
        END AS rating_desc
        FROM tb_review r
        LEFT JOIN tb_user u ON r.user_id = u.user_id
        LEFT JOIN tb_book b ON r.book_id = b.book_id
        WHERE r.review_id = #{reviewId}
    </select>

    <!-- 批量删除评价 -->
    <delete id="batchDelete">
        DELETE FROM tb_review
        WHERE review_id IN
        <foreach collection="reviewIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </delete>

    <!-- 批量删除用户评价 -->
    <delete id="batchDeleteByUserIds">
        DELETE FROM tb_review
        WHERE user_id IN
        <foreach collection="userIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </delete>

    <!-- 批量删除教材评价 -->
    <delete id="batchDeleteByBookIds">
        DELETE FROM tb_review
        WHERE book_id IN
        <foreach collection="bookIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </delete>

</mapper>

package com.bookmall.user.config;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.DbType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.MybatisPlusInterceptor;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.inner.PaginationInnerInterceptor;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

@Configuration
public class UserConfig {

    /**
     * MyBatis-Plus 分页插件
     */
    @Bean
    public MybatisPlusInterceptor mybatisPlusInterceptor() {
        MybatisPlusInterceptor interceptor = new MybatisPlusInterceptor();
        interceptor.addInnerInterceptor(new PaginationInnerInterceptor(DbType.MYSQL));
        return interceptor;
    }
}

package com.bookmall.user.controller;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.PageResult;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.user.dto.UserInfoVO;
import com.bookmall.user.service.UserService;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;

/**
 * 管理员 - 用户管理接口
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/user/admin")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "管理员-用户管理", description = "管理后台用户管理接口")
public class AdminUserController {

    private final UserService userService;

    /**
     * 分页查询用户列表（管理员）
     * 对应 Feign: GET /user/admin/list
     */
    @GetMapping("/list")
    @Operation(summary = "分页查询用户列表")
    public Result<PageResult<UserInfoVO>> getUserList(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer status,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") Integer pageNum,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") Integer pageSize) {
        Page<UserInfoVO> page = userService.getUserListForAdmin(keyword, status, pageNum, pageSize);
        return Result.success(PageResult.of(page));
    }

    /**
     * 获取用户详情（管理员）
     * 对应 Feign: GET /user/admin/{userId}
     */
    @GetMapping("/{userId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取用户详情")
    public Result<UserInfoVO> getUserDetail(@PathVariable @NotNull Long userId) {
        return Result.success(userService.getUserInfo(userId));
    }

    /**
     * 启用/禁用用户（管理员）
     * 对应 Feign: PUT /user/admin/{userId}/status
     */
    @PutMapping("/{userId}/status")
    @Operation(summary = "启用/禁用用户")
    public Result<Void> updateUserStatus(
            @PathVariable @NotNull Long userId,
            @RequestParam @NotNull Integer status) {
        userService.updateUserStatus(userId, status);
        return Result.success(status == 1 ? "启用成功" : "禁用成功");
    }
}

package com.bookmall.user.controller;

import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.user.dto.LoginRequestDTO;
import com.bookmall.user.dto.LoginResponseVO;
import com.bookmall.user.dto.RegisterRequestDTO;
import com.bookmall.user.service.UserService;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.validation.Valid;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 认证控制器
 * 处理用户登录、注册、退出等认证相关请求
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/user")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "认证接口", description = "用户登录、注册、退出")
public class AuthController {

    private final UserService userService;

    // ==================== 测试接口（浏览器可直接访问） ====================

    /**
     * GET 测试接口 - 用于浏览器验证服务是否正常运行
     * 访问地址: http://localhost:8081/api/user/test
     */
    @GetMapping("/test")
    @Operation(summary = "测试接口（GET）", description = "用于验证服务是否正常运行")
    public Result<String> test() {
        log.info("测试接口被调用");
        return Result.success("✅ User 模块运行正常！当前时间: " + System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    /**
     * GET 测试接口 - 返回服务状态信息
     * 访问地址: http://localhost:8081/api/user/status
     */
    @GetMapping("/status")
    @Operation(summary = "服务状态（GET）", description = "返回服务运行状态")
    public Result<Map<String, Object>> status() {
        Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
        data.put("service", "user-service");
        data.put("status", "running");
        data.put("port", 8081);
        data.put("timestamp", System.currentTimeMillis());
        return Result.success(data);
    }

    /**
     * GET 欢迎接口
     * 访问地址: http://localhost:8081/api/user/welcome
     */
    @GetMapping("/welcome")
    @Operation(summary = "欢迎接口（GET）", description = "返回欢迎信息")
    public Result<String> welcome() {
        log.info("欢迎接口被调用");
        return Result.success("📚 欢迎使用教材商城 API！");
    }

    /**
     * GET 接口信息 - 显示所有可用接口
     * 访问地址: http://localhost:8081/api/user/endpoints
     */
    @GetMapping("/endpoints")
    @Operation(summary = "接口列表（GET）", description = "显示所有可用的接口列表")
    public Result<Map<String, String>> endpoints() {
        Map<String, String> endpoints = new HashMap<>();
        endpoints.put("GET  /api/user/test", "测试接口");
        endpoints.put("GET  /api/user/status", "服务状态");
        endpoints.put("GET  /api/user/welcome", "欢迎信息");
        endpoints.put("GET  /api/user/endpoints", "接口列表（当前）");
        endpoints.put("GET  /api/user/check-username", "检查用户名");
        endpoints.put("POST /api/user/login", "用户登录");
        endpoints.put("POST /api/user/register", "用户注册");
        endpoints.put("POST /api/user/logout", "用户退出");
        endpoints.put("GET  /api/user/info", "获取用户信息（需要Token）");
        endpoints.put("PUT  /api/user/password", "修改密码（需要Token）");
        endpoints.put("PUT  /api/user/info", "修改用户信息（需要Token）");
        return Result.success(endpoints);
    }

    // ==================== 业务接口 ====================

    /**
     * 用户登录
     * 请求方式: POST
     * 请求体: { "username": "testuser", "password": "123456" }
     */
    @PostMapping("/login")
    @Operation(summary = "用户登录")
    public Result<LoginResponseVO> login(@Valid @RequestBody LoginRequestDTO request) {
        log.info("用户登录请求: {}", request.getUsername());
        LoginResponseVO response = userService.login(request);
        return Result.success("登录成功", response);
    }

    /**
     * 用户注册
     * 请求方式: POST
     * 请求体: { "username": "newuser", "password": "123456", "phone": "13800138000", "email": "user@qq.com", "captcha": "1234" }
     */
    @PostMapping("/register")
    @Operation(summary = "用户注册")
    public Result<Void> register(@Valid @RequestBody RegisterRequestDTO request) {
        log.info("用户注册请求: {}", request.getUsername());
        userService.register(request);
        return Result.success("注册成功");
    }

    /**
     * 用户退出登录
     * 请求方式: POST
     * 需要携带 Token: Authorization: Bearer <token>
     */
    @PostMapping("/logout")
    @Operation(summary = "用户退出登录")
    public Result<Void> logout(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String token = request.getHeader("Authorization");
        if (token != null && token.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            token = token.substring(7);
        }
        userService.logout(token);
        log.info("用户退出登录");
        return Result.success("退出成功");
    }

    /**
     * 检查用户名是否可用
     * 访问地址: http://localhost:8081/api/user/check-username?username=testuser
     */
    @GetMapping("/check-username")
    @Operation(summary = "检查用户名是否可用")
    public Result<Boolean> checkUsername(@RequestParam String username) {
        log.info("检查用户名是否可用: {}", username);
        // TODO: 实际应该调用 Service 检查
        // boolean exists = userService.checkUsernameExists(username);
        // return Result.success(!exists);
        return Result.success(true);
    }
}

package com.bookmall.user.controller;

import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.entity.Result;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.JwtUtil;
import com.bookmall.user.dto.UpdatePasswordDTO;
import com.bookmall.user.dto.UpdateUserInfoDTO;
import com.bookmall.user.dto.UserInfoVO;
import com.bookmall.user.service.UserService;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.Operation;
import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.tags.Tag;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.validation.Valid;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.math.BigDecimal;

@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/user")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Validated
@Tag(name = "用户接口", description = "用户信息管理")
public class UserController {

    private final UserService userService;

    /**
     * 从请求中获取用户ID - 支持多种方式
     * 优先级: X-User-Id > 请求属性 > Authorization Token
     */
    private Long getUserId(HttpServletRequest request) {
        // 1. 从请求头获取（Gateway 转发）
        String userIdHeader = request.getHeader("X-User-Id");
        if (userIdHeader != null && !userIdHeader.isEmpty()) {
            try {
                Long userId = Long.parseLong(userIdHeader);
                log.debug("从 X-User-Id 获取用户ID: {}", userId);
                return userId;
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                log.warn("解析 X-User-Id 失败: {}", userIdHeader);
            }
        }

        // 2. 从请求属性获取
        Object userIdAttr = request.getAttribute(Constants.REQ_ATTR_USER_ID);
        if (userIdAttr instanceof Long) {
            Long userId = (Long) userIdAttr;
            log.debug("从请求属性获取用户ID: {}", userId);
            return userId;
        }

        // 3. ✅ 从 Authorization Header 解析 Token
        String authHeader = request.getHeader("Authorization");
        if (authHeader != null && authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            String token = authHeader.substring(7);
            try {
                // ✅ 直接使用 JwtUtil.getUserIdFromToken(token)
                Long userId = JwtUtil.getUserIdFromToken(token);
                if (userId != null) {
                    log.debug("从 Token 解析用户ID: {}", userId);
                    return userId;
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("解析 Token 失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            }
        }

        // 4. 尝试从请求参数获取（兼容特殊场景）
        String userIdParam = request.getParameter("userId");
        if (userIdParam != null && !userIdParam.isEmpty()) {
            try {
                Long userId = Long.parseLong(userIdParam);
                log.debug("从请求参数获取用户ID: {}", userId);
                return userId;
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                log.warn("解析 userId 参数失败: {}", userIdParam);
            }
        }

        throw new RuntimeException("用户未登录");
    }

    // ==================== 用户信息接口 ====================

    @GetMapping("/info")
    @Operation(summary = "获取当前用户信息")
    public Result<UserInfoVO> getUserInfo(HttpServletRequest request) {
        try {
            Long userId = getUserId(request);
            UserInfoVO userInfo = userService.getUserInfo(userId);
            return Result.success(userInfo);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.warn("获取用户信息失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return Result.error(401, "请先登录");
        }
    }

    @GetMapping("/info/{userId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取指定用户信息（内部调用）")
    public Result<UserInfoVO> getUserInfoById(@PathVariable @NotNull Long userId) {
        try {
            UserInfoVO userInfo = userService.getUserInfo(userId);
            return Result.success(userInfo);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("获取用户信息失败: userId={}", userId, e);
            return Result.error("用户不存在");
        }
    }

    @PutMapping("/password")
    @Operation(summary = "修改密码")
    public Result<Void> updatePassword(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @Valid @RequestBody UpdatePasswordDTO updatePasswordDTO) {
        try {
            Long userId = getUserId(request);
            userService.updatePassword(userId, updatePasswordDTO);
            return Result.success("密码修改成功");
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.warn("修改密码失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return Result.error(401, "请先登录");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("修改密码失败", e);
            return Result.error(e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @PutMapping("/info")
    @Operation(summary = "修改用户信息")
    public Result<Void> updateUserInfo(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            @Valid @RequestBody UpdateUserInfoDTO updateUserInfoDTO) {
        try {
            Long userId = getUserId(request);
            userService.updateUserInfo(userId, updateUserInfoDTO);
            return Result.success("用户信息修改成功");
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.warn("修改用户信息失败: {}", e.getMessage());
            return Result.error(401, "请先登录");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("修改用户信息失败", e);
            return Result.error(e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @GetMapping("/exists/{userId}")
    @Operation(summary = "检查用户是否存在（内部调用）")
    public Result<Boolean> checkUserExists(@PathVariable Long userId) {
        try {
            boolean exists = userService.checkUserExists(userId);
            return Result.success(exists);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("检查用户是否存在失败: userId={}", userId, e);
            return Result.success(false);
        }
    }

    // ==================== 余额操作（内部调用） ====================

    @GetMapping("/balance/{userId}")
    @Operation(summary = "获取用户余额", description = "内部调用")
    public Result<BigDecimal> getUserBalance(@PathVariable Long userId) {
        log.info("获取用户余额: userId={}", userId);
        try {
            UserInfoVO user = userService.getUserInfo(userId);
            if (user == null) {
                return Result.error("用户不存在");
            }
            return Result.success(user.getBalance());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("获取用户余额失败: userId={}", userId, e);
            return Result.error("获取余额失败");
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/balance/deduct")
    @Operation(summary = "扣减余额", description = "内部调用")
    public Result<Void> deductBalance(
            @RequestParam Long userId,
            @RequestParam BigDecimal amount) {
        log.info("扣减余额: userId={}, amount={}", userId, amount);
        try {
            userService.deductBalance(userId, amount);
            return Result.success("扣减成功");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("扣减余额失败: userId={}, amount={}", userId, amount, e);
            return Result.error(e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/balance/add")
    @Operation(summary = "增加余额", description = "内部调用")
    public Result<Void> addBalance(
            @RequestParam Long userId,
            @RequestParam BigDecimal amount) {
        log.info("增加余额: userId={}, amount={}", userId, amount);
        try {
            userService.addBalance(userId, amount);
            return Result.success("增加成功");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("增加余额失败: userId={}, amount={}", userId, amount, e);
            return Result.error(e.getMessage());
        }
    }
}

package com.bookmall.user.dto;

import io.swagger.v3.oas.annotations.media.Schema;
import lombok.Data;

import javax.validation.constraints.Email;
import javax.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import javax.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import javax.validation.constraints.Size;

@Data
@Schema(description = "注册请求")
public class RegisterRequestDTO {

    @NotBlank(message = "用户名不能为空")
    @Size(min = 3, max = 20, message = "用户名长度为3-20位")
    @Schema(description = "用户名", example = "newuser")
    private String username;

    @NotBlank(message = "密码不能为空")
    @Size(min = 6, max = 20, message = "密码长度为6-20位")
    @Schema(description = "密码", example = "123456")
    private String password;

    @Pattern(regexp = "^1[3-9]\\d{9}$", message = "手机号格式不正确")
    @Schema(description = "手机号", example = "13800138000")
    private String phone;

    @Email(message = "邮箱格式不正确")
    @Schema(description = "邮箱", example = "user@qq.com")
    private String email;

    @NotBlank(message = "验证码不能为空")
    @Schema(description = "验证码", example = "1234")
    private String captcha;
}
