package com.bookmall.user.entity;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.*;
import lombok.Data;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * 用户实体类 - 对应 tb_user 表
 */
@Data
@TableName("tb_user")
public class User {

    @TableId(value = "user_id", type = IdType.AUTO)
    private Long userId;

    @TableField("username")
    private String username;

    @TableField("password")
    private String password;

    @TableField("phone")
    private String phone;

    @TableField("email")
    private String email;

    @TableField("avatar")
    private String avatar;

    @TableField("balance")
    private BigDecimal balance;

    @TableField("status")
    private Integer status;

    @TableField(value = "create_time", fill = FieldFill.INSERT)
    private LocalDateTime createTime;

    @TableField(value = "update_time", fill = FieldFill.INSERT_UPDATE)
    private LocalDateTime updateTime;
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\User\src\main\java\com\bookmall\user\mapper\UserMapper.java ====================

package com.bookmall.user.mapper;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.bookmall.user.entity.User;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Update;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 用户数据访问层
 *
 * @author BookMall Team
 */
@Mapper
public interface UserMapper extends BaseMapper<User> {

    // ==================== 基础查询 ====================

    /**
     * 根据用户名查询用户
     */
    @Select("SELECT user_id, username, password, phone, email, avatar, balance, status, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_user WHERE username = #{username}")
    User selectByUsername(@Param("username") String username);

    /**
     * 根据手机号查询用户
     */
    @Select("SELECT user_id, username, password, phone, email, avatar, balance, status, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_user WHERE phone = #{phone}")
    User selectByPhone(@Param("phone") String phone);

    /**
     * 根据邮箱查询用户
     */
    @Select("SELECT user_id, username, password, phone, email, avatar, balance, status, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_user WHERE email = #{email}")
    User selectByEmail(@Param("email") String email);

    /**
     * 根据用户ID查询（带缓存）
     */
    @Select("SELECT user_id, username, password, phone, email, avatar, balance, status, create_time, update_time " +
            "FROM tb_user WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    User selectByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    // ==================== 余额操作 ====================

    /**
     * 扣减用户余额（带余额校验）
     *
     * @param userId 用户ID
     * @param amount 扣减金额
     * @return 影响行数（0表示余额不足或用户不存在）
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_user SET balance = balance - #{amount} " +
            "WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND balance >= #{amount} AND status = 1")
    int deductBalance(@Param("userId") Long userId, @Param("amount") BigDecimal amount);

    /**
     * 增加用户余额
     *
     * @param userId 用户ID
     * @param amount 增加金额
     * @return 影响行数
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_user SET balance = balance + #{amount} " +
            "WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND status = 1")
    int addBalance(@Param("userId") Long userId, @Param("amount") BigDecimal amount);

    /**
     * 获取用户余额
     *
     * @param userId 用户ID
     * @return 用户余额
     */
    @Select("SELECT balance FROM tb_user WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    BigDecimal getBalance(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    // ==================== 状态操作 ====================

    /**
     * 启用用户
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_user SET status = 1 WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    int enableUser(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 禁用用户
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_user SET status = 0 WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    int disableUser(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 更新用户头像
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_user SET avatar = #{avatar} WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    int updateAvatar(@Param("userId") Long userId, @Param("avatar") String avatar);

    /**
     * 更新用户手机号
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_user SET phone = #{phone} WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    int updatePhone(@Param("userId") Long userId, @Param("phone") String phone);

    /**
     * 更新用户邮箱
     */
    @Update("UPDATE tb_user SET email = #{email} WHERE user_id = #{userId}")
    int updateEmail(@Param("userId") Long userId, @Param("email") String email);

    // ==================== 验证检查 ====================

    /**
     * 检查用户名是否存在
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE username = #{username}")
    int countByUsername(@Param("username") String username);

    /**
     * 检查手机号是否存在
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE phone = #{phone}")
    int countByPhone(@Param("phone") String phone);

    /**
     * 检查邮箱是否存在
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE email = #{email}")
    int countByEmail(@Param("email") String email);

    /**
     * 检查用户名是否存在（排除指定用户ID）
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE username = #{username} AND user_id != #{userId}")
    int countByUsernameExcludeId(@Param("username") String username, @Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 检查手机号是否存在（排除指定用户ID）
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE phone = #{phone} AND user_id != #{userId}")
    int countByPhoneExcludeId(@Param("phone") String phone, @Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 检查邮箱是否存在（排除指定用户ID）
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE email = #{email} AND user_id != #{userId}")
    int countByEmailExcludeId(@Param("email") String email, @Param("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 检查用户是否存在且状态正常
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE user_id = #{userId} AND status = 1")
    int countActiveUser(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    // ==================== 批量操作 ====================

    /**
     * 批量启用/禁用用户（XML实现）
     */
    int batchUpdateStatus(@Param("userIds") List<Long> userIds, @Param("status") Integer status);

    /**
     * 批量删除用户（物理删除，XML实现）
     */
    int batchDelete(@Param("userIds") List<Long> userIds);

    // ==================== 统计查询 ====================

    /**
     * 统计各状态用户数量
     */
    @Select("SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS count FROM tb_user GROUP BY status")
    List<Map<String, Object>> countByStatus();

    /**
     * 统计今日注册用户数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE DATE(create_time) = CURDATE()")
    Long countTodayRegistered();

    /**
     * 统计本月注册用户数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE YEAR(create_time) = YEAR(CURDATE()) " +
            "AND MONTH(create_time) = MONTH(CURDATE())")
    Long countMonthRegistered();

    /**
     * 统计指定时间段注册用户数
     */
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user WHERE create_time BETWEEN #{startTime} AND #{endTime}")
    Long countRegisteredBetween(@Param("startTime") LocalDateTime startTime,
                                @Param("endTime") LocalDateTime endTime);

    /**
     * 统计用户总数（带条件）
     */
    @Select("<script>" +
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tb_user " +
            "<where>" +
            "<if test='username != null and username != \"\"'>" +
            "AND username LIKE CONCAT('%', #{username}, '%') " +
            "</if>" +
            "<if test='status != null'>" +
            "AND status = #{status} " +
            "</if>" +
            "</where>" +
            "</script>")
    Long countUsers(@Param("username") String username, @Param("status") Integer status);

    // ==================== 分页查询 ====================

    /**
     * 分页查询用户列表（XML实现）
     */
    List<User> selectUserPage(@Param("username") String username,
                              @Param("phone") String phone,
                              @Param("status") Integer status,
                              @Param("startTime") LocalDateTime startTime,
                              @Param("endTime") LocalDateTime endTime);

    /**
     * 查询所有正常用户
     */
    @Select("SELECT user_id, username, phone, email, avatar, balance " +
            "FROM tb_user WHERE status = 1 ORDER BY user_id")
    List<User> selectAllActiveUsers();

    /**
     * 根据用户ID列表查询用户
     */
    @Select("<script>" +
            "SELECT user_id, username, phone, email, avatar, balance, status " +
            "FROM tb_user WHERE user_id IN " +
            "<foreach collection='userIds' item='id' open='(' separator=',' close=')'>" +
            "#{id}" +
            "</foreach>" +
            "</script>")
    List<User> selectByIds(@Param("userIds") List<Long> userIds);
}

package com.bookmall.user.service;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.user.dto.*;
import com.bookmall.user.entity.User;

public interface UserService {

    // ==================== 认证 ====================

    /**
     * 用户登录
     */
    LoginResponseVO login(LoginRequestDTO request);

    /**
     * 用户注册
     */
    void register(RegisterRequestDTO request);

    /**
     * 退出登录
     */
    void logout(String token);

    // ==================== 用户信息 ====================

    /**
     * 获取用户信息
     */
    UserInfoVO getUserInfo(Long userId);

    /**
     * 修改密码
     */
    void updatePassword(Long userId, UpdatePasswordDTO request);

    /**
     * 修改用户信息
     */
    void updateUserInfo(Long userId, UpdateUserInfoDTO request);

    /**
     * 获取用户实体
     */
    User getUserById(Long userId);

    /**
     * 验证用户是否存在
     */
    boolean checkUserExists(Long userId);

    // ==================== 余额操作 ====================

    /**
     * 扣减余额
     */
    void deductBalance(Long userId, java.math.BigDecimal amount);

    /**
     * 增加余额
     */
    void addBalance(Long userId, java.math.BigDecimal amount);

    // ==================== 管理员端 ====================

    /**
     * 管理员分页查询用户
     *
     * @param keyword  关键词（用户名模糊匹配）
     * @param status   状态：0-禁用 1-正常，null 表示全部
     * @param pageNum  页码
     * @param pageSize 每页大小
     * @return 用户分页
     */
    Page<UserInfoVO> getUserListForAdmin(String keyword, Integer status, Integer pageNum, Integer pageSize);

    /**
     * 管理员启用/禁用用户
     *
     * @param userId 用户ID
     * @param status 状态：0-禁用 1-正常
     */
    void updateUserStatus(Long userId, Integer status);
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\User\src\main\java\com\bookmall\user\service\impl\UserServiceImpl.java ====================

package com.bookmall.user.service.impl;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.bookmall.common.constant.Constants;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.bookmall.common.exception.ExceptionEnum;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.BCryptUtil;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.JwtUtil;
import com.bookmall.common.utils.RedisUtil;
import com.bookmall.user.dto.*;
import com.bookmall.user.entity.User;
import com.bookmall.user.mapper.UserMapper;
import com.bookmall.user.service.UserService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class UserServiceImpl implements UserService {

    private final UserMapper userMapper;
    private final RedisUtil redisUtil;

    // ==================== 认证 ====================

    @Override
    public LoginResponseVO login(LoginRequestDTO request) {
        // 1. 根据用户名查询用户
        User user = userMapper.selectByUsername(request.getUsername());
        if (user == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.USER_NOT_EXIST);
        }

        // 2. 检查用户状态
        if (user.getStatus() == 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.USER_DISABLED);
        }

        // 3. 验证密码
        if (!BCryptUtil.matches(request.getPassword(), user.getPassword())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PASSWORD_ERROR);
        }

        // 4. 生成Token
        String token = JwtUtil.generateToken(user.getUserId(), user.getUsername(), Constants.ROLE_USER);

        // 5. 缓存用户信息到Redis
        String cacheKey = Constants.REDIS_USER_TOKEN + user.getUserId();
        redisUtil.set(cacheKey, token, Constants.JWT_EXPIRATION, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);

        // 6. 返回登录信息
        return LoginResponseVO.builder()
                .userId(user.getUserId())
                .username(user.getUsername())
                .avatar(user.getAvatar())
                .balance(user.getBalance())
                .token(token)
                .expiresIn(Constants.JWT_EXPIRATION)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void register(RegisterRequestDTO request) {
        // 1. 检查用户名是否已存在
        if (userMapper.selectByUsername(request.getUsername()) != null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.USERNAME_EXIST);
        }

        // 2. 检查手机号是否已存在
        if (StringUtils.hasText(request.getPhone()) && userMapper.selectByPhone(request.getPhone()) != null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PHONE_EXIST);
        }

        // 3. 检查邮箱是否已存在
        if (StringUtils.hasText(request.getEmail()) && userMapper.selectByEmail(request.getEmail()) != null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.EMAIL_EXIST);
        }

        // 4. 创建用户
        User user = new User();
        user.setUsername(request.getUsername());
        user.setPassword(BCryptUtil.encode(request.getPassword()));
        user.setPhone(request.getPhone());
        user.setEmail(request.getEmail());
        user.setBalance(BigDecimal.ZERO);
        user.setStatus(Constants.USER_STATUS_NORMAL);

        userMapper.insert(user);
        log.info("用户注册成功: username={}", request.getUsername());
    }

    @Override
    public void logout(String token) {
        if (StringUtils.hasText(token)) {
            Long userId = JwtUtil.getUserIdFromToken(token);
            if (userId != null) {
                redisUtil.delete(Constants.REDIS_USER_TOKEN + userId);
                log.info("用户退出登录: userId={}", userId);
            }
        }
    }

    // ==================== 用户信息 ====================

    @Override
    public UserInfoVO getUserInfo(Long userId) {
        User user = getUserById(userId);
        return UserInfoVO.builder()
                .userId(user.getUserId())
                .username(user.getUsername())
                .phone(user.getPhone())
                .email(user.getEmail())
                .avatar(user.getAvatar())
                .balance(user.getBalance())
                .status(user.getStatus())
                .createTime(user.getCreateTime())
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updatePassword(Long userId, UpdatePasswordDTO request) {
        User user = getUserById(userId);

        if (!BCryptUtil.matches(request.getOldPassword(), user.getPassword())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PASSWORD_ERROR);
        }

        user.setPassword(BCryptUtil.encode(request.getNewPassword()));
        userMapper.updateById(user);
        log.info("用户密码修改成功: userId={}", userId);

        redisUtil.delete(Constants.REDIS_USER_TOKEN + userId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updateUserInfo(Long userId, UpdateUserInfoDTO request) {
        User user = getUserById(userId);

        if (StringUtils.hasText(request.getPhone())) {
            User existUser = userMapper.selectByPhone(request.getPhone());
            if (existUser != null && !existUser.getUserId().equals(userId)) {
                throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.PHONE_EXIST);
            }
            user.setPhone(request.getPhone());
        }

        if (StringUtils.hasText(request.getEmail())) {
            User existUser = userMapper.selectByEmail(request.getEmail());
            if (existUser != null && !existUser.getUserId().equals(userId)) {
                throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.EMAIL_EXIST);
            }
            user.setEmail(request.getEmail());
        }

        if (StringUtils.hasText(request.getAvatar())) {
            user.setAvatar(request.getAvatar());
        }

        userMapper.updateById(user);
        log.info("用户信息修改成功: userId={}", userId);
    }

    @Override
    public User getUserById(Long userId) {
        User user = userMapper.selectById(userId);
        if (user == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.USER_NOT_EXIST);
        }
        return user;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean checkUserExists(Long userId) {
        return userMapper.selectById(userId) != null;
    }

    // ==================== 余额操作 ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deductBalance(Long userId, BigDecimal amount) {
        int result = userMapper.deductBalance(userId, amount);
        if (result <= 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(ExceptionEnum.BALANCE_INSUFFICIENT);
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void addBalance(Long userId, BigDecimal amount) {
        userMapper.addBalance(userId, amount);
    }

    // ==================== 管理员端 ====================

    /**
     * 管理员分页查询用户
     */
    @Override
    public Page<UserInfoVO> getUserListForAdmin(String keyword, Integer status, Integer pageNum, Integer pageSize) {
        Page<User> page = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize);

        LambdaQueryWrapper<User> wrapper = new LambdaQueryWrapper<>();
        if (StringUtils.hasText(keyword)) {
            wrapper.like(User::getUsername, keyword);
        }
        if (status != null) {
            wrapper.eq(User::getStatus, status);
        }
        wrapper.orderByDesc(User::getCreateTime);

        Page<User> userPage = userMapper.selectPage(page, wrapper);

        Page<UserInfoVO> dtoPage = new Page<>(pageNum, pageSize, userPage.getTotal());
        dtoPage.setRecords(userPage.getRecords().stream()
                .map(u -> UserInfoVO.builder()
                        .userId(u.getUserId())
                        .username(u.getUsername())
                        .phone(u.getPhone())
                        .email(u.getEmail())
                        .avatar(u.getAvatar())
                        .balance(u.getBalance())
                        .status(u.getStatus())
                        .createTime(u.getCreateTime())
                        .build())
                .collect(Collectors.toList()));
        return dtoPage;
    }

    /**
     * 管理员启用/禁用用户
     */
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updateUserStatus(Long userId, Integer status) {
        User user = getUserById(userId);

        // 状态合法性校验
        if (status == null || (status != 0 && status != 1)) {
            throw new BusinessException("状态值非法，只能是 0 或 1");
        }

        user.setStatus(status);
        userMapper.updateById(user);

        // 如果禁用用户，清除其 Token 缓存，强制下线
        if (status == 0) {
            redisUtil.delete(Constants.REDIS_USER_TOKEN + userId);
            log.info("用户已被禁用，清除登录缓存: userId={}", userId);
        }

        log.info("用户状态更新成功: userId={}, status={}", userId, status);
    }
}

==================== D:\study\Textbook_Mall\book-mall\User\src\main\resources\mapper\UserMapper.xml ====================

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">
<mapper namespace="com.bookmall.user.mapper.UserMapper">

    <!-- 结果映射 -->
    <resultMap id="BaseResultMap" type="com.bookmall.user.entity.User">
        <id column="user_id" property="userId"/>
        <result column="username" property="username"/>
        <result column="password" property="password"/>
        <result column="phone" property="phone"/>
        <result column="email" property="email"/>
        <result column="avatar" property="avatar"/>
        <result column="balance" property="balance"/>
        <result column="status" property="status"/>
        <result column="create_time" property="createTime"/>
        <result column="update_time" property="updateTime"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 通用查询字段 -->
    <sql id="Base_Column_List">
        user_id, username, password, phone, email, avatar, balance, status, create_time, update_time
    </sql>

    <!-- ==================== 基础查询 ==================== -->

    <!-- 根据用户名查询用户 -->
    <select id="selectByUsername" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE username = #{username}
    </select>

    <!-- 根据手机号查询用户 -->
    <select id="selectByPhone" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE phone = #{phone}
    </select>

    <!-- 根据邮箱查询用户 -->
    <select id="selectByEmail" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE email = #{email}
    </select>

    <!-- 根据用户ID查询 -->
    <select id="selectByUserId" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
    </select>

    <!-- 查询所有正常用户 -->
    <select id="selectAllActiveUsers" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT user_id, username, phone, email, avatar, balance
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE status = 1
        ORDER BY user_id
    </select>

    <!-- 根据用户ID列表查询用户 -->
    <select id="selectByIds" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT user_id, username, phone, email, avatar, balance, status
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE user_id IN
        <foreach collection="userIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </select>

    <!-- ==================== 分页查询 ==================== -->

    <!-- 分页查询用户列表 -->
    <select id="selectUserPage" resultMap="BaseResultMap">
        SELECT <include refid="Base_Column_List"/>
        FROM tb_user
        <where>
            <if test="username != null and username != ''">
                AND username LIKE CONCAT('%', #{username}, '%')
            </if>
            <if test="phone != null and phone != ''">
                AND phone LIKE CONCAT('%', #{phone}, '%')
            </if>
            <if test="status != null">
                AND status = #{status}
            </if>
            <if test="startTime != null">
                AND create_time >= #{startTime}
            </if>
            <if test="endTime != null">
                AND create_time &lt;= #{endTime}
            </if>
        </where>
        ORDER BY user_id DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 统计用户总数（带条件） -->
    <select id="countUsers" resultType="java.lang.Long">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        <where>
            <if test="username != null and username != ''">
                AND username LIKE CONCAT('%', #{username}, '%')
            </if>
            <if test="status != null">
                AND status = #{status}
            </if>
        </where>
    </select>

    <!-- ==================== 批量操作 ==================== -->

    <!-- 批量启用/禁用用户 -->
    <update id="batchUpdateStatus">
        UPDATE tb_user
        SET status = #{status}
        WHERE user_id IN
        <foreach collection="userIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </update>

    <!-- 批量删除用户（物理删除） -->
    <delete id="batchDelete">
        DELETE FROM tb_user
        WHERE user_id IN
        <foreach collection="userIds" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
            #{id}
        </foreach>
    </delete>

    <!-- ==================== 统计查询 ==================== -->

    <!-- 统计各状态用户数量 -->
    <select id="countByStatus" resultType="java.util.HashMap">
        SELECT
        status,
        COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM tb_user
        GROUP BY status
    </select>

    <!-- 统计今日注册用户数 -->
    <select id="countTodayRegistered" resultType="java.lang.Long">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE DATE(create_time) = CURDATE()
    </select>

    <!-- 统计本月注册用户数 -->
    <select id="countMonthRegistered" resultType="java.lang.Long">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE YEAR(create_time) = YEAR(CURDATE())
        AND MONTH(create_time) = MONTH(CURDATE())
    </select>

    <!-- 统计指定时间段注册用户数 -->
    <select id="countRegisteredBetween" resultType="java.lang.Long">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE create_time BETWEEN #{startTime} AND #{endTime}
    </select>

    <!-- 获取用户余额 -->
    <select id="getBalance" resultType="java.math.BigDecimal">
        SELECT balance
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
    </select>

    <!-- 检查用户名是否存在 -->
    <select id="countByUsername" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE username = #{username}
    </select>

    <!-- 检查手机号是否存在 -->
    <select id="countByPhone" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE phone = #{phone}
    </select>

    <!-- 检查邮箱是否存在 -->
    <select id="countByEmail" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE email = #{email}
    </select>

    <!-- 检查用户名是否存在（排除指定用户ID） -->
    <select id="countByUsernameExcludeId" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE username = #{username}
        AND user_id != #{userId}
    </select>

    <!-- 检查手机号是否存在（排除指定用户ID） -->
    <select id="countByPhoneExcludeId" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE phone = #{phone}
        AND user_id != #{userId}
    </select>

    <!-- 检查邮箱是否存在（排除指定用户ID） -->
    <select id="countByEmailExcludeId" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE email = #{email}
        AND user_id != #{userId}
    </select>

    <!-- 检查用户是否存在且状态正常 -->
    <select id="countActiveUser" resultType="java.lang.Integer">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM tb_user
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
        AND status = 1
    </select>

    <!-- ==================== 状态操作 ==================== -->

    <!-- 启用用户 -->
    <update id="enableUser">
        UPDATE tb_user
        SET status = 1
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
    </update>

    <!-- 禁用用户 -->
    <update id="disableUser">
        UPDATE tb_user
        SET status = 0
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
    </update>

    <!-- 更新用户头像 -->
    <update id="updateAvatar">
        UPDATE tb_user
        SET avatar = #{avatar}
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
    </update>

    <!-- 更新用户手机号 -->
    <update id="updatePhone">
        UPDATE tb_user
        SET phone = #{phone}
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
    </update>

    <!-- 更新用户邮箱 -->
    <update id="updateEmail">
        UPDATE tb_user
        SET email = #{email}
        WHERE user_id = #{userId}
    </update>

</mapper>
